icceyyukaḷāl, ivvaṟaṅkēṭṭalāl varum payaṉ kūṟukiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, “eṉittāṉum nallavi kēṭka aṉit tāṉum āṉṟa perumi tarum”1 eṉṟa karuttiyuṭkoṇṭu, mutaṟkaṇ ikkēḷvi iruviṉikaḷākiya aḻukkik kaḻuvit tūymi ceytu koḷḷum takutiyi yuṇṭupaṇṇum eṉṟaṟku, “kēṭpār iruviṉi kaḻuvum nīrār” eṉṟāṉ. ūṟṟu - iruviṉiyum uyiriyuṟuvatu; uṟuvatu, ūṟṟu - irumpip paḻukkak kāycciya viṭattu ataṉiṭattē uṟṟuttōṉṟum nīrpōla uyiriṭattē iruviṉiyum tōṉṟutalāl ūṟṟāyiṟ ṟeṉpar, “ōtiya viraṇṭum (nalviṉi  tīviṉikaḷ) uyiriṉi yuṟutal, ūṟṟām, tātuṟak kāynta pōḻtil tāṉuṟum nīri yottē”2 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. iḵtu īṉam, atikam, īrāpatakam, cāmparāyam, ñāṉam, aññāṉam, puṇṇiyam, pāvam, taviyam, pariṇāmam eṉap pattu vakiyākum. ivvūṟṟukkaḷ tavattāl aṭikkap paṭuvaṉavām; “ūṉantīrtavattiṉūṟṟuc ceṟitta mātavaṉi yētti”3 eṉa varutal kāṇka. aṭittalāvatu kupti, camiti, tarumam, cinti, aṭakkam, tāpaṉam eṉpaṉavaṟṟāl “parici vellum taṉmi”4 eṉpar. ivaṟṟuḷ aṭakkam, tāpaṉam, parici veṟṟi eṉṟa mūṉṟum, naṉṉiṉivu, nalluṇarvu iraṇṭumkūṭi mutti niliyi yuṇṭupaṇṇum eṉpavākaliṉ, “ūṟṟuc ceṟittuṭaṉ utirppi yākkum” eṉṟāṉ. aṭippu, ceṟippeṉap paṭutaliṉ, ūṟṟuc ceṟippu utirppi yākkum eṉṟāṉāyiṟṟu, “niṉṟa vantattiṉ mūṉṟum. (aṭakkam mutaliya mūṉṟum) niṉippu uṇarvu utirppi yākkum” eṉṟu piṟarum5 kūṟutal kāṇka. ivaṟṟiṉ virivellām patārtta cāram mērumantarapurāṇam mutaliya nūlkaḷuṭ kaṇṭu koḷka. ulaka vāḻviṉ poymmiyum mutti vāḻviṉ meymmiyum vēṟupaṭuttu nōkkumiṭattu, uṭalōṭu kūṭic ceyyum ulaka vāḻvu tuṉpa ētuvāvatu tōṉṟutaliṉ, aṟivuṭiyōr mutti vāḻvit terintuṇarntu, ulaka vāḻviṟku añcit tuṟavu pūṇṭu vīṭṭu vāḻvi urimi ceytukoḷvar eṉṟaṟku, “meyvaki terintu māṟṟi veruviṉarvīṭṭi” yeṉṟāṉ. meyvaki teriyātār ulaka vāḻvil tuṉpamum, terintār vīṭṭu vāḻvil iṉpamum nukarvar eṉṟa karuttēpaṭa, “maṉṟalar muṭiyiṉāy māṟṟum vīṭṭu mām, ceṉṟa tattuvanteḷiyāmi tēṟalāl”1 eṉṟu vāmaṉa muṉikaḷum  urittal kāṇka. mey - tattuvam. vīṭṭiṉpa vāḻvi nōkka, ulaka vāḻvu tuṉpamāy māṟupaṭṭiruttaliṉ “māṟṟu” eṉṟu camaṇ cāṉṟōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vīṭṭuyiriṉ ciṟappāvatu, “vevviṉiyeṇmiyiṉ, kēṭṭil eṇkuṇa meytiyōr kēṭilā, māṭciyāl”2 ulakantoḻa viḷaṅkuvatu. kēṭpataṉāl uḷatām payaṉ teriyāvaḻik kēṭpōrkkuk kēḷvikkaṇ maṉam cellātu eṉṉum karuttuppaṟṟi ivvaḷavum apayaruci tokuttuk kūṟiṉāṉeṉa vuṇarka.
ituvumatu
71.	malamali kurampi yiṉkaṇ maṉateḻu vikaṟpi māṟṟum
	pulamali pōkat tiṉkaṇ ṇāciyip poṉṟu vikkum
	kolimali koṭumi taṉṉik kuṟittiṭu maṉattiṟ kōlac
	cilimali nutali ṉārtaṅ kātalil tīmi ceyyum.
uri:- malam mali kurampiyiṉkaṇ - (mēlē kūṟiya kēḷvi) malam niṟinta uṭampiṉkaṇṇatākiya, maṉattu eḻu vikaṟpi māṟṟum - maṉattil eḻukiṉṟa tiripuṇarccikaḷip pōkki uṇmiyuṇarvu kaḷākkum, pulam malipōkattiṉkaṇ - aimpulaṉkaḷālum nukarappaṭum nukarccikkaṇ uṇṭākum, āciyip poṉṟuvikkum - āciyik keṭuttu vīṭṭiṉ pattil vēṭki nikaḻap paṇṇum, koli mali koṭumi taṉṉik kuṟittiṭum - koli mutaliya tīviṉik kētuvākiya tīyavuṇarccikaḷit taḻiyāvāṟu veṭṭi vīḻttum, kōlac cilimali nutaliṉār tam kātaliṉ - aḻakiya vilpōlum puruvat tiṉiyuṭiya makaḷirpāl uṇṭākum kāmaviccikku, maṉattil tīmi ceyyum - maṉattiṭattē koṭiya veṟuppiṉi yuṇṭu paṇṇum e-ṟu.
malam, aḻukku, kurampi, uṭampu; “pollāp puḻumali nōy puṉ kurampip”1 eṉṟu oḷaviyāruṅ kūṟutal kāṇka. ivvuṭampi iṭamākak koṇṭa maṉam, poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, pāvam, pavam, uruvam, peyar eṉṟa ivaṟṟāl tiripuṇarcci koḷvatātaliṉ, avvuṇarcci ivvaṟavuṇarvāl cemmi yeytum eṉṟaṟku, “maṉatteḻu vikaṟpi māṟṟum” eṉṟāṉ. pōkattiṉkaṭ piṟakkum āci oṉṟu palavāyp perukip piṟavik kētuvā taliṉ, “aciyip poṉṟuvikkum” eṉṟāṉ. vikaṟpavuṇarvum, pōkattiṉkaṇ āciyum koli mutaliya peruntīviṉiyākiya maratti vaḷarttu mēlum pala viṉikaḷākiya kaṉivakikaḷi viḷivittal paṟṟi, “kuṟittiṭum” eṉṟāṉ. makaḷir pāl uṇṭākum kāma vicci maṉattiṉkaṇṇatākaliṉ, avviṭattē ēḻātavāṟu keṭuttaḻikkum eṉṟaṟkut “tīmi ceyyum” eṉṟāṉ. nutal - ākupeyar. neṟṟikkē yurippiṉumām.
ituvumatu
72.	piṟantavar muyaṟci yālē peṟupaya ṉaṭiva rallāl*
	iṟantaṉar piṟanta tilli yiruviṉi tāṉu milleṉ
	ṟaṟintaṉa †raṟivil lāmi yatuviṭut taṟane ṟikkaṇ
	ciṟantaṉa muyalap paṇṇum ceppumip poruṇmi yeṉṟāṉ.
uri:- piṟantavar - maṇṇil uṭampoṭu piṟantavarkaḷ, peṟupayaṉ - peṟutaṟkuriya nalaṅkaḷi, muyaṟciyāl - tattam muyaṟciyiṉāl, aṭivar eṉṟu - peṟuvārkaḷ eṉṟum, allāl - allāmalum, iṟantaṉar piṟantatilli eṉṟu - iṟantavarkaḷ piṟantatu kiṭiyāteṉṟum, iruviṉitāṉum il eṉṟu - iruviṉi eṉpavikaḷum illiyeṉṟum, aṟintaṉar - colpavarkaḷ, aṟivillāmi atu viṭuttu - avvāṟu tām colvataṟkētuvākiya aṟiyāmiyip pōkki, aṟaneṟikkaṇ - aṟaneṟiyiṉ kaṇṇē celutti, ciṟantaṉa - vīṭupēṟṟuk kuriya vaṟṟik kuṟittu, ceppum ipporuṇmi - yām ippōtu collukiṉṟatākiya ivvaṟap poruḷ, muyalappaṇṇum - muyaṟci ceyvikkum e-ṟu.
ipporuṇmi, aṟivillāmi viṭuttu, aṟaneṟikkaṇ celutti, ciṟantaṉa muyalappaṇṇum eṉṟu muṭikka. celutti, yeṉpatu eñci niṉṟatu. ipporuḷiṉ taṉmi itu veṉpāṉ, poruṇmiyeṉ ṟoḻintāṉ. aṟintavar tāmē aṟiyāmi viṭuttu aṟaneṟikkaṇ niṉṟu ciṟantaṉa muyalumāṟu ceyyu meṉṟumām. viṉittoṭar peṉpa toṉṟu vēṇṭā, piṟantavar peṟum payaṉukku avar muyaṟciyē kāraṇa meṉpār, “muyaṟciyālē peṟupayaṉaṭiva” reṉkiṉṟaṉar. maṟupiṟappilli, iruviṉiyilli eṉpaṉa mutalāyiṉa  piṟar kūṟuvatik koṇṭu kūṟiyaṉavām. eṉṟu eṉpatu eṅkum kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. “viṉipaki yeṉṟiraṇṭiṉeccam”1 eṉpuḻippōla.
tāṉ aṟamuṇarnta varalāṟu kūṟal
73.	aṟapporuḷ viḷikkuṅ kāṭci yaruntava raruḷiṟ ṟaṉṟip
	piṟappuṇarn tataṉiṉ yāmē peyarttuṇarn tiṭavum paṭṭa
	tiṟappavu mataṉkaṇ tēṟṟam iṉituvit tiṭumi ṉeṉṟāṉ
	uṟappaṇin tevaru muḷḷan tuvantaṉar kēṭka luṟṟār.
uri:- aṟapporuḷ viḷikkum kāṭci - yāṉ kūṟum aṟamākiya poruḷ viḷivikkum payaṉi, aruntavar aruḷiṟṟaṉṟi - ariya tavattiyuṭiya muṉivararkaḷ urit taruḷiyataṉālumaṉṟi, piṟappuṇarntataṉiṉ - paḻam piṟappu varalāṟukaḷi yaṟiyu maṟivu ṭimiyāl, yāmē - emmāl peyarttum uṇarntiṭappaṭṭatu - maṟupaṭiyum uṇarappaṭṭa tākum, ataṉkaṇ  - ataṉāl avvaṟap poruḷiṉkaṇ, tēṟṟam - karutti, iṟappavum - mikavum teḷivāka, iṉitu vittiṭumiṉ - iṉitu vittuk kēṭpīrāka, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu apayaruci kūṟiṉāṉāka, evarum - āṅkirunta yāvarum, uṟappaṇintu - mikavum paṇintu, uvantaṉar kēṭkaluṟṟār - viruppattuṭaṉ kēṭpārāyiṉar e-ṟu.
aṟamākiya poruḷāl viḷiyum payaṉ karuvikaḷāl kāṇappaṭutaliṉ kāṭci yeṉappaṭṭatu. kōḻiyāyirunta kālattu akampaṉa muṉikaḷum, iraṭṭiyarāyp piṟantapiṉ cutatta muṉikaḷum paḻampiṟappiṉi yuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, “aruntavar” eṉṟāṉ. tiruvaṟam uṇarntu tiruntiya aṟiviṉa rāṉapōtu tāmākavum uṇarntu koṇṭatu eṉpāṉ, “peyarttuṇarntiṭavum paṭṭatu” eṉṟāṉ. ummi pirittuk kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. iṟappavum iṉitu eṉpaṉa vaṉpuṟi kuṟittu niṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu upatēca mukattālum kāṭci vakiyālum uṇarntatu eṉavē, yāvarkkum apporuḷik kēṭṭaṟku nāṭṭa muṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpār, “uṟappaṇintu evarum uḷḷat tuvantaṉar kēṭkaluṟṟār” eṉṟār. uvantaṉar, muṟṟeccam. aracaṉ mutal āṅkirunta makkaḷaṉivarum eṉṟaṟku “evarum” eṉṟār.
mutaṟ carukkam muṭintatu.

iraṇṭāvatu carukkam
ippakutikkaṇ, avantināṭṭu uñcayiṉi nakarattē acōkaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṉ cantiramatiyeṉpāḷi maṇantu yacōtaraṉeṉṉum makaṉippeṟṟu iṉitiruppatum, avaṉ orukāl taṉakku nariyuṇṭāṉatu kaṇṭu varunti, yacōtaraṉi aracaṉākkiviṭṭut tavamēṟ koḷvāṉ kuṇataraṉeṉṉum muṉivaṉpāl aṟaṅkēṭṭut tavam purintiruppatum, yacōtaraṉ, aṟatti nekiḻttup poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷil tōyntu kiṭappatum, avaṟkum avaṉ maṉivi amirtamatiyeṉpāṭkum yacōmatiyeṉṉum makaṉ piṟantiruppatum, cila yāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ orunāḷ amirtamati kaṇavaṉōṭu paḷḷiyil kaṇṇuṟaṅkupavaḷ, aṭṭapaṅkaṉeṉṉum yāṉippākaṉoruvaṉ māḷavapañcamam eṉṉum paṇṇip pāṭakkēṭṭu maṉamuruki avaṉ pāṟ kaḷḷak kāmaṅ koḷvatum, kuṇavati eṉṉum tōḻi palavāṟu eṭuttōti avaḷ ceyali vilakkavum, amirtamati ēlātu, aṭṭapaṅkaṉik kūṭṭuvikka vēṇṭumeṉa akkuṇavatiyi vaṟpuṟuttuvatum, avaḷ icintu ceṉṟu aṭṭapaṅkaṉi matiyuṭam paṭuppatum, amirtamatiyum aṭṭapaṅkaṉum kaḷḷakkāma nukarcci peṟṟuvaruvatum, avaṭku yacōtaraṉpāl muṉivu muṟukuvatum, aracaṉ aiyuṟṟu avaḷatu kaḷḷavoḻukkatti yaṟintu mutaṟkaṇ koli puriya niṉintu piṉ māṟuvatum, aracavikkaṇ amirta matiyuṭaṉ acatiyāṭik kaṟippuriyāl ikaḻvatum, taṉakkuḷḷē makaḷirpōkamum araciyaṟpōkamum veṟuttu, amirtamatiyiṉ kaḷḷavoḻukkatti oru kaṉaviṉmēl vittut tāyākiya cantira matikku urippatum, avaḷ caṇṭamārikku uyirppali tantu vaḻi paṭṭuk kaṉavukāṭṭiya tīṅku oḻika eṉpatum, avaṉ uṭaṉpaṭā toḻivatum, avaḷ ciṉantu māviṉāl kōḻiyoṉṟu ceytu paliyiṭṭuvaḻi paṭukaveṉpatum, avaṉ avvāṟē ceytapōtu mākkōḻi uyiruṭiyatupōl kūvi viḻutalum, avaṉ varuntit tuṟavu pūṇpatum, amirtamati avaṉukkum cantiramatikkum nañcuṇavu tantu kolvatum, iruvarum vilaṅkukaḷāyp piṟappatum, yacōmati  aracaṉātalum piṟavum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
avantināṭṭu uñcayiṉi nakaram
74.vaḷavayal vāriyiṉ malinta palpati
	aḷavaṟu caṉapata mavanti yāmatiṉ
	viḷipaya ṉamararum virumpunīr miya
	tuḷatoru nakaratuñ cayiṉi yeṉpavē.
uri:- vaḷavayal vāriyiṉ - vaḷaviya vayalkaḷākiya varuvāyāl, malinta - celvamniṟinta, palpati - palanakaraṅkaṭku iṭamākiya, avaḷaṟu caṉa patam - aḷavillāta makkaḷiyuṭi nāṭu, avantiyām - avantināṭākum, atiṉviḷipayaṉ - ataṉkaṇ vāḻvārk keytum pōkam, amararum virumpum nīrmiyatu - tēvarum virumpat takka taṉmittākum, oru nakar uḷatu - annāṭṭiṟkut taliyāya nakar oṉṟu uṇṭu, atu uñcayiṉi eṉpa - ataṉi uñcayiṉi eṉṟu periyōr kūṟuvārkaḷ e-ṟu.
vayalvaḷattāl mikka viḷiporuḷum, ataṉ vāyilākap peruñcelvamum perukutaliṉ, vaḷavayali “vāri” yeṉṟār. vāri, varuvāy; “puyaleṉṉum vāri” (14) eṉṟa tirukkuṟaṭkup parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟum uri kāṇka. caṉapatam, nāṭu. ummi, ciṟappu. aḵteṉṉal vēṇṭum. āytam - vikārattāl tokkatu. uñcayiṉi, vaṭacoṟcitivu. eṉpa veṉṟataṉi aciyāk kiṉumamiyum.
nāṭṭaracaṉ acōkaṉ eṉal
75.kantaṭu kaḷimata yāṉi maṉṉavaṉ
	intira ṉeṉuntiṟa lacōka ṉeṉṟuḷaṉ
	cantira matiyeṉum maṭanti taṉṉuṭaṉ
	antami luvakiya ṉamarntu vikunāḷ.
uri:- kantuaṭu kaḷimata yāṉi - kaṭṭuttaṟiyi alikkum matakkaḷip piṉiyuṭiya yāṉikaḷiyuṭiya, maṉṉavaṉ - aracaṉā vāṉ, intiraṉ eṉum tiṟal - tuṟakkavēntaṉāṉa intiraṉēyeṉṟu collattakka tiṟalpaṭitta, acōkaṉ eṉṟu uḷaṉ - acōkaṉ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭuvāṉ uḷaṉ, cantiramati eṉum maṭanti taṉṉuṭaṉ - cantiramatiyeṉṉum maṅkinallāḷuṭaṉ kūṭi, antam iluvakiyil - muṭivillāta iṉpattiyuṭiyaṉāy, amarntu vikumnāḷ - ataṉi virumpivāḻum nāṭkaḷil e-ṟu.
vaṟitēyuḷḷa yāṉi kantiṉi alippataṟkuk kāraṇam matakkaḷippuṭimi yeṉṟaṟku, “kāntaṭu kaḷimatayāṉi” eṉṟār. poḻikiṉṟa matamuṭimi tōṉṟaṟku, “kaḷimatayāṉi” eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu. pōkappēṟṟālēyaṉṟi valiyālum intiraṉi yoppāṉ eṉpār, “intiraṉeṉun tiṟal acōkaṉ” eṉṟār. maṭanti - paruvattālum potuviyalpālum kūṟiyatu. cantiramatiyiṉ kūṭṭam kūṭuntōṟum puttiṉpam payantuvantamiyiṉ, “antamil uvakiyaṉ” eṉṟār. antamiṉmi amarntu vikaṟku ētu.
yacōtaraṉ piṟappu
76.intuvō riḷampiṟi payanta teṉṉavē
	cantira matiyoru taṉayaṉ tantaṉaḷ
	entuyar kaḷipava ṉicō taraṉṉeṉa
	nantiya pukaḻavaṉ nāma mōtiṉāṉ.
uri:- intu - muḻumatiyāṉatu, ōr iḷampiṟi payanta teṉṉa. ōr piṟiccantiraṉip peṟṟāṟpōla, cantiramati - cantira matiyākiya araci, oru taṉayaṉ tantaṉaḷ - oru makaṉip peṟṟāḷ, em tuyar kaḷipavaṉ - eṅkaḷ tuṉpattit tuṭippavaṉāṉa acōkamaṉṉaṉ, nantiya pukaḻa avaṉ - mikkapukaḻiyuṭiya anta makaṉukku, icōtaraṉ eṉa- yacōtaraṉeṉṟu, nāmam ōtiṉāṉ - peyar vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
 cantiramati yacōtaraṉippeṟṟatu, muḻumati iḷampiṟiyi īṉṟatupōla eṉṟār, cantiramatiyiṉ aṟivē yacōtaraṉ matiyit taṉvaḻi yoḻukuvitta tātaliṉ. muḻumati iḷamatiyiyīṉal - ilporuḷuvami. emmippōl piṟavittuyarkaḷiyum nalloḻukka muṭiyaṉeṉpāṉ, “emtuyar kaḷipava” eṉṟu apayaruci kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. em tuyar kaḷipavaṉ eṉṟati yacōtaraṟkē yēṟṟi, tuyarkaḷi pavaṉātalāl yacōtaraṉeṉap peyariṭṭāṉ eṉṟumām.
yacōtaraṉ iḷavaracaṉāy amirtamatiyeṉpāḷi
 maṇantu makiḻtal
77.iḷaṅkaḷiṟ ṟuḻuviyi* ṉēta miṉṟiyē
	vaḷaṅkeḻu kumaraṉum vaḷarntu maṉṉaṉāy
	viḷaṅkiḻi yamiḻtamuṉ matiyi vēḷviyāṉ
	uḷaṅkoḷap puṇarntuṭa ṉuvaki yeytiṉāṉ.
uri:- iḷaṅkaḷiṟṟu uḻuviyiṉ - āṇpulikkuruḷi pōla, ētam iṉṟi - nalivu ciṟitum iṉṟi, vaḷamkeḻu kumaraṉum - valiporuntiya yacōtaraṉum, vaḷarntu - vaḷarccipeṟṟu, maṉṉaṉāy - iḷavaracaṉāki, viḷaṅkuiḻi amiḻtamuṉmatiyi - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷi yuṭiya amirtamatiyeṉpavaḷi, vēḷviyāṉ - tirumaṇattāl, uḷaṅkoḷa - maṉam iṉpam niṟiya, puṇarntu - kūṭi, uṭaṉ uvaki eytiṉāṉ - uṭaṉeytum iṉpatti nukarntu varalāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
kaḷiṟu - vilaṅkiṉ āṇpāṟpeyar; “ēṟum ēṟṟiyum oruttalum kaḷiṟum”1 eṉa āciriyar kūṟutal kāṇka. ik kaḷiṟeṉṉuñ col vēḻam,kēḻal eṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum ciṟappaveytuvippavāyiṉum īṇṭu itu potuvitiyāṉ amintateṉa vuṇarka. “iḷaṅkaḷi ṟuḻuviyiṉ ētamiṉṟiyē” eṉṟu pāṭaṅ
koḷḷiṉ, puliyāl tīṅkiṉṟi iḷaṅkaḷiṟu vaḷarntāṟpōla eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka. acōkaṉāṭciyilātal, yacōtaraṉāṭciyilātal uḻuvi pōlum pakiyuṇmi kūṟappaṭāmiyiṉ, atu pāṭa maṉmiyaṟika. vaḷam - īṇṭu meyvaṉmi mēṟṟu. “maṉṉaṉāy” eṉṟu īṇṭukkūṟiyatu, piṟāṇṭu, “yacōtaraṉ ṉeṉuntaṉayaṉi nilamakaṭṭalivaṉā keṉa”(83)  muṭicūṭṭal kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, iḷavara caṉātalikkuṟittu niṟpatāyiṟṟu. puṇarcci nikaḻumiṭattē iṉpamum uṭaṉnikaḻtalpaṟṟi, “uṭaṉ uvakiyey tiṉāṉ” eṉṟār. puṇarcci yiṉpamē neñcil eññāṉṟum nilavap puṇarntaṉareṉṟaṟku, “uḷaṅkoḷap puṇarntu”eṉac ciṟappittār; itaṉāṟpayaṉ, nallaṟivu kāṭṭum naṉṉeṟiyik kaṭipōkac cellum kaṭippiṭi avaṉpāl iṉmi yuṇarttuvatu.
yacōtaraṉukku yacōmatiyeṉṉum makaṉ piṟattal
78.	iḷiyavaḷ eḻilnalam ēntu koṅkiyiṉ
	viḷipaya ṉicōtaraṉ viḻintu cellunāḷ*
	kiḷiyava ruvakiyiṟ keḻuma īṉṟaṉaḷ
	vaḷiyava ḷicōmati mintaṉ taṉṉiyē.
uri:- icōtaraṉ - yacōtaraṉākiya iḷavaracaṉ, iḷi yavaḷ – iḷami yuṭiyaḷāṉa amiḻtamatiyiṉ, eḻil - aḻakiyum, nalam - kuṇañceykikaḷiṉ nalattiyum, ēntu koṅkiyiṉ viḷipayaṉ - uyarnta muliyiṭattē peṟum iṉpattiyum, viḻintu - virumpi, cellum nāḷ - iṉitirukkuṅkālattil, kiḷiyavar - cuṟṟattār, uvakiyiṉ keḻuma - makiḻcciyāl āravārikka, vaḷiyavaḷ - vaḷiyaṇinta avvamirtamati, icōmati mintaṉ taṉṉi - yacō matiyeṉṉum makaṉi, īṉṟaṉaḷ - peṟṟāḷ, e-ṟu.
yacōtaraṉ amiḻtamatiyiṉ iḷami, eḻil, ēntu koṅki eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉ nalatti viḻintu iṉpuṟṟoḻukiyatum, av voḻukkattiṉ payaṉāka, yacōmatiyip peṟṟatum itaṉkaṭ kūṟiṉār. kāmaviṉpattukkuc ciṟapputtaruvatu iḷamiyātaliṉ, “iḷiyavaḷ” eṉṟeṭuttu, amiḻtamatiyiṉ piṟa eḻil nalaṅkaḷiyum koṅkippayaṉiyum vitantōtiṉār. yacōmatiyiṉ piṟappu acōkaṉatu vaḻiyeñcāmikku ākkamātaliṉ, “kiḷiyavar uvakiyiṟ keḻuma vīṉṟaṉaḷ” eṉṟār. ivvāṟu maṉikku viḷakkākiya amiḻtamati taṉakku viḷakkan tarum makaṉip peṟṟā ḷeṉṟaṟku, “vaḷiyavaḷ icōmatimintaṉ taṉṉi yīṉṟaṉaḷ” eṉa avaḷmē lēṟṟiṉār. avaḷuṭiya eḻilnalac ciṟappi, yacōtaraṉ mēlum, makaṟpēṟṟiṉi avaḷmēlum kūṟiyatu, piṉṉar avaḷ taṉ nalattip poruntā voḻukkattil īṭupaṭuttik keṭuvatum, yacōmati ciṟappuṟṟu mēmpaṭuvatum kuṟikkoṇṭu pōlum. vaḷiyavaḷ - cuṭṭu.
acōkaṉ taṉakku mutumi varavu kāṇṭal
79.	maṟṟornāḷ maṉṉavaṉ makiḻntu kaṇṇaṭi
	paṟṟuvā ṉaṭitoḻap* paṭiva nōkkuvāṉ
	oṟṟivār kuḻaṉmayi rucci veṇmiyi
	uṟṟuṟā vakiyati yuḷintu kaṇṭaṉaṉ.
uri:- orunāḷ - ivvāṟu celluṅkālattu oru nāḷ, maṉṉavaṉ - vēntaṉākiya acōkaṉ, kaṇṇaṭi paṟṟuvāṉ - kaṇṇāṭi yēntikkāṭṭuvōṉ, aṭitoḻa aṭipaṇintu ataṉik kāṭṭa, paṭivam makiḻntu nōkkuvāṉ - taṉ vaṭivattik kāṇavirumpi atiṟ kāṇpavaṉ- vārkuḻal oṟṟimayir - nīṇṭa kaṭikuḻaṉ ṟamayiroṉṟu, ucci - nuṉiyil, veṇmiyi uṟṟu - veḷḷi niṟattip porunti, uṟāvakiyati - puṟattē naṉku tōṉṟāvaki maṟintiruppati, uḷintu kaṇṭaṉaṉ - maṉam varuntip pārttāṉ e-ṟu.
paṇṭiyaracarpāl aṭippi tāṅkuvōr, aṭiyīṭēntu vōr, kaṇṇaṭiyēntuvōr eṉak kuṟṟēval ceyvār palar iruntamiyiṉ īṇṭuk kaṇṇaṭiyēntuvōṉik kuṟippittār. taṉṉi naṉku oppaṉi ceytukoḷḷak karutik kaṇṇāṭi nōkkuṅkāl; makiḻcci piṟattal iyalpātalpaṟṟi, “makiḻntu nōkkuvāṉ” eṉṟār. avaṉ makiḻcci muṟṟum varuttamāy māṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu, “uḷintu kaṇṭaṉaṉ” eṉpataṉāl peṟṟām. oru mayir nuṉiyil narittut taṉ naripput tōṉṟāvaki maṟit tiruppiṉum ataṉik kaṇṭu kaḷintavaṉ, maṉattē varuttamuṟṟāṉeṉa vaṟika. ataṟkuriya kāraṇatti mēlvarum pāṭṭukkaḷāl urik kiṉṟār.
acōkaṉ taṉmaṉattē palapaṭa niṉittal
80.vaṇṭaḷir puritiru mēṉi mātarār
	kaṇṭaka luṟavaru kaḻiya mūppitu
	uṇṭeṉi luḷintika luruva villitaṉ
	vaṇṭuḷa kaṇipayaṉ maṉitark keṉṟaṉaṉ
uri:- vaṇtaḷirpuri - vaḷaviya māntaḷirpōlum, tiru mēṉimātarār  - aḻakiyamēṉiyuṭiyamakaḷir, kaṇṭu - pārttavuṭaṉ, akaluṟa - aruvaruttu nīṅkumpaṭiyāka, varu - varukiṉṟa, kaḻiyamūppu - āṇṭu mikutiyiyuṭiya mutumip paruvamāṉatu, uṇṭueṉil - uḷatāyiṉ atu variyiṟṟāṉ, uḷintu - maṉamtirintu, ikal - māṟupāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya, uruva villitaṉ - aḻakiya villiṉi yuṭiya maṉmataṉatu, vaṇṭuuḷa kaṇi - vaṇṭumoykkum pūvākiya ampu, maṉitarkkup payaṉ - makkaṭkuk kāmavēṭṭiyākiya payaṉi viḷivikkum, eṉṟaṉaṉ - eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē collik koṇṭāṉ e-ṟu.
taḷireṉap potuppaṭakkūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ciṟappuṭiya māntaḷir koḷḷappaṭṭatu; “māviṉ avirtaḷirpōlum mēṉiyar”1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōrum kūṟutal kāṇka. kaṇṭār maṉam kavarum aḻaku tikaḻum mēṉiyit “tirumēṉi” yeṉṟār. mikka kātalarāyiṉum, mūp peytiyavaḻi akkātalāṟ peṟum kāmaviṉpamkuṉṟutaliṉ, avar kāmak kātalum nīṅkum eṉpār, “kaṇṭu akaluṟa” eṉṟum, yāṇṭu mikamika mūppumikutaliṉ, “kaḻiya mūppu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. eṉil-eṉṟāl. kāmavēṭkiyuṟṟārkku maṉattuṉpam iyalpātaliṉ “uḷintu” eṉṟār. kāmaṉ uruviliyāyiṉum, avaṉ vil uru vuṭittāya, cāṉṟōrkku accam taruvatātaliṉ, “uruvavilli” eṉṟār. vaṇṭuḷa kaṇi - vaṇṭu taṅkiyirukkum pūṅkaṇi. malark kaṇi, kāma vēṭkiyik kiḷarac ceyvatu, payaṉātaliṉ, ataṉi, “vaṇṭuḷakaṇi payaṉ” eṉṟāṉ.
acōkaṉ iḷamiyiṉ vaḷamiyiṉmiyit 
taṉṉuḷ niṉittal
81.iḷamiyi ṉiyalpitu vāku meṉṉiṉiv*
	vaḷamiyi liḷamiyi maṉattu vippateṉ
	kiḷimiyu maṉiyatē keḻumu nammuḷat
	taḷimiyi viṭuvatē takuva tāmiṉi.
uri:- iḷamiyiṉ iyalpu ituvākum eṉṉiṉ - iḷamip paruvattiṉ iyalpu ittaṉmittākumāyiṉ, ivvaḷamiyil iḷamiyi - ivvaḷappamillāta iḷamip paruvatti, maṉattu vippatu eṉ-makkaḷ tam maṉattē poruḷāka matittukkoḷvatu eṟṟukku, kiḷimiyum aṉṉatē - ēṉiyōr uṟavumuṟimiyum attaṉmittēyākum, iṉi-iṉimēl, nam uḷam keḻumum taḷimiyi - nammaṉattē eḻum aṉpiṉi, viṭuvatu takuvatām - paṟṟaṟaviṭṭoḻippatē cālpākum e-ṟu.
iḷamikuṉṟātu iṉpattiṟ cemmān tirukkiṉṟēmeṉat tarukkiya vuṇarvāl makiḻcciyuṟṟuk kaṇṇāṭi nōkkiya vēntaṟku mayiriṉ naritōṉṟi avviḷamiyiṉ kaḻiviṉik kāṭṭakkaṇṭu, ataṉ niliyāmiyiṉiyum tērntu kūṟukiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, “iḷamiyiṉiyalpituvākum eṉṉiṉ” eṉṟāṉ. iḷamiyē vaḷami yeṉṟum, “iḷamiyiṟ ciṟantavaḷamiyumilli”1 eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvaṉa koṇṭu, ataṉip pēṇutalum ulakiyalātaliṉ, ataṉi muṉintu, “ivvaḷamiyil iḷamiyi maṉattu vippatu eṉ” eṉṟāṉ; eṉavē, iḷamiyiṉ vaḷamiyāvatu, nili pēṟuṭittāy iṉpa nukarccittāy iruttaleṉpatum, atukaḻinta vaḻic celvamum iṉpantārā teṉpatum peṟṟām “iḷami kaḻinta piṉṟi vaḷami, kāmam tarutalumiṉṟē”2 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. maṉivi makkaḷ aṭiyākap piṟakkum kiḷiñar uṟavum, aracu, celvam mutaliya paṟṟukkōṭāka, “uṟiṉ naṭṭu aṟiṉ” oruvum iyalpiṟṟātali yuṇarntu niṟṟaliṉ, “kiḷimiyum aṉiyatē” eṉṟāṉ. aṉpiṉ payaṉ, ataṉic ceyvōrum ceyyappaṭuvōrum piṇippuṇṭu niṟṟalātaliṉ, ataṉit “taḷimi”yeṉṟum, atukāraṇamāka vēṇṭutal vēṇṭāmi tōṉṟit tuṉpam viḷivittaliṉ, “viṭuvatu takuvatām” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. ēkāram - iraṇṭum tēṟṟam.
puṇṇiyamē ceyaṟpāṟṟeṉa acōkaṉ 
niṉintu teḷital
82.muntucey nalviṉi muḷippa vittalic
	cinticey poruḷoṭu celva meytiṉām
	muntiyiṉ mummaṭi muyaliṟ* puṇṇiyam
	intira vulakamum eytalā meṉā†
uri:- muntu cey nalviṉi - muṉṉip piṟaviyiṟ ceyta nalviṉiyāṉatu, it tali muḷippa - ippiṟaviyil taṉ payaṉit tōṟṟuvikka, cinticey poruḷoṭu - uyarntōr matikkattakka nallaṟivuṭaṉ; celvam eytiṉām - araca pōkattiyum aṭintuḷḷōm, muntiyiṉ muṉṉiyiṉum, mummaṭi puṇṇiyam  muyaliṉ - mūṉṟu maṭaṅku mikutiyāka nalviṉiyic ceyyiṉ, intira vulakamum eytalām - tēvarulakattup peṟum pōkattiyum peṟalām, eṉā - eṉṟu e-ṟu.
oruvaṉ ceyyum viṉi avaṉ uyiriṉkaṭkiṭantu, avaṉ pukkuḻippukum iyalpiṟṟeṉpavātaliṉ, “muntuceynalviṉi muḷippa vittali” eṉṟār. “muḷippa” eṉṟa kuṟippu, nalviṉi yākiyaviti uyirākiyanilattu vitikkappeṟutali yuṇartti niṉṟatu; “vevviṉi ceyyumāntar uyireṉum nilattuvitti avviṉiviḷiyuḷuṇṇum avviṭattu”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. oruvaṉ cinticeyvaṉa, immikkaṇ pukaḻum maṟumikkaṇ puṇṇiyamum vīṭupēṟumātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟ kētuvāya pukaḻum uṟutip poruḷaṟivum kūṟuvār, “cinti cey poruḷoṭu celvam eytiṉām” eṉṟār. celvattiṉum poruḷciṟantu niṟṟaliṉ, “poruḷoṭu ” eṉṟār. piṟanta piṟappāl, iṟanta piṟaviyiṟ ceyta viṉiyiṉit terintu koṇṭāṉātaliṉ, “cinticey poruḷoṭu celvameytiṉā” meṉṟum, “muntu ceynalviṉi” yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉeṉavuṇarka. mīṭṭum muṉ ceyta aḷavātal, irumaṭiyātal nalviṉi ceyyiṉ, maṟumiyil maṇṇulakattē aracarpōkamum, avar ellāruḷḷum taliyāya pōkamumē eytumeṉat teḷitaliṉ, “mummaṭi puṇṇiyam muyaliṉ”  eṉṟum, ataṉāṟpayaṉ viṇṇula kattuppeṟum intirapōkam eytumeṉṟu kaṇṭu, “intiravulakamum eytalām” eṉṟum taṉakkuḷ terinturip pāṉāyiṉaṉ. ivvārāycci yuṭiyavaṉ, niliyilpōkamākiya intiravulaka pōkattiyum piṉṉart tuṟantu vīṭupēṟṟiṟkē muyal vāṉ eṉṟaṟika.
acōkaṉ tuṟavu
83.iṉiyaṉa niṉivuṟīi yacōta raṉeṉun
	taṉayaṉi nilamakaḷ taliva ṉākeṉak
	kaṉamaṇi vaṉimuṭi kavittuk kāvalaṉ
	puṉivaḷi matimati pulampap pōyiṉāṉ.
uri:- kāvalaṉ - vēntaṉāṉa acōkaṉ, iṉiyaṉa niṉi vuṟīi - ittaṉmiyāṉavaṟṟi niṉintu, yacōtaraṉ eṉum taṉayaṉi yacōtaraṉ eṉappaṭum taṉ makaṉi, nilamakaḷ talivaṉ āka - nilattukkara caṉākuka, eṉa - eṉṟu colli, kaṉamaṇi vaṉimuṭi kavittu - periya maṇikaḷik koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa muṭiyic cūṭṭi, puṉivaḷi matimati pulampa - vaḷiyaṇinta cantiramatiyākiya taṉtēvi taṉittu varunta, pōyiṉāṉ - araciyal toṭarpu tuṟantu ceṉṟāṉ e-ṟu.
“muntiyiṉ mummaṭi muyaliṟ puṇṇiyam, intiravulakamum eytalām”  (82) eṉpataṉōṭu, immi maṟumi vīṭupēṟukaṭku ētuvākum iyalpuṭiya viṉittiṟamum, avaṟṟiṉ kaṭṭum, akkaṭṭiṉ nīṅkun tiṟamum, nīṅkāvaḻippaṭum tuṉpamum eṉṟa ivaṟṟiyum palvēṟu niliyāmikaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum niṉittami tōṉṟa, “iṉiyaṉa niṉivuṟīi” eṉṟār. nāṭṭu vēntaṉi, nāṭākiya makaṭkuk kaṇavaṉeṉpavākaliṉ, vēntaṉā vāṉi, “nilamakaṭṭa livaṉāka” eṉṟāṉ. colli yataṉōṭu amiyātu, mantiriccuṟṟat tārōṭu kalantu tiru muṭiyum yacōtararkkuc cūṭṭiṉāṉ. eṉṟār. piṉpu acōkaṉ, araciyaṟ ṟoṭarpu nīṅki, maṉiviyākiya cantira matiyiyum tuṟakka veṇṇiyapōtu, avaḷ pirivāṟṟāmi kūṟi varuntavum avaṭku iṇaṅkātu tuṟavēpūṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. eṉpār, “puṉi vaḷimatimati pulampap pōyiṉāṉ” eṉṟār. vaḷivuṉi mati matiyeṉpatu etuki nōkki “puṉivaḷimati eṉa vantatu” puṉivaḷi” eṉa vaḷiyi eṉa vaḷiyi vitantōtiyatu, acōkaṉatu tuṟavu moḻi ceviyiṟ paṭṭamāttiriyē avaḷ muṉkiyiṉ nillātu kaḻaṉṟōṭi avaḷatu pirivāṟṟāmiyi meyppaṭuttik kāṭṭiṟṟātaliṉ. iḵtu uṭampu naṉi curuṅkal. matimati - cantiramati. 
acōkaṉ kuṇatara muṉikaḷpāl aṟaṅkēṭṭut 
tavam ceyvāṉ vaṉattukkuc cellutal
84.	kurikaḻa lacōkaṉ meykkuṇataraṟpaṇin
	taricar kaḷimpatiṟ ṟiruvar tammuṭaṉ
	uriceya aruntavat* tuṟavu koṇṭupōy
	variyuṭi vaṉamatu maruvi ṉāṉarō.
uri:- kurikaḻal acōkaṉ - olikkiṉṟa vīrakaṇṭi yaṇinta vēntaṉāṉa acōkaṉ, meykkuṇataraṉ paṇintu - meyyaṟi viṉiyuṭiya kuṇatara muṉikaḷi vaṇaṅki avar uritta aṟaṅkaḷik kēṭṭu, aricarkaḷ aimpatiṟṟiruvar tammuṭaṉ - tuṇiyāy vanta aracar nūṟṟuvaruṭaṉ, uri ceya aruntavattu - collutaṟkariya tavakkōlam pūṇṭu, variyuṭi vaṉamatu - maliyiyuṭiya kāṭoṉṟi, pōy maruviṉāṉ - pōyc cērntāṉ e-ṟu.
meyyaṟivupeṟṟut tavavoḻukkam ciṟantuniṟṟaliṉ, kuṇatara muṉikaḷi, “meykkuṇataraṉ” eṉṟār. ivarpāl acōkaṉ ciṉa tarumamum, ciṉa tīkkiyum peṟṟāṉ eṉṟaṟkup “paṇintu” eṉṟār; tīkkiyum aṟamum peṟumuṉpum piṉpum paṇitalviṉi nikaḻtaliṉ, “paṇintu ” eṉa urikkappaṭṭaṉa; ittarumamum tīkkiyum ācā rāṅkameṉṉum nūluṭkūṟap paṭukiṉṟaṉa; avi pañcaparamēṭṭikaḷiṉ patamupatēcittal, mayir paṟittal mutalāyiṉa. pēraracaṉātaliṉ, ciṟṟaracar nūṟṟuvar uṭaṉ ceṉṟāreṉavaṟika. tavatturuvu, tavavēṭam; “uṟṟanōy nōṉṟal uyirkkuṟu kaṇ ceyyāmi, aṟṟē tavattukkuru”1 eṉṟapaṭi, nōṉpiyum uṟukaṇceyyāmiyiyum koḷḷalu moṉṟu. variyuṭivaṉam, maliyic cārnta kāṭu; mūṅkil kaḷiyuṭiya kāṭumām.
yacōtaraṉ nāṭāṭci mēṟkoḷḷal
85.	erimaṇi yimikkum pūṇā ṉicōtara ṉiruni lattāṭ*
	korumaṇit tilakam pōlu muñcayi ṉikku nātaṉ†
	arumaṇi muṭikoḷ ceṉṉi yaracaṭip paṭuttu yarnta
	kurumaṇik kuṭiyiṉ nīḻaṟ kuvalayaṅ kāval koṇṭāṉ.
uri:- erimaṇi imikkum pūṇāṉ - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa maṇi kaḷālākiya oḷivīcum aṇikalaṉkaḷiyuṭiyaṉākiya, icōtaraṉ - yacōtaraṉ, irunilattāṭku - periya nilamākiya maṭantikku, oru maṇittilakam pōlum - oppaṟṟa aḻakiya tilakam pōlvatākiya, uñcayiṉikku nātaṉ - uccayiṉi nakarattukku aracaṉāy, arumaṇi muṭikoḷ ceṉṉi aracu - ariya maṇikaḷāl iyaṉṟa muṭiyaṇinta taliyiṉi yuṭiya aracarkaḷi, aṭippaṭuttu - taṉṉaṭi vaṇaṅkac ceytu, uyarnta - ōṅkiya, kurumaṇik kuṭiyiṉ nīḻal - niṟamuṭiya māṇikkaṅkaḷ vittiḻitta koṟṟak kuṭiyiṉ nīḻalilē, kuvalayam - ulakam taṅka, kāval koṇṭāṉ - āṭcipuriyattalip paṭṭāṉ e-ṟu.
acōkaṉ tuṟavupūṇa niṉintavuṭaṉē yacōtaraṉi muṭi cūṭṭi aracaṉākkiviṭṭāṉātalāl, “uñcayiṉikkunātaṉ” eṉṟār. irunilattāḷ - nilamakaḷ, periyanakaraṅkaḷi nilamakaṭkut tilakamākak kūṟuvatu kavimarapu; “nilamakaḷ mukamō tilakamō kaṇṇō”1 eṉṟu kamparum kūṟutal kāṇka. nāṭukākkum vēntar ēṉiyaracarit taṉ aṭippaṭutti uyartalē taṉ araciyaṟkuc ciṟappeṉṟum, valikku māṇpeṉṟum karutupavātaliṉ, ataṉic ceytāṉ yacōtaraṉ eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. koṟṟakkuṭi veḷḷitāyiṉum māṇikkaṅkaḷum iḻikkap peṟutalpaṟṟi, “kurumaṇikkuṭi” eṉṟār. kuvalayam - nilamākiya vaṭṭam.
pūṇāṇākiya icōtaraṉ nātaṉāyk kāvalpūṇṭāṉ eṉka. kuvalayaṅkāval - vikāram.
yacōtaraṉ celvac cerukkāl 
aṟattiṟam nekiḻttal 
86.	tiruttaku kumaraṉ celvac cerukkiṉāl nerukkap paṭṭē
	marutteṟi kaṭaliṟ poṅki maṟukiya maṉatta ṉāki
	urutteḻu ciṉattiṟ ceṉṟa vuḷḷameym moḻiyō ṭoṉṟi
	arutticey tarutta kāmat taṟattiṟa maṟattu ṟantāṉ.
uri:- tiruttaku kumaraṉ - tirumakaḷiṉ viruppam takkirukkiṉṟa aracakumaraṉāṉa yacōtaraṉ, celvaccerukkiṉāl - celvamikutiyāl uḷatākum mayakkattāl, nerukkuppaṭṭu - aṭarppuṇṭu, maruttu eṟi kaṭaliṉ - kāṟṟāl mōtappaṭṭa kaṭal poṅki alamaruvatupōla, poṅki maṟukiya maṉattaṉāki - avāmutaliyavaṟṟāl poṅkik kalaṅkiya maṉamuṭiyaṉāy, uruttu eḻu ciṉattiṉ - mikku eḻukiṉṟa ciṉamutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ, ceṉṟa - paṭarkiṉṟa, uḷḷam mey moḻiyōṭu oṉṟi - maṉam, uṭal, col eṉṟa mūṉṟum oruppaṭṭu niṟka, arutta kāmattu arutti ceytu - poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷilē vēṭki mikak koṇṭu, aṟattiṟam aṟat tuṟantāṉ - aṟattiṉ vakikaḷi muṟṟum kiviṭṭoḻukuvāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
puṇṇiyamuḷḷaḷavum oruvaṉippaṟṟi niṉṟu, atu nīṅkiya vaḻi nīṅku miyalpuṭiya tirumakaḷ, puṇṇiyamuṭimiyāl aracapōkan tuykkum yacōtaraṉpāl nīṅkātirukkiṉṟami tōṉṟa “tiruttaku kumaraṉ” eṉṟum, tāṉ peṟṟa celvattiṟkut tāṉ mēṟpaṭṭa talivaṉātali yuṇarātu, ataṟku aṭimiyāyt taṉ iyaṟkiyaṟivi yiḻantāṉeṉṟaṟkuc “cerukkiṉāl nerukkuppaṭṭu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. “puṇṇiyamulantapiṉ poruḷilārkaḷik, kaṇṇilar tuṟantiṭuṅ kaṇikimārkaḷ pōl, eṇṇilaḷ ikaḻntiṭum”1 eṉṟum, “paṟṟiṉar pālaḷ”2 eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. taṉṉiyuṭiyāṉ eḷiyaṉāyiṉ, avaṉi mayakkuvatu celvattiṉ ciṟappiyalpātaliṉ, “celvac cerukkiṉāl nerukkuppaṭṭā” ṉeṉṟumām; “maṉṉal ciṟitāy mayakkam peritākip puṉmiyuṟukkum puriyil arumporuḷ” eṉṟu vaḷiyāpatiyum kūṟiṟṟu. “marutteṟikaṭal” eṉṟa uvamiyāl, avā mutaliyavaṟṟāl yacōtaraṉ alikkap paṭṭami peṟutum. ciṉatti vitantōti yataṉāl, ataṟkētuvāya kāmamum, payaṉākiya mayakkamum koḷka. kāmavekuḷi mayakkaṅkaḷē ataṟku neṟiyāyiṉa miyiṉ, anneṟikkaṇ avaṉuṭiya niṉivum ceyalum collum oruppaṭṭu nilaviṉa eṉpār, “ciṉattiṟceṉṟa uḷḷa meymmoḻiyōṭoṉṟi” eṉṟār. ivviyalpuṭiyārkku vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉa kāmaviṉ pamum, ataṉi iṉitu nukarat tuṇi ceyyum poruḷumēyātaliṉ avaṟṟi, “arutti ceytu”, “aṟattiṟam aṟattuṟantāṉ” eṉṟār. arutti - ārvam.
poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷi arutti 
ceytoḻukum tiṟam kūṟal
87.	añcuta lilātu tevva raviyamē laṭarttuc ceṉṟu
	vañcaṉi palavu nāṭi vakuppaṉa vakuttu maṉṉaṉ
	puñciya poruḷu nāṭum puṇartiṟam puṇarntu neñcil
	tuñcuta lilāta kaṇṇaṉ tuṇivaṉa tuṇintu niṉṟāṉ.
uri:- tevvar aviya - taṉakku añcip paṇintoḻukāta pakivēntar vali keṭumāṟu, añcutal ilātu - accam ciṟitu miṉṟi, mēlceṉṟu - paṭiyuṭaṉ mikkuc ceṉṟu porutu, vañcaṉi palavum nāṭi - avar ceyyum vañcaṉi poruṭṭut tāṉum vañcaṉikaḷ palavaṟṟiyum nāṭic ceytu, puñciya poruḷum - avaruṭiya mikkatākiya poruḷum. nāṭum - ataṟku varuvāyākiya nāṭum, puṇartiṟam - tāṉ kavarntu kōṭaṟkuriya cūḻccittiṟaṅkaḷi, neñcil puṇarntu - maṉattē ārāyntu koṇṭu, tuñcutal ilāta kaṇṇaṉ - nāḷum kaṇṇuṟakkam illāṉāy, tuṇivaṉa tuṇintu - ceyyattakuvaṉavaṟṟic ceytu, niṉṟāṉ - attuṟiyilē teḷi viṉṟi yoḻukuvāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
paki vēntari veṉṟu oṭukki nāṭṭil nalakkuṟivu nikaḻā vakiyiṟ kāttal vēntarkku iyalpāyiṉum, acceyaṟkaṇ aṟamallatu nikaḻvatu eṇṇi yañcuvatu nalvēntar maṉappaṇpāka, ivaṉ, ataṟku añciṉāṉilli yeṉṟaṟku, “añcutalilātu” eṉṟum, appakiyaracar vañcaṉi ceytārāyiṉum, aḵtu aṟamaṉ ṟeṉa voḻiyātu tāṉum avaṟṟic ceytami tōṉṟa, “vakuppaṉa vakuttu” eṉṟum, avarkkuriya poruḷum nāṭum kavarum tiṟamē niṉintu, taṉ poruḷum nāṭum mikutaṟkēṟpaṉa vaṟṟi niṉintila ṉeṉṟaṟku, “puṇartiṟam neñciṟ puṇarntu”  eṉṟum, niṉivoṭu nillātu ceyaṟkaṇṇum ceytoḻukiṉā ṉeṉpār. “tuṇivaṉa tuṇintu niṉṟāṉ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itaṉāl, “aṟattiṟam aṟattuṟan tāṉ” poruḷ ceyal kūṟappaṭṭatu.
88.	tōṭalār kōti mātar tuyariyiṟ ṟoṭutta viṉpap
	pāṭalo ṭiyinta paṇṇiṉ iciccuvi parukip palkāl
	ūṭalaṅ kiṉiya miṉṉiṉ olkiya makaḷi rāṭum
	nāṭakam nayantu kaṇṭum nāḷcila cellac ceṉṟāṉ.
uri:- tōṭu alar kōtimātar - itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya pūkkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa māliyaṇinta kūntaliyuṭiya pāṭal makaḷir, tuyariyiṉ toṭutta - yāḻnarampiṉāl toṭuttup pāṭum, iṉpap pāṭaloṭu iyinta paṇṇiṉ - iṉpattit tarum pāṭaloṭu iyintu niṟkum paṇṇiṭattu eḻum, icic cuvi paruki - iciyākiya amutattip paruki, palkāl - paṉmuṟiyum, ūṭal aṅku iṉiya - ūṭiya viṭattum puṇarntāṟ pōlum iṉpattit tarukiṉṟa, miṉṉiṉ olkiya - miṉṉa lippōla acintāṭum, makaḷir āṭalmakaḷir, āṭum nāṭakam nayantu kaṇṭum - āṭukiṉṟa nāṭakaṅ kaḷi virumpip pārttu makiḻntum, nāḷ cila cella - nāṭkaḷ cila kaḻiya, ceṉṟāṉ - oḻukivaralāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
itaṉāl iṉpatti arutticeytu oḻukum tiṟam kūṟukiṉṟār. tōṭu - pūvitaḻ. tuyari - yāḻnarampu; “tūmam kamaḻum kōti toṭuttatuyarimuliyāt, tēmeṉkītam pālāc curantu”1 eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. “paṇṇeṉṉām pāṭaṟ kiyipiṉṟēl”2 eṉpavākaliṉ, “pāṭaloṭiyinta paṇṇiṉ” eṉṟār. kāmakkāta luṭiya makaḷir ūṭutalum kāmattiṟ kiṉpamātaliṉ, “uṭalaṅkiṉiya makaḷir” eṉṟār; “ūṭiṉum puṇarnta tottiṉiyavaḷ”3 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟuvar. paruki yeṉpuḻi - eṇṇummi vikārattāl tokkatu.
yacōtaraṉ amirmatiyoṭu kūṭi yiṉpuṟum ciṟappu
89.	maṟṟornāḷ maṉṉar tammi maṉipuka viṭuttu mālik
	koṟṟavē lavaṉṟaṉ kōyiṟ kuḷirmaṇik kūṭa moṉṟiṟ
	cuṟṟuvār tiriyiṟ ṟūmaṅ kamaḻtuyiṟ cēkki tuṉṉik
	kaṟṟivār kavari vīcak kaḷiciṟan tiṉiti runtāṉ.
uri:- maṟṟu ōr nāḷ - vēṟorunāḷ, maṉṉartammi - aracavikkaṇ vantirunta aracarkaḷi, maṉipukaviṭuttu - tattam maṉikkuc cellaviṭuttu, māli koṟṟam - māliyum veṟṟiyu muṭiya, vēlavaṉ - vēlēntiya vēntaṉākiya yacōtaraṉ, taṉ kōyil - taṉ antappurattēyuḷḷa, kuḷirmaṇik kūṭam oṉṟil - kuḷirnta oḷivīcum maṇikaḷāl iyaṉṟa oru kūṭattil amikkap paṭṭirunta, cuṟṟuvār tiriyiṉ - cuṟṟilum nīṇṭa tirikaḷi yuṭiya, tūmam kamaḻ - naṟumpukiyiṉ maṇamkamaḻum, tuyil cēkki tuṉṉi - paḷḷi yaṇi yiyaṭintu, kaṟṟivār kavari vīca - kaṟṟiyākavuḷḷa cāmariyi makaḷir vīca, kaḷiciṟantu - kāmakkaḷippu mikuntu, iṉitu iruntāṉ - amirtamatiyiṉ varavunōkki iṉi tiruntāṉ e-ṟu.
“maṉṉartammi maṉipukaviṭuttu” eṉavē, iravuppōtiṉ varavu kuṟittavāṟu peṟṟām. pakaṟpōtil makaḷir toṭutta pāṭṭum, āṭiya nāṭakamum kaṇṭu kāmam ciṟantu antappurattiyaṭi kiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, nērē paḷḷiyaṟiyit tuṉṉiṉāṉ eṉṟār. “kuḷirmaṇikkūṭam” eṉṟār, kāmaveppattāl vetumpivarumāṟu tōṉṟa. kaḷi - kāmakkaḷippu. makaḷir kavari vīciyatu, kāmaveppattik taṇittaṟku. iṉitiruntāṉ eṉṟār, amirtamatipāl tāṉ nukara virukkum kāmaviṉpamē niṉintiruttaliṉ.
90.	cilampoṭu cilampit tēṉun tirumaṇi vaṇṭum pāṭak
	kalampala vaṇinta valkuṟ kaliyoli kalavi yārppa
	nalaṅkalan tiṉiya* kāmar naṟumalart toṭiya lēpōl
	alaṅkalaṅ kuḻalpiṉ tāḻa amiḻtamuṉ matiya ṇintāṉ.
uri:- tēṉum - tēṉiṉamum, tirumaṇi vaṇṭum - aḻakiya nīla maṇipōlum vaṇṭiṉamum, pāṭa - pāṭṭicikka, kalam pala - aṇikalam palavaṟṟōṭu, alkul aṇinta kaliyoli - alku liṭattē yaṇinta mēkaliyiṉ oliyāṉatu , cilampoṭu kalavi cilampiyārppa - cilampoliyoṭukalantu mikkolikka, nalam calantu - peṇminalam mutirntu, iṉiya kāmar naṟumalart toṭiyal pōl - iṉiya aḻakiya maṇaṅkamaḻum pūmāli varutalpōla, alaṅkal - pūmāli cūṭiya, am kuḻal piṉtāḻ - aḻakiya kūntalāṉatu mutukiṭattē tāḻntaciya. amiḻtamuṉmati amutamati, aṇintāḷ - yacōtaraṉirunta paḷḷiyi yaṭintāḷ e-ṟu.
tēṉum vaṇṭum pāṭa, kaliyoli, cilampoṭu kalavi, cilampiyārppa,kuḻal piṉ tāḻa. aṇintāḷ eṉa iyiyum, iṉik,kiṭantapaṭiyē, tēṉum vaṇṭum cilampippāṭa, kaliyoli ēṉikkalaṉkaḷuṭaṉ kalviyārppa, kuḻal piṉtāḻa aṇintāḷ eṉiṉumām. tēṉ, vaṇṭiṉattuḷ oṉṟu, “vaṇṭukāḷ makiḻ tēṉiṉaṅkāḷ”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. kali, mēkali. naṟumalart toṭiyal aṇivatupōla aṇintāḷ eṉka; “maruvaḷarmāliyor vallibṉālki yaṉanaṭi vāyn, turuvaḷar kāmaṉṟaṉ veṉṟik koṭipōṉ ṟoḷirkiṉṟatē”2 eṉṟu tiruk kōviyāruḷḷum varutal kāṇka.
91.	āṅkava ḷaṇinta pōḻti ṉiṅkaṇik kuricil tanta
	pūṅkaṇi māri veḷḷam porutuvan talippap pulli
	nīṅkala roruva ruḷpuk kiruvaru moruva rākit
	tēṅkama ḻamaḷi tēmpac ceṟintaṉar tiḷittu viḷḷār.
uri:- āṅku - avviṭattiṟku, avaḷ aṇinta pōḻtiṉ - avaḷ vantaṭintapōtu, aiṅkaṇik kuricil - aivakip pūkkaḷālākiya ampukaḷiyuṭiya maṉmataṉ, tanta - viṭutta, pūṅkaṇimāri - malarampu maḻipōl vantu vīḻa, veḷḷam porutu vantu alippa - kāmaveḷḷamāṉatu aṟivu mutaliya karikaḷi alittukkoṇṭu vantu avartam neñci varutta, iruvarum pulli - iruvarum tammiltaḻuvi, nīṅkalar - nīṅkārāy, oruvar uḷ oruvar pukku - oruvar uṭaṟkuḷ oruvar pukuntu, oruvarāki - iruvarum oruvarēyāy, tēmkamaḻ amaḷi tēmpa - tēṉmaṇakkum paṭukkiyacin tolikka, ceṟintaṉar - puṇarntu, tiḷittu viḷḷār - oruvaroruvarip piṇitta ki nekiḻātu aṇimītē kiṭantaṉar e-ṟu.
malarampukaḷi maḻipōlac corintataṉāl, ataṉ payaṉākiya kāmavēṭki veḷḷamāyp perukutaliṉ, “pūṅkaṇi māriveḷḷam” eṉṟār. kāmam kimmikkavaḻi aṟivum, niṟiyum, piṟavum nīṅkutaliṉ, avaṟṟik kariyākki, “poruntuvantalippa” eṉpārāyiṟṟu. oruvaruḷ oruvar pukkup puṇarum puṇarcciyi nākar puṇarcci yeṉpar. “nañcuṟṟa kāmam naṉinākariṟṟuytta vāṟum”1 eṉṟaviṭattu, “nākarōṭuvami oruṭampātalum nīṅkalvaṉmiyum paṟṟi” eṉa varum ataṉ uri kāṇka. viḷḷal - nīṅkutal.
92.	maṭaṅkaṉin tiṉiya nallāḷ vaṉamulip pōka mellām
	aṭaṅkala ṉayarntu tēṉvā yamirtamum paruki yampoṟ
	paṭaṅkalan* takaṉṟa valkuṟ pāmpaṇi †puṇiya tāka
	iṭaṅkaḻin toḻivi liṉpak kaṭaliṭi mūḻki ṉāṉē.
uri:- maṭam kaṉintu - iḷami mikuntu, iṉiya - iṉiyaḷākiya, nallāḷ vaṉamulip pōkamellām - nalla amutamatiyiṉ aḻakiya muliyiṭattē peṟum iṉpa muḻutum, aṭaṅkalaṉ ayarntu - aṭaṅkāta ārvattōṭu nukarntu, tēṉvāy amirtamum paruki - eyiṟṟiṭattūṟiya tēṉpōlum nīripparuki, ampoṉ pāmpu paṭam kalantu - aḻakiya pāmpiṉ paṭattiyottu, akaṉṟa alkul aṇi - akaṉṟa alkulākiya paṭukkiyiyē, puṇiyatāka - teppamākak koṇṭu, iṭam kaḻintu - neñciṭam nirampi vaḻintum, oḻivil - nīṅkutalillāta, iṉpakkaṭaliṭi - kāmaviṉpak kaṭaṟkuḷḷē, mūḻkiṉāṉ - vīḻntu kariyēṟa māṭṭātu ataṉuḷḷē mūḻki viṭṭāṉ e-ṟu.
maṭam - iḷami; aḻakumām. muṉṉēkūṭi, yacōmati yeṉṉum makaṉip peṟṟuḷāṉāyiṉum, yacōtaraṉ avaḷpāl vēṭki yaṭaṅkāṉāyiṉāṉeṉṟaṟku, “aṭaṅkalaṉayarntu” eṉṟār. kaḻi kāmattaṉeṉpatu karuttu. eyiṟūṟiyanīr, “pāloṭu tēṉkalantaṟṟu”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutaliṉ, “tēṉvāyamirtam” eṉṟār. parukutali, atarapāṉam eṉpar. pāmpaṇi eṉpatu pirittukkūṭṭappaṭṭatu; alkulākiya pāmpaṇiyeṉṟu kōṭalu moṉṟu. pāmpiṉ paṭamum puṇiyum muṟiyē uruvum toḻilum paṟṟi vantaṉa. iṭamkaḻital - neñcakam nirampit tuḷumputal, “iṭaṅkaḻikāma moṭaṭaṅkāṉāki”2 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟupa.
puṇarcci vacattāl iruvarum kaṇṇuṟaṅkal
93.	iṉṉaric cilampun tēṉum eḻilvaḷi niriyu mārppap
	poṉṉavir tārō ṭāram puṇarmuli porutu poṅka
	maṉṉaṉum maṭanti tāṉum mataṉakō pattiṉ* māṟāyt
	toṉṉalan toliya uṇṭār tuyilkoṇṭa viḻika ḷaṉṟē.
uri:- iṉ aric cilampum - iṉiya ōci ceyyum paralkaḷi yuṭiya kāṟcilampum, tēṉum - vaṇṭiṉamum, eḻil vaḷiniriyum - aḻakiya variciyuṟṟa vaḷiyalkaḷum, ārppa - olikka, poṉ avirtārōṭu - yacōtaraṉaṇinta poṉṉāli yaṉṟa māliyum, āram - amutamati yaṇinta muttumāliyum, puṇarmuli porutu poṅka - puṇarcci nikaḻumiṭattu, muliyāl tākkuṇṭu mēleḻu, maṉṉaṉum maṭantitāṉum - yacōtaraṉum amutamatiyum, mataṉa kōpattiṉ - kāmak kaḷimayakkiṉāl, tolnalam māṟāyt toliya - puṇarātamuṉpirunta mēṉinalam māṟupaṭṭu meliya, uṇṭār - kāmaviṉpattit tuyttu āyntārāka, viḻikaḷ tuyil koṇṭa - avaruṭiya kaṇkaḷ uṟaṅkuvaṉavāyiṉa e-ṟu.
tār, mārpilaṇiyum māli; iḵtu āṭavarkku urittu, tār eṉap pirittamiyiṉ, āram amirtamati yaṇinta muttumālik kāyiṟṟu. “mintar taṇṭār makaḷir peyyavum”3 eṉpuḻittār āṭavarkkurittākak kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka, kāmavēṭkiyāl neñcu kalaṅkutalpaṟṟi, “māṟāy” eṉṟāreṉṟumām. “toṉṉalam toliyavuṇṭār” eṉṟār mīṭṭum avar ivviṉpam nukarā toḻivatu karuti. uṟakkatti avar viṉiyākkātu, viḻimēl ēṟṟiyataṉāl, uṭal ayarntaṉarēyaṉṟi, uḷḷattāl kāmanukarccik kaṇ ayarvuṟṟilareṉṟu koḷka.
aṭṭapaṅkaṉ icitta iciyāl amirtamati 
aṟivu tirital
94.	āyiṭi yatti kūṭat tayaleḻun tamirta mūṟac
	cēyiṭic ceṉṟōr kītañ cevipuka viṭutta lōṭum
	vēyiṭit tōḷi mella viḻittaṉaḷ viyantu nōkkāt
	tīyiṭi meḻuki ṉinta cintiya† ḷuruki ṉāḷē.
uri:- āyiṭi - appōtu, attikūṭattu ayal eḻuntu - yāṉi kaṭṭumiṭattiṉ puṟattē eḻuntu, amirtam ūṟa - kēṭpōr uḷattē iṉpamuṇṭākumāṟu, cēyiṭic ceṉṟa - neṭuntolivu paṭarntu makkaḷ icikkiṉṟa, or kītam - oru pāṭṭu, cevipuka viṭuttalōṭum - cevikkaṭceṉṟa taṉ iciyiṉ pattiyūṭṭavē, vēyiṭittōḷi - mūṅkilpōlum tōḷi yuṭiya amirtamati, mella viḻittaṉaḷ - taṉ kaṇkaḷi mellat tiṟantu, viyantu nōkkā - avviciyiṉ patti viyappōṭu kēṭṭu maruṇṭu nāṟṟiciyum pārttu, tīyiṭi meḻukiṉ - neruppiliṭṭa meḻukupōla, ninta cintiyaḷ - melinta maṉattiyuṭiyaḷāy, urukiṉāḷ - urukalāṉāḷ e-ṟu.
āyiṭi - cuṭṭu nīṇṭu yakaram peṟṟatu. yāṉikkūṭattiṉ kaṇ irunta aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṭutticitta pāṭṭu eṅkum parantu, kēṭpōr cevivaḻic ceṉṟu iṉpatti yuṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu eṉpatām. ceṉṟa eṉṉum peyareccat takaram vikārattāl tokkatu. iṉṉici ceṉṟu amirtamatiyiṉ ceviyiṭiyicittalum avaḷ tuyiluṇarn tāḷākaliṉ, “mella viḻittaṉaḷ” eṉṟār. viḻittāṭku avviciyiṉ iṉpam neñciṉi yurukki melivitta teṉṟaṟku, “tīyiṭi meḻukiṉninta cintiyaḷurukiṉāḷē” eṉṟār.
amirtamati matimayaṅkutal
95.	paṇṇiṉuk koḻuku neñciṟ pāviyip paṇkoḷ cevvāy
	aṇṇaluk kamirta māya variviyark kuriya pōkam
	viṇṇiṉuk kuḷateṉ ṟeṇṇi veytuyirt tuytal cellāḷ
	maṇṇiṉuk karacaṉ tēvi matimayak kuṟṟi runtāḷ.
uri:- paṇṇiṉukku - pāṭṭiṉ pattukku, oḻukum neñciṉ pāvi - nīrāy uruki yōṭum neñciṉiyuṭiya pāvi pōṉṟa amutamati, ippaṇkoḷ cevvāy aṇṇalukku - ivviciya mutattic coriyum civanta vāyiyuṭiya periya vaṉiṭattē, ariviyarkku uriyamakaḷir peṟutaṟkurittāyuḷḷa, amirtamāya pōkam - pēriṉpattit taravalla kāmapōkam, viṇṇiṉukku uḷatu - viṇṇulakattē peṟum pōkamākum, eṉṟu eṇṇi - eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē niṉittu, veytuyirttu - perumūcceṟintu, uytal cellāḷ - uyvakiyaṟiya māṭṭāḷāy, maṇṇiṉukku aracaṉ tēvi - avantināṭṭuk karacaṉākiya yacōtaraṟku maṉiviyākiya ivaḷ, matimayakku uṟṟu - aṟivu kalaṅki, iruntāḷ - kiyaṟavu paṭṭiruntāḷ e-ṟu.
oḻukutal - nīrā yuruki vaḻil; “oḻukunīr āralpārkkam kuruku”1 eṉpuḻippōl. muṉṉic ceyyuḷil “urukiṉāḷ” eṉṟārātaliṉ, īṇṭu “oḻukum neñciṉ” eṉa vāḷāmoḻintār. nāṇvaḻi yoḻukum pāvipōla, paṇṇiṉ cuvivaḻiyōṭum neñciṉaḷātalpaṟṟi, “pāvi” yeṉṟār. itaṉāl maṉavuṇar viṉmiyum peṟa vittārāyiṟṟu. ceviccuvi yamutamākiya iciyiṉi vaḻaṅkum ciṟappu nōkki, “cevvāyaṇṇal” eṉa aṭṭapaṅkaṉik karutukiṉṟāḷ. aṇṇalukku, viṇṇiṉukku eṉpuḻi ēḻāvataṉkaṇ nāṉkāvatu vantu mayaṅkiṟṟu. amirtamāya pōkam, ariviyarkkuriya pōkam eṉa iyiyum. uḷatu - uḷatākiya pōkam. viṇṇiṉuk kuḷateṉṟu eṇṇiya tuṇiyē, peṟutaṟ karitupōlum eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu varuntaluṟṟāḷ eṉpār, “veytuyirt tuytal cellāḷ” eṉṟār. maṇṇiṉuk karacaṉ tēviyāy iṉpakkuṟi pāṭiṉṟiyē iruntāḷāyiṉum, aṟivaṟi pōki aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl pollāk kāmavēṭki koṇṭu maṉam puḻuṅkum puṉmikaṇṭu ikaḻvār, “maṇṇiṉuk karacaṉ tēvi” eṉṟu vitantōtiṉār.
peṇmiyiṉ puṉmi kūṟal
96.	miṉṉiṉu niliyiṉ ṟuḷḷam viḻivuṟiṉ viḻinta yāvum*
	tuṉṉiṭu miṉattiṉ tūymi cūḻcciyu moḻiya niṟkum†
	piṉṉuṟu paḻiyiṟ kañcā peṇṇuyirp perumi pēṇā
	teṉṉumim moḻikaṭ kantō vilakkiya māyi ṉāḷē.
uri:- peṇ - peṇkaṭku, uḷḷam - maṉamāṉatu, miṉṉiṉum niliyiṉṟu - miṉṉalikkāṭṭilum niliyillātatu, viḻi vuṟiṉ - evaṟṟiyēṉum virumpumāyiṉ, viḻinta - avvirumpiyavaṟṟip paṟṟiya, yāvum tuṉṉiṭum - niṉivu ceyalkaḷiyē poruntiyirukkum, iṉattiṉ tūymi - tuṇiyā yiṉār kūṟum tūymiyum, cūḻcciyum - ārāycciyuriyum, oḻiya - keṭa, niṟkum - tāṉ cellakkarutiya neṟiyilēyē ūṉṟi niṟkum, piṉṉuṟu paḻiyiṟku añcā - piṉṉē eytukiṉṟa paḻi pāvaṅkaṭkuc ciṟitum añcātu, uyirpperumi pēṇātu - peṇṇuyirākiya tāṉ piṟanta kuṭipperumiyum karutātu, eṉṉum immoḻikaṭku - eṉṟu aṟintōr aṟinturitta ivvilakkaṇaṅkaṭku, ilakkiya māyiṉāḷ - ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭāyiṉāḷ, antō - aiyō, ivvamutamatiyiṉ iyalpu iruntavāṟu eṉṉē e-ṟu.
“peṇṇuyir..... pērāttiṇmiyum uṭiyavalla” (47) eṉa muṉpē kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, īṇṭu vāḷā uvamamukattāl, “miṉṉiṉum niliyiṉṟuḷḷam” eṉṟum, oru poruṇmēṟ cellum viḻiviṉi nīkkiniṟuttum matukiyilli yeṉpatiyum muṉṉar, “peruntiṟa laṟivum uṭiyavalla” (46) eṉṟataṉi viḷakki, “viḻivuṟiṉ viḻintayāvum tuṉṉiṭum” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. viḻivu tōṉṟiya viṭattu, avviḻiviṉi niṟivu ceytu kōṭaṟkētuvāya niṉivu ceyalē avar uḷḷattiṟ porunti vēṟu evvakic ceyaṟkaṇṇum cellātoḻitaliṉ, “viḻinta yāvum tuṉṉiṭum” eṉṟār; viḻinta veṉṉum peyareccam karuviyoṭu muṭintatu. tuṇiyāy oḻukum kuṇavati yeṉpāḷatu tūymiyum aṟivuriyum keṭac ceyyum ivaḷatu ceyal mēlvarum nikaḻccikaḷāl uṇarappaṭum. añcāteṉpatu- īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟatu. uyirpperumi, īṇṭuk kuṭippiṟappiṉ mēṟṟu, ivvamutamatiyiṉ - eṉpatu mutalāyiṉa kuṟippeccam.
kuṇavati eṉṉum tōḻi uṟṟatu viṉavutal
97.	tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅkat tuṇimuli tamiya ḷāki
	iṉṉici yavaṉi neñcat tiruttiṉa ḷirunta ellit
	tuṉṉiṉaḷ tōḻi tuṉṉit tuṇivaril tamiya rēpōṉ
	ṟeṉṉitu niṉinta tuḷḷat tiṟivinī yaruḷu keṉṟāḷ.
uri:- tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅka - poruntiyirunta irāppōtu kaḻiya, tuṇimuli tamiyaḷāki - iraṇṭākiya mulikaḷiyuṭiya amutamati ōriṭattē taṉittiruntu, iṉṉici yavaṉi - iṉiya iciyiyuṭiyaṉākiya aṭṭapaṅkaṉi, neñcattu iruttiṉaḷ - maṉattē kāmakkātalaṉākak koṇṭu, irunta elli - vēṟuceyal maṟantu iruntapōtu, tōḻi tuṉṉiṉaḷ - uyirttōḻiyākiya kuṇavati avaḷiyaṭintāḷ, tuṉṉi - (aṭintavaḷ) amutamatiyi neruṅki, iṟivi - araciyē, tuṇivaril tamiyar pōṉṟu - maṉakkiṉiya kātalar uṭaṉillāmiyāl taṉittuppulampum makaḷirip pōla, itu - vēṟupaṭṭirukkum icceyal, eṉ - eṉṉi, nī uḷḷattu niṉintatu - nī niṉmaṉattil karutiyataṉi, aruḷuka - aṭiyēṉukkut terivittaruḷka, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ e-ṟu.
“tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅka” eṉa vitantōtiyatu, aḵtu avaṭku ōr ūḻiyāyt tōṉṟi neṭitu kaḻintami yuṇaraniṉṟatu. yacōtaraṉōṭu kūṭiyiruppavum, neñcilē “iṉṉiciyavaṉi” iruttikkoṇṭamiyiṉ, avaṉiyiṉṟi yirukkumatu avaṭkut taṉimiyāyiṟ ṟeṉṟaṟkut “tamiyaḷāki” eṉṟār. yacōtaraṉiyum ēṉittōḻimārkaḷiyum nīṅkit taṉittiruntami tōṉṟa, “tamiyaḷāki” eṉṟāreṉṟalumām. tuṉṉi yeṉṟa mikiyāl, kuṇavati pōtarakkaṇṭum avaḷi varavēṟkātu tāṉ vāṭiya mukattuṭaṉ cāmpiyiruntamiyum, avaḷ tiṭukkiṭṭu niṉṟu nōkki mella arukaṇintamiyum, amutamatiyiṉ maṟukiya neñcamum miyalnōkkamum meyppaṭṭut tōṉṟiṉamiyum peṟṟām. iṟivaṉākiya yacōtaraṉuṭaṉ piriviṉṟik
kūṭiyiruttaliṉ, “iṟili” yeṉṟum, pirivāl meliyum makaḷirkkuri mēṉi vēṟupāṭukoṇṭaliṉ, “tuṇivaril tamiyar pōṉṟu” eṉṟum, kāraṇam kāṇamāṭṭāmiyiṉ, “eṉṉitu” veṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
amirtamati uṟṟatu urittal
98.	tavaḷavāḷ nakiyāy* taṇṭār maṉṉavaṉ takimi yeṉṉuṅ
	kavaḷamā rakatteṉ ṉuḷḷak karuṅkaḷi matanal yāṉi
	pavaḷavāy maṇikki koṇṭa paṇṇiyal tōṭṭi paṟṟit
	tuvaḷumā ṟoruvaṉ ellit tuṭakkiṉāṉ ṟuyara† eṉṟāḷ.
uri:- tavaḷavāḷ nakiyāy - veḷḷiya oḷiporuntiya paṟkaḷiyuṭiyāy, taṇtār maṉṉavaṉ - kuḷirnta māliyaṇinta vēntaṉākiya yacōtaraṉuṭiya, takimi yeṉṉum - kātalaṉ peṉṉum, kavaḷam ār - kavaḷattiyuṇkiṉṟa, akattu - iṉpa voḻukkattiyuṭiya, eṉuḷḷam - eṉ maṉamākiya, karuṅkaḷi matanalyāṉi - kariya matakkaḷippiṉiyuṭiya nallayāṉiyi, oruvaṉ - evaṉō voruvaṉ, pavaḷavāy maṇikki - pavaḷam pōlum taṉvāyākiya aḻakiya kiyiṭattē, koṇṭa - vittuḷḷa, paṇ iyal tōṭṭi - iciyākiya aṅkucattāl, paṟṟi - kippaṟṟi, tuvaḷumāṟu - meliyumāṟu, elli - nēṟṟiravu, tuyarattuṭakkiṉaṉ - varunta vaṇakkip piṇittuk koṇṭāṉ e-ṟu.
collāṭaliṉṟit tuyaruṟṟirunta amirtamati cila collat toṭaṅkiyatum kuṇavati mukamalarntu muṟuvalittuk kāṭṭiṉa miyiṉ, “tavaḷavāṇakiyāy” eṉṟāḷ. ippōtu amutamatiyiṉ uḷḷam yacōtaraṉiṟ pirintu nīṅkiṉamiyiṉ, piriyāmi muṉṉirunta nilimiyiṉi, “taṇṭār maṉṉavaṉ takimiyeṉṉum kavaḷam,” eṉṟāḷ. kavaḷam -  yāṉiyuṇṇum uṇavu. ārtal, uṇṭal; “ārntōr vāyiṟṟēṉum puḷikkum” (kuṟun. 354) eṉpuḻippōla. akam, ākupeyarāy iṉpavoḻukkattik kuṟittu niṉṟatu. iciyavaṉ pāl kaḷaviṉāl kāmaviṉpam tuykkak karutukiṉṟāḷātaliṉ, avaḷ uḷḷattik “karuṅkaḷima tanalyāṉi” yeṉṟār. yāṉiyiṉ karumiyi uḷḷat tukkum koḷka, kaḷaviṉāl tīya kāmam nukarak karututaliṉ; “kaḷa veṉṉum kāraṟivāṇmi”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. iciyavaṉ vāyi muṉpu, “paṇkoḷ cevvāy” (95) eṉṟāḷātaliṉ, īṇṭu, “”pavaḷavāy eṉṟāḷ. yāṉi nallicikku vaṇaṅkutal paṟṟi, neñciṉi yāṉiyoṭu uruvakam ceytār, uḷḷatti matayāṉi yeṉṟum, ataṉi iciyāl oruvaṉ vaṇakkiṉaṉ eṉṟum kūṟiyataṉāl, yacōtaraṉāl ataṉi vaṇakka
māṭṭāmiyum, “iṉṉiciyavaṉ” vaḻiyē celvatiyum kuṟittāḷāyiṟṟu. ici yicittavaṉ aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṉpatu aṟiyāmiyāl, “oruvaṉ” eṉṟāḷ. “tuvaḷumāṟu” eṉṟum, “tuyara” veṉṟum kūṟiyatu, avaṉatu iṉṟiyamiyāmiyum taṉatu āṟṟāmiyum uṇarttiniṉṟatu.
kaṇṭatu kaṉaveṉak kuṇavati kaḻaṟal
99.	aṅkava ḷakattuc ceyki yaṟintaṉa ḷalla ḷēpōl*
	koṅkaviḻ kuḻali maṟṟak kuṇavati yuṟaḻntu† kūṟum
	naṅkiniṉ perumi naṉṟē naṉaveṉak kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa
	paṅkama tuḷḷi yuḷḷam parivukoṇ ṭiṉiya‡ leṉṟāḷ.
uri:- aṅku - avviṭattu, avaḷ akattucceyki - amutamati taṉmaṉattē karutiyirukkum ceykiyi, aṟintaṉaḷ - terintu koṇṭāḷāyiṉum, allaḷpōla - teriyātavaḷ pōla, koṅku aviḻ kuḻali akkuṇavati - tēṉporuntiya pūccūṭiya kūntaliyuṭi yaḷākiya akkuṇavati yeṉṉum tōḻi, uṟaḻntu kūṟum - avaḷōṭu māṟupaṭṭukkūṟuvāḷāyiṉaḷ, naṅki - naṅ kiyē, niṉ perumi - niṉak kuḷḷa perumiyō, naṉṟu - peritākum, kaṉaviṉāl - kaṉaviṉkaṇ, naṉaveṉak kaṇṭapaṅkamatu - naṉavupōlat tōṉṟak kaṇṭa iḻitakka nikaḻcciyi, uḷḷi - niṉintu, uḷḷam parivukoṇṭu - maṉattil varuttamuṟṟu, iṉiyal - varuntātē koḷ; eṉṟu urittāḷ e-ṟu.
niṉivuvaṭivākavē niṉṟoḻiyātu ceykikkaṇṇum oṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ, eṇṇameṉṉātu, “avaḷakattuc ceyki” yeṉṟār. amutamatiyiṉ karuttaṟintum ataṉ tīmikaruti vilakkum karuttiṉaḷātaliṉ, “aṟintaṉaḷallaḷēpōl uṟaḻntu kūṟum” eṉṟār. “uyarmoḻik kuriya vuṟaḻuṅkiḷavi”1 eṉa āciriyar tōḻikkum uritteṉṟaliṉ, tōḻi īṇṭu tēviyi uṟaḻntu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. naṉṟē eṉpuḻi ēkāram etirmaṟiyākakkoṇṭu, itu niṉperumikkut tītu eṉṟu urippiṉumām. paṅkam - iḻivu; īṇṭu iḻitakka kaṉaviṉmēṟṟu, iṉivatu pētiyār ceyal eṉpāḷ, “iṉiyal” eṉṟāḷ. maṟṟu - acinili.
amutamati coṟkēṭṭuk kuṇavati añcutal
100.	eṉmaṉat tivaru minnōy* ivaḷaṟin tilaḷko leṉṟē
	taṉmaṉat tiṉiya vaṭkut tāṉurit tiṭuta lōṭum
	niṉmaṉat tilāta colli nīpuṉin taruḷiṟ† ṟeṉkol
	ciṉmalark kuḻali yeṉṟē ceviputit tiṉitu coṉṉāḷ.
uri:- eṉ maṉattu - eṉ neñcil, ivarum - mēṉ mēṉmikum, innōy - ikkāmanōyi, ivaḷ aṟintilaḷ - ivaḷ iṉṉum aṟiyavilli, eṉṟu - eṉṟu amutamati niṉittu, taṉmaṉattiṉi - taṉ maṉattirunta eṇṇaṅkaḷi, avaṭku - akkuṇavatikku, tāṉ - tāṉē vāyviṭṭu, urittiṭutalōṭum - urittalum, cilmalarkkuḻali - cilavākiya pūkkaḷi yaṇinta kūntaliyuṭiyāy, niṉmaṉattu ilāta - niṉneñcil illāta iccoṟkaḷi, nīpuṉintu - nīyē paṭittuk koṇṭu, aruḷiṟṟu - urittatu, eṉkol - eṉṉi, eṉṟu – eṉṟu colli, cevi putittu - taṉ irukātukaḷiyum kikaḷāl pottikkoṇṭu, iṉitu coṉṉāḷ - aṟavuriyik kuṇavati coṉṉāḷ e-ṟu.
ivartal - mēlum mikuntēkutal. “ivartanteṉ mēṉi mēlūrum pacappu” 1 eṉpuḻippōla. kolliraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu aiyam, piṉṉa tacinili. kulamakaḷirk kovvāta colli amutamati kūṟutaliṉ, “niṉmaṉattu ilāta col” eṉṟum, atu meyyaṉ ṟeṉṟaṟku “nī puṉintaruḷiṟṟu” eṉṟum, atu nakiyēyum vēṇṭaṟpāṟ ṟaṉṟākaliṉ, “eṉkol” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “puṉinturi yaṉṟu; meyyē” eṉa amutamati vaṟpuṟuttiṉamiyiṉ, kuṇavati poṟātu añciṉami tōṉṟac “ceviputittu” eṉṟum, aṟavuri pakarntami viḷaṅka, “iṉitu coṉṉāḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. aṟavuri iṉpam payappatātaliṉ, “iṉi” teṉṟār; aṅkaṇat tuḷukka vamiḻtupōl payaṉpaṭā toḻin tamiyiṉ, iṉiteṉṟē yoḻintār.
amutamati taṉatu āṟṟāmi kūṟal
101.	māḷavap pañca vappaṇ makiḻntava ṉamuta vāyiṟ
	kēḷala māyi ṉāmuṅ kēḷala mātu māvi
	nāḷava māki yiṉṉē naṭantiṭu naṭuvoṉ ṟilli
	vāḷaḷa vuṇkaṇ* mātē maṟutturi moḻiyi ṉeṉṟāḷ.†
uri:- vāḷ aḷavu uṇkaṇ mātē - oḷikalantamiyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya aṉpuḷḷa tōḻi, avaṉ amutavāyil - anta iṉṉiciyōṉuṭiya amutam poruntiya vāyāl, māḷavappañca vappaṇ - māḷava pañcamam eṉṟa paṇṇiṉ iciyi, makiḻntu  kēḷalamāyiṉ - avaṉik kūṭi makiḻntu kēḷātoḻivō māyiṉ, nāmum kēḷalamātum - nāmum tōḻamiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkiviṭu vōm, maṟuttu urimoḻiyiṉ - nī eṉviruppatti maṟuttumāṟu kūṟuvāyāyiṉ, nāḷ - uyirōṭu kūṭivāḻum vāḻnāḷum, avamāki - payaṉaṟṟatāki, iṉṉē āvinaṭantiṭum - ippoḻutē uyir nīṅkiviṭum, naṭu oṉṟu illi - itaṟkiṭiyē nammi vāḻvippatu vēṟoṉṟum kiṭiyātu e-ṟu.
maṟutturi moḻiyiṉ, nāḷ avamākiya āvi iṉṉē naṭantiṭum eṉa iyit turittuk koḷka. māḷavapañcamam eṉpatu oruvakip paṇ; māḷava kuḷam, kētāra kuḷam eṉpa pōlvatu, iḵtu aṭṭapaṅkaṉ pāṭiyatu. kēḷāvaḻippaṭum payaṉ itu veṉ pāḷ, “nāmum kēḷalamātum” eṉṟum, maṟuttu moḻiyiṉ varum payaṉi, “aviyiṉṉē naṭantiṭum” eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. oṉṟu - nāḷ eṉpuḻic ciṟappummi vikārattāl tokkaṉa. vāḷ - oḷi, aḷavu - aḷavutal, kalattal. ivvāṟu aṟantiṟampiya neṟiyoḻukupavaḷ kēṇmiyum uyirvāḻvum peṟutaliṉum peṟātoḻivatu peṇṇulakiṟkuc ciṟappāmeṉṟu karuti maṟuppiṉum maṟuppaḷeṉṟa eṇṇattāl, varum pāṭṭil, kuṇavatiyuṭaṉ mikanayamāka iv vamutamati pēcutalik kāṇmiṉ.
avviciyavaṉ yāvaṉeṉṟaṟiyak kuṇavati tuṇital
102.	eṉṉuyirk karaṇam niṉṉō ṭiṉṉici puṇartta* kāḷi
	taṉṉiṉmaṟ ṟoruva rillit takkatu tuṇika tāḻkkiṉ†
	eṉṉuyirk kēta meyti ṉitupaḻi peruku meṉṟāḷ‡
	tuṉṉumvā yavaḷō ṭeṇṇāt § tōḻiyu meṇṇi ṉāḷē.
uri:- eṉ uyirkku araṇam - eṉ uyirkkup pātukāp pāvār, niṉṉōṭu - uṉṉōṭu, iṉṉici puṇartta kāḷitaṉṉiṉ - iṉiya iciyippāṭiya akkāḷiyēyaṉṟi, maṟṟu - vēṟē, oruvar illi - oruvarum illi, takkatu tuṇika - ceyyattakuvatu yātō ataṉic ceyka, tāḻkkiṉ - kālam nīṭṭippiṉ, eṉ uyirkku ētam - eṉ uyirkku iṟutiyuṇṭākum, eytiṉ - avvā ṟaḵtu uṇṭāyiṉ, itu - ittāḻkkiyiṉāl, paḻi perukum - peṇpaḻi tōṉṟi niṉvaḻivaḻip perukiniṟkum, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu amutamatiyurit tāḷāka, tōḻiyum - tōḻiyākiya kuṇavatiyum, avaḷōṭu - avvamutamatiyuṭaṉ, tuṉṉum vāy - avviciya vaṉit tēṭi yaṭitaṟkuriya neṟikaḷi, eṇṇi - ārāyntaṟintu, eṇṇiṉāḷ - avaṉ irukkumiṭattit tēṭic cellat tuṇintāḷ e-ṟu.
“uyirkku araṇamāvār ataṟkuk kēṭu ceyyār” eṉpatu paṟṟi, “eṉṉuyirkkaraṇamāvār” eṉṟeṭuttu “niṉṉōṭu” eṉṟu uyarpiṉvaḻittākiya oṭukkoṭuttuc ciṟappittāḷ; oṭu - ōṭeṉa vikāram. tōḻivāyilāka iciyavaṉip peṟum iyipu paṟṟi, avaṭku uyarpu tantāḷ eṉa vaṟika. palaraṟiya iciyavaṉit tēṭik kāṇṭal amutamatiyiṉ karuttu niṟivēṟaṟkākāmiyiṉ, “takkatu tuṇika” eṉṟum, ataṉiyum virintu ceyalvēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku “tāḻkkiṉ ētam” eṉṟum, “paḻiperukum” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. iravup pōtil uṟaṅkuṅkālattē avviṉṉiciyi amutamati kēṭṭāḷā taliṉ, ici vanta pakkam, iciyavaṉuṟiviṭam, ataṉ aṇimi cēyminili, avaṉik kāṇuntiṟam, avaṉi yuṭaṉpaṭu vittaṟku vēṇṭum coṟporuḷkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṉō raṉṉavaṟṟit tōḻi amutamatiyōṭu kalantē yārāyavēṇṭi yiruntamiyiṉ, “tuṉṉum vāy avaḷō ṭeṇṇā” eṉṟum, eṇṇiyapiṉ eṇṇiyāṅkuc ceyvaṉa ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉā ḷeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. eṇṇuvataṉ payaṉ iyaṟṟalē yātaliṉ, “eṇṇiṉāḷ” eṉṟu upacarittār. eṇṇā - ceyyā veṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉi yeccam.
ici pāṭiyavaṉ aṭṭapaṅka ṉeṉṟu kuṇavati makiḻcciyuṭaṉ mīḷutal
103.	maḻukiru ḷiraviṉ viki māḷavap pañca vattēṉ
	oḻukiya miṭaṟṟōr kāḷi yuḷḷava ṉiyāva ṉeṉṟē
	kaḻuturu vavaṉi* nāṭik kaṇṭaṉaḷ kaṇṭu kāmat
	toḻukiya vuḷḷan tiyaṟ koḻiyumeṉ ṟuvantu mīṇṭāḷ.
uri:- iruḷ maḻuku iraviṉ viki - naḷḷiruḷ nekiḻum kaṭiyāmattil taṉ irukkiyilē iruntu, māḷavap pañcavattēṉ  - māḷavapañcamam eṉṟa paṇṇākiya tēṉi, oḻukiya miṭaṟṟu - corinta kaṇṭattiyuṭiya, kāḷiyuḷḷavaṉ - kāḷipparu vattāṉ, yāvaṉ eṉṟu - yāvaṉō  eṉṟu, nāṭi - tēṭic ceṉṟu, kuḻutu uru avaṉi - pēyiṉ vaṭivattiyuṭiya avaṉi, kaṇṭaṉaḷ - kuṇavati taṉkaṇkaḷāl pārttāḷ, kaṇṭu - avvāṟu kaṇṭa taṉāl, tiyaṟku - tiyalākiya amutamatikku, kāmattu oḻukiya uḷḷam - kaḻikāmattuṟiyil perukiyōṭum maṉamāṉatu, oḻiyum - iṉi ataṉiṉṟum nīṅkum, eṉṟu uvantu - eṉṟu niṉintu makiḻntu, mīṇṭāḷ - amutamatipālvantu cērntāḷ e-ṟu.
naṭuviyāmattē ceṟintirukkum tiṇiyiruḷ, kaṭiyāmattē kaṭṭuviṭṭu nekiḻntu maḻukiyiruttaliṉ, ataṉi “maḻukiruḷiraviṉ” eṉṟār. avaḷ accamiṉṟic cēṟaṟkum atu naṉṟātaliṉ, maḻukiruḷi eṭuttōtiṉār. paṇṇiṉit tēṉāka vuruvakañ ceytamiyiṉ, ataṟkēṟpap  pāṭutali oḻukutaleṉṟār. ōr - aci. kāḷiyuḷḷavaṉ yāvaṉ eṉpataṟku, kāḷi pōlvāṉāy uḷḷāṉ oruvaṉ, avaṉ yāvaṉ eṉṟu kūṟalu moṉṟu. kaḻutu - pēy. yāvaṉ eṉṟu nāṭik kaṇṭaṉaḷ eṉa iyikka. kāṇappōnta kuṇavatikku amutamatiyiṉ kāmavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ viruppa miṉmiyiṉ, aṭṭa paṅkaṉatu pēypōlum kāṭci aruvaruppi viḷittatāyiṉum orupāl mikka makiḻcciyē viḷittateṉpār, ataṟku ētuviṉ mēlvittu, “kāmattoḻukiya vuḷḷam tiyaṟ koḻiyum eṉṟu uvantu” eṉṟum, “mīṇṭāḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. “mīṇṭāḷ” eṉṟataṉāl, iṉit taṉakku ittakiya viṉi nikaḻātu; iḵtu ivvaḷavil muṭintoḻintatu eṉṟa karuttuṭiyaḷāyk kuṇavati tirumpiṉami peṟṟām; eṭutta viṉi muṟṟiṉallatu mīḷutal viṉi ceyvār toḻilaṉṟātaliṉ.
kuṇavati tāṉ kaṇṭataṉi amutamatikkuk kūṟal
104.	maṉṉaṉmā tēvi niṉṉi varuttuvāṉ vakutta* kītat
	taṉṉava ṉatti pāka ṉaṭṭamā paṅka ṉeṉpāṉ
	taṉṉimey teriyak kaṇṭē taḷarntukaṇ putittu mīṇṭēṉ
	eṉṉi nī muṉivi yeṉṉā vicikkila† ṉavaṟkī teṉṟāḷ.
uri:- maṉṉaṉmātēvi - araca mātēvi; niṉṉi varuttuvāṉ - niṉ neñcu varuntumpaṭiyāka, vakutta kītattu aṉṉavaṉ - pāṭiya iciyiṉi yuṭiyavaṉ, attipākaṉ - ōr yāṉippākaṉāvāṉ, aṭṭa māpaṅkaṉ eṉpāṉ - aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭuvāṉ, taṉṉi - avaṉi, meyteriyak kaṇṭu - uṭal muḻutum naṉku kaṇṭu, taḷarntu - maṉam cōrntu, kaṇputittu mīṇṭēṉ - kaṇkaḷi mūṭik koṇṭu tirumpiṉēṉ, eṉṉi nī muṉivi eṉṉā - ceṉṟa karumatti avaṟku urippiṉ eṉṉi nī vekuḷvāy eṉṟu añci, ītu avaṟku icikkilaṉ - itaṉi avaṉukku yāṉ collavilli, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu kuṇavati kūṟiṉāḷ e-ṟu.
maṉṉaṉpāl oṉṟi avaṟku urimippaṭṭirunta maṉam nīṅki, ayalāṉ oruvaṉmēṟ ceṉṟamiyiṉ, amutamatiyi, aracaṉtēvi yeṉṉātu, “naṅki” (99) eṉṟum, “ciṉmalarkkuḻali” (100) eṉṟum kūṟippōntavaḷ, iṉi, avvāṟu celvatoḻintu aracaṟkē yurittāṉamiyiṉ, “maṉṉaṉ mātēvi” eṉṟāḷ. aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl taṉakkuṇṭāṉa aruvaruppik kuṇavati, avaṉicitta paṇmē lēṟṟi, “varuttuvāṉ vakutta kītam” eṉṟāḷ. palavēṟupaṇkaḷāka vakukkappaṭutaliṉ, “vakuttakītam” eṉṟār. atti- yāṉi - mā - aci. aṭṭapaṅkaṉ vaṭivu muḻutum viḷaṅkakkaṇṭami tōṉṟa, “mey teriyakkaṇṭu” eṉṟum, avvaṭiviṉ pollāṅkiṉit “taḷarntu kaṇputittu” eṉṟu kuṟittum, ataṉik kāṇiṉ amutamati aṟavē avaṉi veṟuppa ḷeṉṉum tuṇivāl, “eṉṉi nī muṉivi yeṉṉā vicikkila ṉavaṟkī” teṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. taḷarcci amuta matiyiṉ poruṭṭu.
aṭṭapaṅkaṉ vaṭivik kuṇavati kūṟal
105.	narampukaḷ vicitta meyyaṉ naṭiyiṉiṟ kaḻuta ṇintōṉ
	tiraṅkiya viralaṉ kiyaṉ ciṟumukaṉ ciṉavaṉ cīṟiṟ*
	kuraṅkiṉi yaṉiyaṉ† kūṉaṉ kuḻittupuk kaḻinta kaṇṇaṉ
	neruṅkalu niralu miṉṟi nimirntuḷa cilapal leṉṟāḷ.
uri:- narampukaḷ vicitta meyyaṉ - narampukaḷ puṟattē tōṉṟumāṟu eḻunta meyyiṉiyuṭiyavaṉ, naṭiyiṉil kaḻutu aṇintōṉ - naṭiyāl pēyk kōṭpaṭṭāṉi yoppaṉ, tiraṅkiya viralaṉ kiyaṉ - tōlcuruṅkit tēynta viraliyum kiyiyumuṭiyavaṉ, ciṟumukaṉ - ciṟutta mukattiyuṭiyavaṉ, ciṉavaṉ - muṉkōpi, cīṟiṉ kuraṅkiṉiyaṉiyaṉ - ciṉantavaḻi mukam kuraṅkiṉi yoppaṉ, kūṉaṉ - kūṉiya mutuki yuṭiyaṉ, kuḻintu pukku - kuḻintu uṭceṉṟu, aḻintakaṇṇaṉ - itaḻ puṇṇuṟṟu aḻukiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyavaṉ, neruṅkalum niralum iṉṟi - nerukkamum  variciyumiṉṟi aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟu māka, cilapal nimirntu - cilapaṟkaḷē mika nīṇṭu, uḷa - avaṉ vāyil uḷḷaṉa, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu eṭutturittāḷ e-ṟu.
naram peḻuntu vicittatu pōlat tōṉṟaliṉ, “vicitta meyyaṉ” eṉṟāḷ. puḻuti yaḷinta mēṉiyum maruṇṭanōkkamum taḷḷāṭunaṭiyum uṭimiyiṉ, “naṭiyiṉiṟ kaḻutaṇintōṉ” eṉṟāḷ. tiraṅkiya eṉpataṉik kiyaṉ eṉpuḻiyum kūṭṭuka. talikkum uṭaṟkum ovvātu ciṟuttiruttaliṉ, “ciṟumukaṉ” eṉṟum, acciṟumi virivil vekuḷum iyalpiṉikkāṭṭaliṉ “ciṉavaṉ” eṉṟum. ciṉantu cīṟuṅkāl mukam peṟum vaṭiviṉi “cīṟiṟ kuraṅkiṉi yaṉiyaṉ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. kuḻinteṉavē yamiyumāyiṉum, “pukku” eṉṟatu, neṭi tāḻntu kiṭantami tōṉṟa. itaḻpuṇṇūṟṟu mayirutirntu cīyum pīḷiyum nīrum oḻukutal paṟṟi, “aḻinta kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟāḷ; “cilapal” eṉṟum “nimirntuḷa” eṉṟum kūṟiyataṉāl; irukaṭivāyilum muṉṉum mēlum kīḻum ivviraṇṭu paṟkaḷuḷa veṉṟum, muṉṉuḷḷavi mika nīṇṭu puṟattē veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟiṉa veṉṟum koḷka.
iṉṉa iyalpiṉaṉāṉa ivaṉi nī vēṇṭaṟka 
eṉak kuṇavati kūṟal
106. pūtikan tattiṉ meyyiṟ puṇkaḷuṅ kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā
     cātiyun takka taṉṟā lavaṉvayiṟ ṟaḷaru muḷḷam
     nītavirn tiṭuka neñciṉ niṟiyiṉic ciṟicey keṉṟāḷ
     kōtavitaḻn tiṭṭa vuḷḷak kuṇavati kompa ṉāḷē.
uri:- pūti kantattiṉ - puḻuti nāṟṟamum, meyyiṟ  puṇkaḷum - uṭaliṭattēyuḷḷa puṇkaḷum, kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā - kaṇkaḷ tāmum pārttaṟku uṭaṉ paṭā, cātiyum takkataṉṟu - avaṉuṭiya kuṭippiṟappum takutiyuṭi yataṉṟu, avaṉ vayiṉ taḷarum uḷḷam - avaṉ poruṭṭu varunti meliyum niṉmaṉa viruppatti, nī tavirntiṭuka - nī viṭṭoḻittu, neñciṉ - avaṉpāṟcellum niṉ neñcatti, niṟiyiṉi - niṉakku iyalpāyuḷḷa niṟiyik koṇṭu, ciṟiceyka - taṭuttu niṟuttik koḷka, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu parivōṭu kūṟiṉāḷ, kōtu aviḻntiṭṭa - kuṟṟam illāta, uḷḷak kuṇavati - maṉatti yuṭiya kuṇavati yeṉṉum, kompaṉṉāḷ - pūṅkompu pōlum tōḻi e-ṟu.
pūti - puḻuti. kantam - nāṟṟam. kāṇṭaṟku nam neñcu oru kāl virumpiṉum, karuviyākiya kaṇkaḷ ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭā eṉap pārttaṟku mikka aruvaruppuṭiya avaṉatu uṭal vaṭivi yuṇarttu vāḷ, “kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā”  eṉṟāḷ. meyyiṟ puṇ kūṟavē, av vaṭṭapaṅkaṉ kuṭṭa nōyāl varuntutal peṟṟām. cāti, īṇṭu  iṟpiṟappiṉ mēṟṟu. neñciṉ eṉpuḻi aiyurupu, cāriyi niṟkat tāṉniliyātāyiṟṟu. niṟiyiṉi yeṉpuḻik koṇṭu eṉṟu orucol varuvikka. niṟiyiṉi yeṉpuḻi aiyurupip pirittu neñcōṭu kūṭṭi, neñciṉi niṟiyiṉāl ciṟi ceyka eṉṟurip piṉumām. iṉi, neñcu il niṟiyiṉic ciṟi ceyka eṉṟu koṇṭu, neñciṭattē nillā toḻukum niṉ niṟiyiṉi niṟuttuc ciṟi ceyki eṉiṉumamiyum. kōtu, kuṟṟam. kuṇavatiyik “kompaṉāḷ” eṉa vicēṭittataṉāl, amutamatiyākiya pūṅkoṭikkuk koḻukompāy niṉṟu, akkoṭi paṭara virumpum iṭattiṟkuk kompu tuṇiceytu niṟkumāṟu pōla, amutamati virumpum aṭṭa paṅkaṉi aṭintu makiḻtaṟku ikkuṇavati tuṇiyāvatu peṟṟām.
kuṇavati kūṟiyavaṟṟi amutamati 
mēṟkoḷḷātu maṟuttal.
107.	eṉṟalu mivaṟṟi ṉāleṉ ṉiṟivaḷi yavaṉka ṇārvam
	ceṉṟatu ciṟantu muṉṉē tiruvoṭu tiṟalun tēcum
	oṉṟiya aḻakuṅ* kalvi yoḷiyami kulattō ṭellām
	niṉṟucey payaṉu nallār neñcamum peṟuta laṉṟō.
uri:- eṉṟalum - eṉṟu kuṇavati kūṟiṉatum, iṟivaḷi - iṟiporuntiya vaḷiyuṭiyāy. ivaṟṟiṉāl eṉ - nī kūṟiya ikkuṟipāṭukaḷāl varum kēṭu eṉṉi, avaṉkaṇ ārvam ciṟantu muṉṉē ceṉṟatu - avvaṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl eṉ neñcam kāmakkātal mikuntu muṉṉamē ceṉṟoṭuṅkiviṭṭatu. tiruvoṭutiṟalum - celvattōṭu kūṭiya valiyum, tēcum - pukaḻum, oṉṟiya aḻakum - uṭalōṭu oṉṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟum vaṉappum, kalvi - kalviyaṟivum, oḷiyami kulattōṭu - pukaḻ niṟinta kuṭippiṟappōṭu, ellām - kūṟappaṭāta piṟavumellām, niṉṟu - eñcāmal niṉṟu, ceypayaṉum - oruvarkkuviḷikkak kūṭiya payaṉtāṉum, nallār neñcamum - aḻakiya makaḷiriṉ maṉamum ataṉ vāyilāka eytum avaratu pōkamum, peṟutal - peṟuvatām , eṉṟu colli mēlum kūṟukiṉṟāḷ e-ṟu.
kuṇavati kūṟiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷik kēṭṭa amutamati, avaḷi maṟukkumāṟṟāl, kuṇameṉak karutappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟik kūṟaluṟṟu, tiruvum, tiṟalum, tēcum, aḻakum, kalviyum, oḷiyum, kulaṉum, piṟavumellām oṉṟi niṉṟu oruvarkkuc ceyyum payaṉ aḻakiya makaḷir neñcum avar tarum pōkamum peṟutalēyām; peṟāvaḻi aviyum kuṟṟamām; kuṟṟameṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa tāmum appayaṉi eytuvikkumāyiṉ, kuṇamām eṉpatu kuṟippāl urittāḷāyiṟṟu. ellām eṉṟatu, kūṟappaṭāta kuṇamellām eñcāmal taḻuvi niṉṟatu. payaṉum - eṉpuḻi ummi ciṟappu; neñcamum - eṉpuḻi ummi, eccap poruṭṭu; piṟa eṇṇupporuḷil vantaṉa. aṉṟō - aci.
108.	kāriyam muṭinta piṉṉuṅ kāraṇa muṭivu kāṇal
	kāriya maṉṟi teṉṟē karutiṭu kaṭavuṭ kāmaṉ
	yāruḻi yaruḷic ceyyu mavaṉamak kiya ṉāka*
	nēriḻi niṉintu pōki nīṭali muṭiyi teṉṟāḷ.
uri:- nēriḻi - nēriya iḻiyiṉiyuṭiya tōḻi; kāriyam muṭinta piṉṉum - oruvaṉ eṭutta kāriyam muṭinta piṉṉarum, kāraṇa muṭivu kāṇal - akkāriyam toṭaṅku muṉ kāṇavēṇṭiya kāraṇamum payaṉum ārāytal, kāriya maṉṟu eṉṟu - ceyyattaku vataṉṟu eṉṟum, itu eṉṟum - ituvum atupōlvatē yeṉṟum, karutiṭu - uṇarvāyāka, kaṭavuḷ kāmaṉ - kaṭavuḷākiya kāmatēvaṉ, yāruḻi aruḷic ceyyum - evaṉpāl taṉ aruḷic ceykiṉṟāṉō, avaṉ namakku aiyaṉ - avaṉē namakkuk kāmak kaḻivaṉāvāṉ, āka - ātalāl, niṉintu pōki - itaṉi niṉittuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu, nīṭali - nīṭṭiyātu, itu muṭi - itaṉi muṭittuk koṇṭu varuka, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu amutamati coṉṉāḷ e- ṟu.
oruvaṉ oru kāriyattic ceyya vēṇṭiṉ, toṭakkattē akkāriyattukkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟāl atu muṭivatiyum, ataṉālām payaṉiyum cīrtūkkik kāṇṭal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, muṭintapiṉ kāṇṭalāl oru payaṉum viḷiyāteṉṟum poruḷ nūl kūṟutaliṉ, “kāriya muṭinta piṉṉum kāraṇa muṭivu kāṇal, kāriya maṉṟu”  eṉṟāḷ. eṉṟu eṉpataṉi aṉṟeṉpataṉōṭum kūṭṭuka. itu veṉpuḻi eccavummiyi virittu aṉṉatē eṉa oru col varuvittu muṭikka. iṉi, tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭirukkum ceyaṟku ōr amiti kūṟuvāḷāy, makaḷir āṭavarpāl kāmaviṉpan tuyttalē poruḷākavuṭiya reṉṟum, akkāmaviṉpatti niyamikkum kāmakkaṭavuḷ yāvarpāl ataṉik kūṭṭukiṉṟāṉō, avarpāl ammakaḷir ataṉip peṟaṟpālar eṉṟum colvāḷ, ‘kaṭavuṭ kāmaṉ yāruḻi aruḷic ceyyum avaṉ namakku aiyaṉ’ eṉṟāḷ. muṉpu vēṟu niṉivoṭu ceṉṟamiyiṉ muṭittili; ippōtu ikkarutti niṉintu ceṉṟu muṭikka veṉpāḷ, “niṉintu pōki” eṉṟum , virintu ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku, “nīṭali” yeṉṟum, muṭittukkoṇṭaṉṟi vaṟitu mīḷalākā teṉpāḷ, “muṭi” virivu tōṉṟa “yitu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
kuṇavati maṉam naṭuṅki niṟṟal
109.	tēvinī kamali yāvāy tiruvuḷat taruḷap paṭṭāṉ
	āvicel kiṉṟa vennō yarunavi ñamali yākum
	pūviṉvār kaṇiya ṉeṉṉē puṇarttavā ṟitaṉi yeṉṉā
	nāviṉā luḷintu kūṟi naṭuṅkupu voṭuṅki niṉṟāḷ.
uri:- tēvi - aracamātēvi, nī kamaliyāvāy - nī tāmari mēlirukkum tirumakaḷi yoppāy, tiruvuḷattu - niṉ aḻakiya maṉattāl, aruḷappaṭṭāṉ - virumpappaṭṭavaṉō, āvi celkiṉṟa - uyir pōtaṟkētuvākiya, vennōy arunavi - koṭiya kuṭṭa nōyāl uṟṟa tāṅkamuṭiyāta tuṉpattiyuṭiya, ñamali yākum - nāyiṉi yoppāṉ, pūviṉvār kaṇiyaṉ - pūvākiya nīṇṭa ampiṉiyuṭiya kāmakkaṭavuḷ, itaṉip puṇarttavāṟu - niṉṉi avaṉukku niyamitta vakitāṉ. eṉṉē - eṉṉeṉpatu, eṉṟu naṭuṅkupu - eṉṟu maṉaṅkalaṅki, nāviṉāl uḷintu kūṟi - nāvāl varuntic colli, oṭuṅki niṉṟāḷ - uṭal curuṅki niṉṟoḻintāḷ e-ṟu.
kamali, kamalatti irukkiyākavuṭiyavaḷ; kamalam - tāmari. puṇṇiya muṭiyāri yaruḷuvatē tirumakaḷiṉ uḷappaṇpu eṉṟaṟkut “tiruvuḷam” eṉṟum, niṉ uḷattāl virumpap paṭṭavaṉ aṉṉaṉākātu perum pāvattiṉ payaṉāka veytum kuṭṭa nōyum koṭuntuṉpamum uṭiyāṉeṉpāḷ, “aruḷap paṭṭāṉ āvi celkiṉṟa vennōy arunavi ñamaliyākum” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. aruvaruppu mikuti tōṉṟaṟku, “āvi celkiṉṟa vennōy” eṉṟāḷ. “pūviṉvār kaṇiyaṉ itaṉip puṇartta vāṟu eṉṉē” eṉṟatu, tirumakaḷi oru nāyiṭattē kāmaviṉpan tuykkumāṟu niyamikkum kāmakkaṭavuḷi ikaḻnta vāṟām. ivvāṟu kāmak kaṭavuḷi ikaḻntum, tēviyiṉ ceykiyi veṟuttum, tāṉ avaḷ ceykikkut tuṇiyāka nērntataṟku varuntiyum veṟupput tōṉṟum coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, “nāviṉāluḷintu kūṟi” yeṉṟum, piṉviḷivu niṉintu añci uḷaṅkulivatu tōṉṟa, “naṭuṅkupu voṭuṅki niṉṟāḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
kuṇavati aṭṭapaṅkaṉiṭam cellutal
110.	āṭavaraṉṟi mēlā raruvarut* taṇaṅka ṉāruṅ†
	kūṭalar tuṟantār‡ nōṉmik kuṇampurin tuyartaṟ kākap
	pīṭuṭi yayaṉār tanta perumaka ḷivaḷeṉ ṟeṇṇit§
	tōṭalar kuḻali tōḻi tuṇintaṉaḷ peyarttuc ceṉṟāḷ.
uri:- aṇaṅkaṉārum - tirumakaḷi yotta makaḷir tāmum, āṭavaraṉṟi - tām virumpum āṇmakkaḷi yaṉṟi, mēlār - maṇantu kūṭutaṟkamintavar mikka mēlōrāyiṉum, aruvaruttu - avari veṟuttu, kūṭalar - kūṭuvatoḻittu, nōṉmik kuṇam purintu - tavamum viratamum mēṟkoṇṭu, uyartaṟkāka - tēvarāyt tām viḻinta pōkattit tuyppatu kuṟittu, tuṟantaṉar - tuṟavu pūṇṭaṉar, pīṭuṭi ayaṉār - perumi poruntiya piramaṉāl. tanta - paṭikkappaṭṭa, perumakaḷ ivaḷ - perumiyiṉiyuṭiyavaḷ ivvamutamati, ātaliṉ ivaḷ tuṟattaliṉ iṟattalē tavaṟātu ceyvaḷ, eṉṟu eṇṇi - eṉṟu  taṉakkuḷḷē ārāyntu, tuṇintaṉaḷ - aṭṭapaṅkaṉ pāṟceṉṟu tēvi virumpiya kāriyatti muṭittuttara maṉam tuṇintu, peyarttu - maṟupaṭiyum, tōṭu alar kuḻali tōḻi - pūkkaḷi yaṇinta kūntaliyuṭiya amutamatikkut tōḻiyākiya kuṇavati, ceṉṟāḷ - aṭṭapaṅkaṉ irukku miṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟāḷ e-ṟu.
āṭavar, meyvaṉmiyum iḷimic cevviyum uṭiya āṇ makkaḷ, ummi - ciṟappu. ettuṇi mēlōrāyiṉum tam maṉam cellāvaḻi makaḷir aruvaruppareṉpatu ulakaṟinta ceyalātaliṉ, “aruvaruttu aṇaṅkaṉārum kūṭalar” eṉṟum, avar karuttaṟiyātu ammēlōrip puṇarttavaḻi ammakaḷir ceyyum ceyki kūṟuvāḷ, “tuṟantār nōṉmikkuṇam purintuyartaṟkāka” eṉṟum, aḻakum tiruvum iḷamiyum aṭiya mikkiruttaliṉ, piramaṉ ciṟappāka muyaṉṟu paṭitta perumiyuṭiyaḷ ivvamutamati yeṉṟaṟku, “pīṭuṭiyayaṉār tanta perumakaḷ” eṉṟum, kaṇavaṉōṭu kūṭiyiruttaliṉ, tuṟavu pūṇpati viṭuttu iṟattaliyē ceyvaḷ eṉpāḷ, “perumakaḷ ivaḷ” eṉṟum kuṇavati eṇṇiṉāḷāyiṟṟu. “iṟappāḷ” eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilaḷ, neñciṉuḷ ataṉit teḷiya vuṇarntu koṇṭa miyiṉ. kuṇavatiyum amutamatiyiṉ karuttukku nērnta uṭam paṭṭamiyiṉ, “tōṭalar kuḻali tōḻi” eṉṟum, tuṇiyātiruntavaḷ, ippōtu aṭṭapaṅkaṉ pāl celvatu takkatu eṉṟu tuṇintāḷeṉpār, “tuṇintaṉaḷ” eṉṟum, iṭiyaṟaviṉṟi uṭaṉē cellutal tōṉṟa, “tuṇintaṉaḷ ceṉṟāḷ” eṉṟum, muṉṉorukāṟ ceṉṟiruttaliṉ, “peyarttu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. nōṉmi, tavam. kuṇam, viratam. tavamuṭiyārkku viratam paṇ pātaliṉ, ataṉik “kuṇam” eṉṟār. cīvakaṉi maṇakkak karutiyirunta kuṇamālikku vēṟṟu varivu pēcappaṭṭapōtu, “kāḷikku entiyum yāyum nērāviṭiṉ iṟattaloṉṟō, cintaṉi piṟi toṉṟākic ceytavam muyaṟaloṉṟō”1 eṉak kūṟutal kāṇka.
camaṇ camayattavar, makaḷirkku vīṭupēṟu illi eṉpa vākaliṉ,  uyartal eṉpataṟkut tēvarāy iṉpuṟutal eṉṟu uri kūṟappaṭṭatu. “viratacī  latta rākit tāṉameyt tavarkkuc ceytu, arukaṉa caraṇa mūḻki yāṉṟavarc ciṟappuc ceytu, karutu naṟ kaṇavaṟ pēṇuṅ kaṟpuṭi makaḷi rinta, uruvatti nīṅkik kaṟpat tuttama tēvarāvār” (739) eṉṟum, “mātavan tāṅki viyattiyarāy vantu tōṉṟi, ētamoṉṟiṉṟi vīṭu meytuvar” (740) eṉṟum mērumantara purāṇam kūṟutal kāṇka. tēvar peṟum iṉpatti,
“āṭātu moḷitikaḻu māraṇaṅku tirumēṉi
 vāṭāta kaṇṇiyiṉar maḻuṅkāta pūntukilar
 ēṭārnta toṅkalarāy iṉpanīrp peruveḷḷam
 nīṭārak kuḷittāṭum nilimiyarē yavarellām” (tuṟavu 211)
eṉat tōlāmoḻit tēvar kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
kuṇavati aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl tēviyiṉ 
viḻivit terivittal
*111. muṭippaṭu nāṟṟa mēṉi muḻutaḻuk kāṭi pōrttuk
	 kaṭippaṭu tukaḷu maṇṇuṅ kañaliya kūṭat tāṅkōr
	 puṭikkiṭan tuṟaṅku vāṉip puḻuṅkiya maṉattō ṭaṇmi
	 viṭipparun tāṉi vēntaṉ ṟēviyiṉ viḻivu coṉṉāḷ.
uri:- muṭippaṭu nāṟṟam mēṉi -  muṭināṟṟam nāṟukiṉṟa vuṭampu, muḻutu - muḻutum, aḻukkāṭi pōrttu - aḻukkēṟiya āṭiyāl pōrttukkoṇṭu, kaṭip paṭutukaḷum - tūymiyaṟṟatākak karutappaṭum kūḷamum, maṇṇum - maṇpoṭiyum, kañaliya kūṭattu - niṟinta yāṉik kūṭattil, ōr puṭi - oru puṟattē, kiṭantu uṟaṅkuvāṉi - paṭuttuṟaṅkiya aṭṭapaṅkaṉi, puḻuṅkiya maṉattōṭu aṇmi - aruvarutta maṉattuṭaṉ arukē ceṉṟu, viṭippu arun tāṉi vēntaṉ tēviyiṉ viḻivu - vēṟupaṭuttaṟkariya tāṉi yiṉiyuṭiya vēntaṉāṉa yacōtaraṉukku maṉiviyākiya amutamatiyiṉ viruppatti, coṉṉāḷ - cilavāya coṟkaḷāl kuṇavati mellac coṉṉāḷ e-ṟu.
mēṉi muḻutum puṇṇuṟṟut tīnāṟṟam nāṟak kiṭattaliṉ, “muṭippaṭu nāṟṟa mēṉi muḻutum” eṉṟār. “meyyiṟ puṇkaḷum kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā” (106) eṉṟu kuṇavatiyum, “puṇ peṟṟa meyyaṉ” (113) eṉṟu avaṉēyum kūṟutal kāṇka. muṭippaṭu nāṟṟam, muṭiyuṇṭākum nāṟṟam. cīvaṭitaliṉ ī mutaliyaṉa moykkā vaṇṇam pōrttuk kiṭantāṉ eṉpār, “mēṉi muḻutum aḻukkāṭi pōrttu” eṉṟār. yāṉi tiṉṟu kaḻinta puṟṟukaḷum - vittukaḷum ataṉ ciṟunīrāl naṉintu ataṉ malam aḷintu pularntu kiṭattaliṉ, “kaṭippaṭutuka” ḷeṉṟār. kāṇṭaṟkut tuṇintu pōntāḷāyiṉum, avaṉatu mēṉiyum, āṭip pōrviyum iṭattiṉ iḻivum kāṇakkāṇa avaṭku aruvaruppu neñci leḻuntamiyiṉ, “puḻuṅkiya maṉattōṭu” aṇukiṉāḷ. pakivarāl vēṟupaṭuttaṟkariya tāṉiyātaliṉ, viṭipparun tāṉiyāyiṟṟu;  “viṭipparun tāṉi vēntaṉ vēṇṭu veṟuppa nalki”1 eṉṟu tēvarum kūṟutal kāṇka. cila vāya coṟkaḷāl tam karutti mellac collutal iḷiya makaḷirkku iyalpātaliṉ, ataṉi eṭuttō tātu “viḻivu coṉṉāḷ” eṉṟoḻintār; aṉṟi, eṭuttōtaṟku nāṇiyatu tōṉṟa uyttuṇara vittāreṉiṉumām. vēṟupaṭut taṟkariya tāṉi koṇṭu nāṭṭukku vēṇṭum kāvali naṉku ceyyum vēntaṉāyiṉum, avaṉ tēvikku akkāval payaṉpaṭā tāyiṟṟeṉpār, “viṭipparuntāṉi vēntaṉ tēvi ” eṉṟār.
aṭṭapaṅkaṉ uṭaṉpaṭātu vekuḷutal
112.	kēṭṭalu mañcu neñcaṉ kēṭeṉak keytiṟ ṟeṉṟē
	vāṭṭamum naṭukku muṟṟē māṇpila moḻintāy maṉṉaṉ
	vīṭṭiṭuñ celkeṉ ṟēva vēypuri tōḷi tōḻi
	kāṭṭuva kāṭṭi yaṉṉāṉ karuttiyuṅ kalittiṭ ṭāḷē.
uri:- kēṭṭalum - kuṇavati colliyatu kēṭṭamāttiriyē, añcum  neñcaṉ - añcukiṉṟa maṉamuṭiyavaṉāṉa aṭṭapaṅkaṉ, eṉakkuk kēṭu eytiṟṟu eṉṟu - eṉakku nācakālam vantatu eṉṟu niṉittu, vāṭṭamum naṭukkam uṟṟu - maṉavāṭṭamum naṭukkamu maṭintu, māṇpu ila moḻintāy - tēviyiṉ perumikkut takātavaṟṟic colkiṉṟāy, maṉṉaṉ vīṭṭiṭum - itaṉi yaṟintāl vēntaṉ koṉṟuviṭuvāṉ, celka - nī ippōtē ivviṭat tiṉiṉṟum pōyviṭuka, eṉṟu ēva - eṉṟu ciṉantu kūṟa, vēypuri tōḷi tōḻi - mūṅkilpōlum tōḷkaḷiyuṭiya amutamatiyiṉ tōḻiyākiya kuṇavati, kāṭṭuvakāṭṭi - avaṉ aiyuṟātu ēṟkattakka cāṉṟukaḷi eṭuttukkūṟikkāṭṭi, aṉṉāṉ karuttiyum kalit tiṭṭāḷ - avaṉuṭiya maṉakkaruttik kalittut tēviyiṉ viruppattukku icivittuk koṇṭāḷ e-ṟu.
iyalpākavē accamuṭiyaṉātaliṉ, kuṇavati kūṟiyataṉāl atu mikuntu varuttuvatupaṟṟi “añcum neñcaṉ,” eṉṟār; “accamē kīḻkaḷatu ācāram”1 eṉpataṉāl, accam aṭṭapaṅkaṟku iyal pātalaṟika. aracaṉ kōyiliṟ paṇipurivōr aṭaṅkiya maṉamila rāyiṉ, kēṭeytal oru taliyātaliṉ, “kēṭeṉak keytumeṉṟu vāṭṭamum naṭukkamum” koṇṭāṉ; “maṉṉar kōyil uṟivār poṟi ceṟitta māṇpiṉarē”2 eṉṟu tirutakkatēvar kūṟutal kāṇka. tēvikkut tōḻiyāvāḷ avaḷ māṇpiṟku iḻukkut taruvataṉic colvatu tī tātaliṉ, “māṇpila moḻintāy” eṉṟum, avaḷi accuṟuttip pōkkum karuttāl, “maṉṉaṉ vīṭṭiṭum celka” eṉṟum, eṉṟataṉōṭami yātu, avaṉ collakkēṭṭum kuṇavati puṭipeyarāmiyiṉ, avaṉē avaḷip pōkkak karutiṉāṉeṉpār “ēva” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. vēypuri tōḷi tōḻiyeṉṟatu, tāṉ karutiyatu muṭittuk koṇṭallatu ēkāta iyalpiṉaḷātali yuṇarttiṟṟu. ivvāṟu avaṉ maṉantiṟampāṉātalik kaṇṭa kuṇavati avaṉ ēṟkattakka coṟkaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṟkuc cāṉṟākak kāṭṭat takuvaṉavum vaḻaṅki, avaṉi matiyuṭampaṭuttāḷ eṉpār, “kāṭṭuva kāṭṭi yaṉṉāṉ karuttiyum kalittiṭṭāḷē” eṉṟār. pētiyātaliṉ, piṟaṉil viḻiyum it tīviṉikku avaṉum icintāṉeṉa vaṟika.
aṭṭapaṅkaṉ maṉaṅkalintu makiḻnturittal
113.	paṇpeṟṟa moḻiyāy yāṉum paṇpeṟṟa payaṉum peṟṟēṉ
	puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ pollāp puḻutiyiṟ ṟuḷiyuṅ kiyaṉ
	maṇpeṟṟa iṟivaṉ tēvi maṉampeṟṟu makiḻvē ṉeṉṉiṉ
	eṇpeṟṟa tavamiyār peṟṟār yāṉpeṟṟa pēṟi teṉṟāṉ.
uri:- paṇ peṟṟa moḻiyāy - paṇṇicipōlum coṟkaḷiyuṭiyavaḷē, paṇpeṟṟa payaṉ - paṇpāṭum aṟivu peṟṟataṉālaṭiyum payaṉi, yāṉum - peṟaṟkākāta yāṉum, peṟṟēṉ - iṉṟu peṟṟēṉ, (evvāṟeṉil) puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ - puṇkaḷ niṟinta uṭampiṉiyuṭiyēṉ, pollāp puḻutiyil - nallatallāta puḻutiyilē, tuḷiyum kiyaṉ - puraṇṭukiṭakkum ciṟumiyumuṭiyēṉ, maṇpeṟṟa iṟivaṉ tēvi - maṇṇula kattiyāḷum aracaṉukku maṉiviyiṉuṭiya, maṉam peṟṟu makiḻ vēṉ - kātalaṉpu peṟṟu makiḻkiṉṟēṉātalāl, eṇpeṟṟa tavam - yāvarum eṇṇi virumpattakka tavappayaṉi, eṉṉiṉ - eṉṉip pōla, yār peṟṟār - yāvar peṟṟār, itu yāṉ peṟṟa pēṟu - itu yāṉ aritākap peṟṟa pēṟākum, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu aṭṭapaṅkaṉ kuṇavatikkuk kūṟiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
ummi - ciṟappu. “puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ”, “puḻutiyiṟ ṟuyiyuṅ kiyaṉ” eṉat taṉ nilimiyi yuṇarntiruttaliṉ, “yāṉum peṟṟēṉ” eṉṟum, toṭakkattē paṇpilavāyt tōṉṟiya moḻikaḷ iṟutiyil iṉpap payaṉ tantamiyiṉ, “paṇpeṟṟa moḻiyāy” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. taṉ nilimiyi nōkka aracamā tēviyiṉ iṉpappēṟu taṉakku vāyttataṟkut taṉ tavamēyaṉṟip piṟitoru kāraṇamum kāṇamāṭṭāmiyiṉ, “eṉṉiṉ eṇ peṟṟa tavamiyār peṟṟār” eṉṟāṉ. aritiṟ peṟṟa pēṟu eṉa viyanturittaliṉ, “yāṉ peṟṟa pēṟitu” eṉa vitantōtappaṭṭatu.
kuṇavati pōntu araciyik kāṇṭal
114.	ivvaki moḻivōṉ ṟaṉṉi yiṟiyavaṉ tēvi mēvum
	cevviyuṅ kuṟiyuñ ceppic ceṉṟava ḷeyta lōṭum
	kavviya kāmat tīyāṟ kayaṅkiya māli yoppāḷ
	navvinēr viḻiyāy naṉṟō navilkaniṉ karuma meṉṟāḷ.
uri:- ivvaki moḻivōṉ taṉṉi - ivvāṟu colli makiḻum aṭṭapaṅkaṟku, iṟiyavaṉ tēvi - aracaṉ maṉiviyākiya amutamati, mēvum cevviyum kuṟiyum ceppi - varakkūṭiya kālamum iṭamum urittuviṭṭu, ceṉṟavaḷ - tēvipāṟ ceṉṟa kuṇavati, eytalōṭum - amutamatiyi nōkki yaṇukavarutalum, kavviya kāmattīyāl - paṟṟi varuttum kāmattīyiṉāl, kayaṅkiya māli oppāḷ - cāmpiya pūmāliyi yoppavaḷāṉa amirtamati; navvinēr viḻiyāy - māṉpōlum kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyāy, niṉkarumam - nī mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa kāriyam, naṉṟō - nalantantatō, navilka - colluka, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ e-ṟu.
cevvi- kālam. kuṟi - kuṟikkum iṭam. “ceppi” yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṟṟi yavaṉ viṉaviṉami peṟṟām. yāṉikkūṭattiṉ nīṅki amirtamatiyirunta antappuratti yaṭintāḷeṉṟaṟku, “ceṉṟavaḷ eytalōṭum” eṉṟum, kuṇavati mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa kāriyam muṭiyumō muṭiyātō eṉṟa aiyam oru puṭiyalippa, avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟa kāmavēṭki orupuṭiyalippa uṇarvum uṭampum vāṭiyiruttaliṉ, “kavviya kāmattīyāl kayaṅkiya māli yoppāḷ” eṉṟār. tītō eṉṉāmi, ataṉiyavaḷ virumpāmiyālām.
kuṇavati nikaḻntatu kūṟakkēṭṭu makiḻtal
115.	paṭṭatu nalaṅkā ṇeṉpāḷ pākaṉik kaṇṭa vāṟum
	aṭṭamā paṅkaṉ cīṟi yaḻaṉṟiṭṭa vāṟun tēvi
	iṭṭattiṟ kavaṉi yāṅkē yiyivitta vāṟuṅ kūṭaṟ
	koṭṭiya kuṟiyuñ coṉṉā ḷoḷḷiḻi yuvappuṟ ṟāḷē.
uri:- nalampaṭṭatu eṉpāḷ - nalamākavē muṭintatu eṉṟu collit toṭaṅkiya tōḻi, pākaṉik kaṇṭa vāṟum - tāṉ ceṉṟu avyāṉippākaṉik kaṇṭatum, aṭṭapaṅkaṉ cīṟi aḻaṉṟiṭṭavāṟum - aṭṭapaṅkaṉeṉṉum peyariṉaṉākiya avaṉ cīṟic ciṉantatum, tēvi  iṭṭattiṟku - aracamātēviyākiya amir tamatiyiṉ viruppattukku,  avaṉi iyivittavāṟum - avaṉi yuṭaṉpaṭuvittatum, kūṭaṟku oṭṭiya kuṟiyum - avaṉic ceṉṟu kūṭutaṟku niyamitta kālamum iṭamum, coṉṉāḷ - kuṇavati kūṟiṉāḷ, oḷḷiḻi - oḷḷiya iḻiyaṇinta amutamati, uvap puṟṟāḷ - makiḻcci mikuntāḷ e-ṟu.
“naṉṟō navilka niṉkarumam” eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṭku nīṭṭiyātu viṭiyiṟuppāḷ, “paṭṭatu nalam” eṉṟāḷ; kāṇ-acinili. mā-aci. aḻalutal-vekuḷutal. iṭṭam-vaṭacoṟṟiripu. āṅku eṉpatu uriyaci. kuṟiyiṉit tāṉē kuṟittāḷātaliṉ, aṭṭa paṅkaṟkēyaṉṟi amirtamatikkum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. uṟṟāḷ- uṟu eṉṉum uriccollaṭiyākap piṟanta muṟṟuviṉi.
amirtamati aracaṉpāl muṉivu koṇṭoḻukal
116.	taṉivayi ṉikuḷi yāṉi tarappaṭu cāra ṉōṭu
	kaṉipuri kiḷavi kāmaṅ kalantaṉaḷ kaṉintu ceṉṟāḷ
	muṉiviṉi maṉṉaṉ ṟaṉmēṉ muṟukiṉa ḷoḻuku muṉpōl
	iṉiyava ḷalla ḷeṉko leṉamaṉat teṇṇi ṉāṉē.
uri:- kaṉipuri kiḷavi - kaṉipōlum coṟkaḷi yuṭiya amutamati, ikuḷi - tōḻiyākiya kuṇavatipāl, tarappaṭu yāṉic cāraṉōṭu - kūṭṭappaṭṭa yāṉippākaṉākiya aṭṭa paṅkaṉuṭaṉ, taṉivayiṉ - taṉiyāṉa ōriṭattē, kāmaṅkaṉintu - kāmavēṭki ciṟantu, kalantaṉaḷ ceṉṟāḷ - puṇarntuṭaṉoḻuki varupavaḷ, maṉṉaṉ taṉmēl - maṉṉaṉākiya yacōtaraṉpāl, muṉiviṉi muṟukiṉaḷ oḻukum - veṟuppumikak koṇṭoḻu kaluṟṟāḷāka, muṉpōl iṉiyavaḷ allaḷ eṉkol eṉa - muṉpōl ivaḷ nampāl iṉpuṟukiṉṟāḷilli itaṟkukkāraṇam yātākum eṉṟu, maṉattu eṇṇiṉāṉ - yacōtaraṉ taṉmaṉattuk kuḷḷē cintikkalāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
taṉivayiṉ, tōḻi kuṟitta kuṟiyiṭam. ikuḷitarappaṭuyāṉic cāraṉōṭu taṉivayiṉ kāmaṅ kaṉintu kalantaṉaḷ ceṉṟāḷ eṉa iyittukkoḷka. kāmaṅkalantaṉaḷ kaṉintu ceṉṟāḷ eṉṟē koṇṭu kāmappuṇarcci peṟṟu ataṉkaṭkaṉṟi yoḻukupavaḷ eṉṟurippiṉumām. cāraṉpāl maṉatteḻunta aṉpu ceṉṟamiyiṉ, aracaṉpāl veṟuppu mikuvāḷāyiṉāḷeṉa vaṟika. ammikuti aracaṉuḷattē aiyatti yeḻuppiṉamiyiṉ, “muṉpōlaṉpila ḷeṉkol” eṉṟāṉ; itaṉip piṟar  pāl kūṟi yārāyātu taṉmaṉattakattē yeṇṇi ārāyat toṭaṅkiṉā ṉeṉpār, “maṉatteṇṇiṉāṉ” eṉṟār.
maṉṉaṉ poyyuṟakkam koṇṭu amutamatiyiṉ kaḷavoḻukkiṉik kāṇṭal
117.	aracavi viṭuttu meyyā laṟuciṉa ṉoppa maṉṉaṉ
	uriyala ṉamaḷi taṉmē luṟaṅkutal purinta pōḻtiṉ
	virikamaḻ kuḻali mēvi meyttuyi leṉṟu kāmat
	tuṟiyiṉaḷ peyarntu tōḻi kuṟiyiṭan tuṉṉi ṉāḷē.
uri:- avi aracu viṭuttu - aracavikkaṇ vantirunta aracarkaḷit tattam irukkikkuc cellaviṭuttu, meyyāl  aṟuciṉaṉ oppa - puṟattē maṉattuṭ ciṟitum ciṉam illāṉ pōlak kāṭṭi, uriyalaṉ - yāvarōṭum ētum collāṭaliṉṟi, amaḷi taṉmēl - taṉṉuṭiya paṭukkiyiṉmēl kiṭantu, maṉṉaṉ uṟaṅkutal purinta pōḻtu - maṉṉaṉākiya yacōtaraṉ poyyuṟakkaṅ koṇṭiruk kiyil, virikamaḻ kuḻali - maṇam kamaḻum kūntali yuṭiya amirtamati, mēvi - avaṉarukē pōntu, meyttuyil eṉṟu - meyyākavē uṟaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu karutik koṇṭu, kāmat tuṟiyiṉaḷ - kāmaviṉ pantuykkum kaḷavu neṟiyiṉaḷāy, peyarntu - paḷḷiyaṟiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, tōḻi kuṟiiṭam tuṉṉiṉāḷ - tōḻi kuṟitta kuṟiyiṭatti yaṭintāḷ e-ṟu.
amirtamatiyiṉ oḻukalāṟṟiṟ piṟanta aiyam nāḷukkunāḷ mikuntu yacōtaraṉ neñci yalittamiyiṉ, aracavikkaṇ iruntu ceyyum muṟimikkaṇ karuttuc cellāmiyiṉ, “aracavi viṭuttu” eṉṟum, viṭṭāṉāyiṉum, piṟar ayirāmip poruṭṭu vekuḷiyip puṟattē kāṭṭātoḻukiṉa ṉeṉṟaṟku, “meyyālaṟu ciṉaṉoppa”  eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. aṟuciṉaṉ eṉpatu, “aruṅkēṭaṉ”1 eṉṟāṟ pōlvatu. poyyuṟakkaṅ koṇṭā ṉeṉpār. uṟaṅkiṉā ṉeṉṉātu, “uṟaṅkutal purinta pōḻtu” eṉṟār; “purinta” eṉṟataṉāl, kāṇpavar meyyākavē uṟaṅku kiṉṟāṉeṉa uṇarat tōṟṟiṉā ṉeṉpatu peṟṟām. maṇaṅkamaḻum kuḻaliyātaliṉ, amirtamati vantu tāṉ uṟaṅkutalik kaṇṭeḻu vatik kaṇṇālaṉṟi maṉattā laṟintukoṇṭami tōṉṟa, “virikamaḻ kuḻali” yeṉṟār. kaḷavuk kāmat tuṟiyil oḻukukiṉ ṟāḷātaliṉ, uṭaṉē avviṭat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅkuvatu tōṉṟa, “peyarntu” eṉṟum, “kuṟiyiṭam tuṉṉiṉā” ḷeṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
yacōtaraṉ avaḷip piṉtoṭarntu ceṉṟu
akkuṟiyiṭattē maṟintu niṟṟal
118.	tuyiliṉi yoruvi maṉṉaṉ cuṭarkkatir vāḷki yēnti
	mayaluḻi* vaḻiccel kiṉṟa vāḷari yēṟu pōlak
	kayalviḻi yavaḷtaṉ piṉṉē karantaṉa ṉotuṅki yāṅkaṇ
	ceyaliṉi yaṟitu meṉṟu ceṟintaṉaṉ maṟintu niṉṟāṉ.
uri:- maṉṉaṉ - amirtamati cellakkaṇṭa yacōtaraṉ, tuyiliṉi yoruvi - tāṉ koṇṭirunta poyyuṟakkatti viṭuttu, cuṭarkkatirvāḷ - cuṭarukiṉṟa oḷikkatirkaḷi vīcum vāṭpaṭiyi, kiyēnti - kiyil ēntik koṇṭu, mayal uḻivaḻi - mayaṅkiyōṭu kiṉṟa māṉoṉṟip piṉpaṟṟi, celkiṉṟa vāḷ ariēṟu pōla - cellumoru ciṅkavēṟu pōla, kayalviḻiyavaḷtaṉ piṉṉē - kayalmīṉ pōlum kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya amirtamatiyiṉ piṉṉē, karantaṉaṉ otuṅki - maṟintu ceṉṟu, āṅkaṇ - akkuṟiyiṭattē, ceyaliṉiyaṟitum eṉṟu - nikaḻum nikaḻcciyikkāṇpām eṉṟu niṉittukkoṇṭu, ceṟintaṉaṉ maṟintu niṉṟāṉ - yārum aṟiyāvakiyil oṭuṅki maṟintu niṉṟu nōkkiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
nikaḻcciyiṉik kāṇṭal vēṇṭu meṉṟē poyyuṟakkaṅ koṇṭāṉātaliṉ, amutamati cellakkaṇṭatum ataṉi yoru viṉāṉ eṉavaṟika. uḻi-māṉ. kāmat tuṟiyiṉaḷeṉṟā rākaliṉ, ataṟkēṟpa, avaṭku mayaṅkiya māṉi uvamam kūṟiṉār. vāḷ ariyēṟupōla eṉṟu uvamiyāṟṟalāl yacōtaraṉ mikka ciṉattuṭaṉ celkiṉṟami peṟṟām. piṟar kāṇpareṉṟa accattāl aṭikkaṭi cuṟṟumuṟṟum parakka viḻittup pārttukkoṇṭē ceṉṟā ḷeṉṟaṟku, amirtamatiyik “kayal viḻiyavaḷ” eṉṟār. taṉṉiruppup piṟarkkut teriyāmal, piṟar mellap pēcuvatum tāṉ naṉku kēṭkakkūṭiyatāyuḷḷa iṭattiyaṭinta maṟin tiruntāṉātaliṉ, “ceṟintaṉaṉ” eṉṟār.
amirtamati tāḻttuvarakkaṇṭa aṭṭapaṅkaṉ 
avaḷit tuṉpuṟuttal
119.	kaṭiyaṉak kamalap pāvi karuṅkuḻal paṟṟik kiyāl
	iṭinilañ cella īrttiṭ ṭirukiyi ṉālu mōccip
	puṭipala puṭittut tāḻtta poruḷitu pukalka eṉṉāt
	tuṭiyiṭi tuvaḷa vīḻttu nilattiṭit tukittiṭ ṭāṉē.
uri:- kaṭiyaṉ - kīḻmakaṉākiya aṭṭapaṅkaṉ etir nōkki vantu, akkamalappāvi - tirumakaḷip pōlvāḷākiya avvamirta matiyiṉ, karuṅkuḻal kiyālpaṟṟi - kariya kūntalit taṉkiyāl paṟṟi, iṭinilam cella īrttiṭṭu -avviṭat tiṟkum kuṟiyiṭattiṟkum iṭikkiṭanta nilattil avaḷi iḻuttukkoṇṭu ceṉṟu, tuṭiyiṭi tuvaḷa nilattiṭi vīḻttu - uṭukkipōlum iṭiyiṉiyuṭiya avaḷi nilattil taḷḷikkiṭatti, iruki yiṉālum  ōcci - irukikaḷālum ōṅki, puṭi - valamiṭamākiya irukaṉṉattilum, pala puṭittu - palamuṟiyum aṭittu, tāḻtta poruḷitu pukalka - kālan tāḻttuvanta kāraṇattic colluka, eṉṉā - eṉṟu, tukit tiṭṭāṉ - utittu varuttiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
“kamalappāvi” yik kāṇpavaṉ, aṉpuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭṭu avaḷaruḷip peṟātu avaḷarumiyu muṇarātu varuttutal paṟṟi, aṭṭapaṅkaṉik “kaṭiyaṉ” eṉṟum, kamalappāviyāyiṉum kaṭiyaṉic cārvāḷēl, ittuṉpattiyē yaṭiyaṟpālaḷ eṉṟaṟku, “kamalappāvi” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. kuṟitta kālattu vārātu tāḻttamiyiṉ, kuṟiyiṭattiṉīṅki veḷiyē pōntu avaḷatu varuki nōkkic ciṉantiruntavaṉ, kaṇṭamāttirattē cīṟippāyntu avaḷ kūntalippaṟṟi īrttukkoṇṭu akkuṟiyiṭat tukkuc ceṉṟu, nilattiṟṟaḷḷip puṭittu utittu varuttiṉāṉeṉ patām. tuṭiyiṭi- cuṭṭumāttiriyāy niṉṟatu. itupōlum varuttam evarālum eppōtum avvamirtamatikku nikaḻnta tiṉmiyiṉ, “tuvaḷat takittiṭṭāṉē” eṉa āciriyar varuntik kūṟukiṉṟār. puṭi- kaṉṉam. poruḷ, kāraṇam.
amirtamati āṟṟātu ayartal
120.	iruḷiṉā laṭarkkap paṭṭa veḻiṉmatik kaṭavuḷ pōla
	veruḷiyāṉ mitippuṇ ṭitē* vimmiya miṭaṟṟa ḷākit
	teruḷkalā ḷuriyu māṭāḷ ciṟitupō taciyak kaṇṭa
	maruḷiyāṉ mayaṅki mātar malaraṭi ceṉṉi vittāṉ.
uri:- iruḷiṉāl aṭarkkappaṭṭa - iruṭkūṭṭattāl nerukkappaṭṭa, eḻilmatik kaṭavuḷpōla - aḻakiya muḻumatiyam pōla, tēvi - aracamātēviyākiya amirtamati, veruḷiyāl mitippuṇṭu - aṭṭapaṅkaṉ pāṟceṉṟa kaḻikātalāl neñcaṭip puṇṭu , aitu vimmiya miṭaṟṟaḷāki - mellitāka vīṅkiya kaḻuttiṉi yuṭiyaḷāy, teruḷkalāḷ - taṉ tīyoḻukkattiṉ tīmiyi yuṇarāḷāy, uriyumāṭāḷ - oru collum collātu, ciṟitu pōtu aciya - ciṟitu nēram ayarn tiruntāḷāka, kaṇṭa - pārtta, maruḷiyāṉ - maṉamaruṭkiyuṭiyaṉāṉa aṭṭapaṅkaṉ, mayaṅki - tāṉ ārāyātu varuttiyataṟku maṉam varunti, mātar malaraṭi - amirtamatiyiṉ tāmarippūp pōṉṟa pātatti, ceṉṉivittāṉ - taliyil vittu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
“iruḷiṉāl aṭarkkappaṭṭa eḻiṉmatik kaṭavuḷ pōla” eṉṟa uvamam, veruḷiyāl mītūrappaṭṭa amirtamatiyic cuṭṭi niṉṟateṉa vaṟika. veruḷi - kaḻikāmattāl piṟakkum maṉamayakkam. maṉattē avaṉkaṭ ceṉṟa kaḻikāmattāl vēṟupaṭāḷāyiṉum, avaṉtaṉ kuḻalpaṟṟi yīrttup puṭittu viḻttit tuṉpuṟuttiya tāṟpiṟanta āṟṟāmiyāl miṭaṟuvimmi acivu koṇṭāḷ eṉka. aitu-ciṟitu. āṟṟātu peruvaruttamum nōyumeytaṟ pālāḷ, veruḷi mītūrntamiyiṉ, ciṟitē vimmiṉāḷ eṉpār, “aitē vimmiya miṭaṟṟaḷāki” eṉṟum, ittuṇittuṉpameytiyum taṉ tavaṟṟiṉiyavaḷ uṇarntila ḷeṉpār, “teruḷkalāḷ” eṉṟum, taṉ varuttattip pulappaṭuttiyō, avaṉōṭu kātalaṉ pāl pulantō ētēṉu  moṉṟukūṟiṉ, avaṉ vēṟupaṭṭu nīṅkuvaṉeṉṟa accattāl vāḷāviruntāḷeṉṟaṟku, “uriyu māṭāḷ” eṉṟum, avaṉi vaṇakkum vāyilātaliṉ “ciṟitu pōtaciya” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. neṭitacintiruppiṉ, aṭṭapaṅkaṉ añci avviṭattiṉiṉṟum ōṭiviṭuvaṉātaliṉ, “ciṟitu pōtu” eṉṟār. tāṉ ceyta tuṉpattiyum, ataṉāl amirtamati āṟṟātu acivuṟṟatum niṉintu kaṇṭu, maṉam maruṇṭu, teḷinta aṟiviṉmiyiṉ avaḷ kālil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟuṇarka. vaṇaṅkak kaṇṭatum avaḷ acivu nīṅki avaṉpāl ārākkātal koṇṭāḷeṉpār. “mātar” eṉṟār. ceṉṉivittāṉ- vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ.
amirtamati tāṉ tāḻttu vantamikkuk 
kāraṇam kūṟutal
121.	tiyalāḷ mellat tēṟic cāraṉi makiḻntu nōkki
	veyyanī muṉivu cellaṉ mētiṉik kiṟivaṉ ṟaṉṉō
	ṭiyavā caṉatti ṉumpa raracavi yiruntu kaṇṭāy
	veyyapā vaṅkaḷ ceytēṉ viḷampalaṉ viḷinta teṉṟāḷ.
uri:- tiyalāḷ - puṇarcci vēṭkiyaḷākiya amirtamati, tēṟi - acivunīṅki, makiḻntu - aṭṭapaṅkaṉ ceyal kaṇṭu maṉam makiḻntu, mellanōkki - taṉ kaṇkaḷi mellat tiṟantu pārttu, aiya - aiyaṉē, veyyanī - eṉṉāl virumpappaṭṭanī, muṉivu cellal - yāṉ kālam tāḻttu vantatu kuṟittu veṟātēkoḷ, mētiṉikku iṟivaṉ taṉṉōṭu - maṇṇaracaṉāṉa yacōtaraṉuṭaṉ, aracavi - aracar vantirukkum avikkaṇ; ācaṉattiṉ umpar - aracu kaṭṭiliṉ mēl, iruntu - tiruvōlakkamiruntu, veyya pāvaṅkaḷ ceytēṉ - nī vemmi koḷḷattakka kuṟṟaṅkaḷic ceytuviṭṭēṉ, viḷintatu - nikaḻntatu ituvē, viḷampalaṉ - itaṉi muṉṉarē tōḻivāyilākat teriviyā toḻintēṉ, eṉṉip poṟuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭukiṟēṉ, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ e-ṟu.
