kāṭṭam - viṟaku. īṇṭut tīkkaṭiyum viṟakiṉmēṟṟu. viṟa koṭu viṟakik kūṭṭik kaṭintapōtu ataṉiṭit tīppiṟantāṟ pōla, uṭaliṭattē ataṉiṉ vēṟāya uyir tōṉṟum eṉpatām. ivvuyir aṉātiyeṉṉum oruvarāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭataṉṟu eṉṟum camaṇ cāṉṟōr kūṟupa. “veyyeri” eṉavē, vemmip paṇpiṉi yuṭiya tī aḵtillāta viṟakiṭattē kāṇappaṭuvatāl ataṉi viṟakiṉ kāriyameṉṉātu, tīyiṉ kāraṇapporuḷ niṟṟaṟkuc cārpā meṉpatum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu, civañāṉacitti parapakkattu nikaṇṭa vātattuṭ kāṇappaṭum “anta vāymoḻiyiṉāl” eṉṟa tiruvirutta vuriyil, āciriyar ñāṉap pirakācar, “kirahya carīra pariṇāmapramāṇāti niṉṟa pañcēntiriya maṉōvāk kāya svācamākiyavaṟṟāl jīviyā niṟpaṉa cīvaṉākum” eṉṟu urippar. vēṇṭumeṉṟa taṟkēṟpa ākamaṅ kaḷeṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu; paṭavē, iḵtu ākamattuk kūṟappaṭṭa teṉpatām.
237.	cikkeṉa vāyu vēṟṟit tittivāyc cemmit tūkkip
	pukkavav vāyu nīṅkip pōyapiṉ ṉiṟicey tālum
	okkamē yoruvaṉ caṅkō ṭorunila māḷi kikkē
	tikkeṉat toṉicey tiṭṭa tevvaḻi vanta tākum.
uri:- tittivāy - turutti yoṉṟiṉ vāyi, cemmi - naṉṟāy mūṭi, tūkki - niṟuttu nōkki, cikkeṉa vāyu ēṟṟi - piṉpu ataṉkaṇ kāṟṟi mika aṭittu, pukka avvāyu nīṅkip pōyapiṉ - aṭikkap pukunta kāṟṟu nīṅkiviṭṭa piṉṉar, niṟi ceytālum - niṟuttup pārttālum, okkum - iruvaḻi niṟi yottirukkum, orunilam - oru paranta nilattēyiruntu, oruvaṉ, caṅkoṭu - caṅkiṉāl, tikkeṉat toṉi ceytiṭṭatu - tikatikaveṉa muḻakkañ ceytatu, māḷikikku - oru peru māḷikikkuḷḷiruntē kēṭka, vantatu - vantaṭintatu, evvaḻiyākum - evvāṟu vantatām e-ṟu.
cīvaṉuṇmi  kāṇṭaṟkuc caṇṭakarumaṉ kaḷvaṉoruvaṉi vati ceyyum muṉṉum piṉṉum niṟiceytu pārttuk kāṇa māṭṭāmi kūṟiṉāṉātaliṉ, avaṉ kūṟṟiṟku maṟumoḻi kūṟutaluṟṟārāy, tittiyuvamam kūṟiṉār. titti kāṟṟaṭitta muṉṉum, aṭitta kāṟṟu nīṅkiya piṉṉum niṟiyot tiruppatu cīvaṉuṇmi kāṇṭaṟ kuc caṇṭakarumaṉ ceyta ceyali viḷakki niṉṟatu. iṉi, ivvuṭaṟ kuḷ cīvaṉ pukuntu nilavun tiṟatti viḷakkuvār caṅkiṉōci māḷikik kaṇ iruppōr ceviyiṟ kēṭkap pukun tiṟattāl uvamittār. cikkeṉa eṉṟatu, aṭikkap paṭum kāṟṟiṉmikuti kuṟitta kuṟippuk col; “cikkayātta”1 eṉpuḻippōla iṟuki mikutal mēṟṟu. cemmutal - mūṭutal. tūkkil vittu niṟuppatit tūkkutal eṉṟār. okkumē - ēkāram tēṟṟam. caṅkoṭu - oṭu āṉurupiṉ poruṭṭu. māḷikikku, urupu mayakkam. tikatikaveṉa eṉpatu tikkeṉa eṉa vantatu.
238.	ivvaki yākuñ cīva ṉiyalputā ṉiyalpu vēṟām
	veyyatī viṉika ḷālē veruvuṟu tuyariṉ mūḻki
	miyaluṟ ṟaḻuṅki* nāṉku katikaḷuṭ keḻumic celva†
	tiyamil kāṭci ñāṉat toḻukkattō raṟiva tākum.
uri:- cīvaṉ iyalputāṉ ivvakiyākum - cīvaṉatu taṉmiyum ittiṟamākum, iyalpu vēṟām - ic cīvaṉatu iyalpu vēṟā talāl uṇṭāvaṉavākiya, veyya tīviṉikaḷāl - koṭiya tīviṉik kūṭṭattāl, veruvuṟu tuyariṉ mūḻki - accan tarukiṉṟa tuṉpattil aḻunti, miyal uṟṟu aḻuṅki - maṉa mayakkattāl varunti - nāṉku katikaḷuḷ keḻumic celvatu - nālvakip piṟappukkaḷilum piṟantiṟantu piṟantuḻalvatu, aiyamil kāṭci ñāṉattu oḻukkattōr - naṟkāṭci, naṉñāṉam, nalloḻukka meṉṟa mūṉṟiyumuṭiya cāṉṟōr, aṟivatākum - teḷiyavaṟiyum ceytiyākum e-ṟu.
paranta nilaveḷiyil piṟanta caṅkōci māḷikiyiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa cuvar, mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṭantu ataṉuḷḷē iṉitu ceṉṟaṭivatu pōlac cīvaṉum ñāṉāvaraṇāti kaṉmat taṭikaḷit kaṭantu vīṭṭiṉi yiṉitaṭiyum eṉṟaṟku, “ivvakiyākum cīvaṉ iyalputāṉ” eṉṟār. ivvāṟu cellum cīvaṉkaḷic cupāva ñāṉak kāṭcikaḷip peṟṟa cīvaṉkaḷ eṉpa. ivviyalpu vēṟupaṭṭa vaḻi, ulaka vāḻviṟ ceṉṟu uḻalumāṟṟāl piṟap piṟappukkaṭ kētuvākiya viṉikaḷic ceytu kōṭaliṉ, “iyalpu vēṟām veyyatī viṉikaḷālē veruvuṟu tuyariṉ mūḻki” eṉṟār. vēṟām eṉpuḻi, kāraṇap poruṭṭāya peyareñcu kiḷavi īṟṟu mici yukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu niṉṟatu. intac cīvaṉkaḷic camcāracīvaṉ eṉpa. ulakavāḻvu, vīṭṭu vāḻviṉ māṟupaṭṭatākaliṉ, māṟṟeṉap paṭum. “māṟṟiṉiṉṟatu viyaka mūṉṟiṉum, āṟṟavum pariyaṭṭamō rintiṉāl. tōṟṟam vītaltoṭarntiṭiyilviṉik, kāṟṟiṉāṟ katināṉkiṟ cuḻalumē”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. nāṟkatiyilum paṟpala yākkiyiṉi yeṭuttu viṉikaḷ payakkum tuṉpattāl aṟivu mayaṅkutaliṉ, “miyaluṟṟaḻuṅki” eṉṟār. nāṉku katikaḷuḷ eṉpuḻi muṟṟumi vikārattāl tokkatu. aiyamil kāṭciyeṉavē, naṟkāṭciyātal peṟṟām. immūṉṟumuṭimi uyarnta voḻukka mākum. “naṉṟāya kāṭciyuṭaṉāya ñāṉantaṉṉō, ṭoṉṟāki yuḷḷat toḻiyāmi oḻukka meṉpa”2 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. aṟivatu eṉavē, aṟintu tīviṉikkañci uyirkaṭkut tīṅku ceytal mutaliya tīviṉi yāvum tuṟantu oḻukuvatu peṟṟām.
caṇṭa karumaṉ kūṟal
239.	ākamat taṭika ḷeṅkaṭ katuperi taritu kaṇṭīr
	ēkacit tatta rāya viṟivarkaṭ keḷitu pōlum
	pōkacit tattō ṭoṉṟip poṟivaḻip paṭaru nīrārk
	kākumaṟ ṟuṟutik kētu aruḷuka teruḷa eṉṟāṉ.
uri:- ākamattu aṭikaḷ - aruka paramaṉ aruḷiya paramāka mattiṉ meyp poruḷi yuṇarnta aṭikaḷē, atu - naṟkāṭci mutaliya mūṉṟum koṇṭu oḻukuvatu, eṅkaṭku - eḷiyavarkaḷākiya eṅkaḷukku, peritu aritu - mikavum ariya ceyalākum, ēka cittattarāya iṟivarkaṭku - ellāp poruḷiyum oruṅkuṇarum ñāṉavāṉkaḷāṉa taṅkaḷ pōlum muṉivarkaṭku, peritu eḷitu - mikavum eḷitākum, pōka cittattōṭu - ulaka pōkantuykkum viruppuṟṟa - maṉattuṭaṉ, oṉṟi - ataṉkaṇ ūṉṟi niṉṟu, poṟi vaḻip paṭarum nīrārkku - aimpoṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka ōṭiyuḻalum iyalpuṭiya eṅkaṭku, ākum - iyinta, uṟutikku ētu - uṟutip poruḷi yaṭitaṟku ētuvākiyavaṟṟi, teruḷa aruḷuka - viḷaṅka urittaruḷvīrāka, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu caṇṭakarumaṉ vēṇṭiṉāṉ.
ākamatti muṉivarkku uṭimiyāka vurikkum iyipu paṟṟi, “ākamattaṭikaḷ” eṉṟāṉ; “meyyuri muṉivartam ākamam”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟupa. aṭikaḷ - aṇmi viḷi. naṟkāṭci mutaliya mūṉṟiyum “tiriyōkamaruntu” eṉṟum, “irattiṉattirayam” eṉṟum orumi vāypāṭṭāṟ kūṟum marapu paṟṟi, “atu” eṉṟār. peritaritu eṉpatu ākātatoṉṟeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟatu. tāṉum piṟitumākiya poruḷ kuṇam tōṟṟaṅkaḷi oruṅkē teḷiya vuṇarum kēvala ñāṉamuṭiyōr eṉṟaṟku, “ēka cittattarāya iṟivarkaṭku” eṉṟāṉ. ēṉimuṉivarkaḷiyum uḷappaṭutti yurikkum muṉṉilippuṟam. pōkacittam, pōkattiṉkaṇ mūḻki ataṉiyē nacciniṟkum maṉam. ēka cittattarip pōlap pōkacittattārum uṇmi neṟiyuṇarntu kaṭittēṟutaṟku vēṇṭum eḷiya neṟi yoṉṟu aruḷal vēṇṭumeṉpāṉ “uṟutikku ētu aruḷuka teruḷa” veṉṟāṉ.
naṉñāṉam mutaliya mūṉṟālu meytum payaṉit tokuttu akampaṉar potuvakiyil vitturittal
240.	aṟṟami laṟivu kāṭci yaruntaki yoḻukka mūṉṟum
	peṟṟaṉar purintu pēṇip peruṅkuṇat toḻuku nīrārk
	kuṟṟiṭu mumpa riṉpa mulakitaṟ kiṟimi tāṉum
	muṟṟamuṉ ṉuritta pēṟum vantuṟum muṟimi yeṉṟāṉ.
uri:- aṟṟam il aṟivu - kuṟṟamillāta naṉ ñāṉamum, kāṭci - naṟ kāṭciyum, aruntaki oḻukkam mūṉṟum - peṟutaṟ kariya takutiyik koṭukkum nalloḻukkamum eṉṟa mūṉṟum, peṟṟaṉar - mēṟkoṇṭu, purintu pēṇi - mika virumpi, peruṅ kuṇattu oḻukum nīrārkku - ciratti mutaliya peruṅkuṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ oḻukum iyal puṭiyōrukku, umpar iṉpam uṟṟiṭum - maṟumikkaṇ tēvarulaka viṉpam uṇṭākum, ulaku itaṟku - immikkaṇ ivvulakiṟku, iṟimitāṉum - iṟivaṉān taṉmiyum, muṉṉuritta pēṟu muṟṟavum muṟimi vantuṟum - muṉṉē uritta pēṟukaḷ muḻutum muṟippaṭi vanteytum, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu akampaṉa muṉivaṉ urittāṉ e-ṟu.
aṟṟamil eṉpataṉik kāṭcikkuk kūṭṭuka. takuti - viḻuppam. oḻukkamuṭiyārkku viḻuppa meytutal takutiyātaliṉ, “taki” yeṉṟār; aḵtu avvoḻukkattāṉaṉṟi yeytuta laritākaliṉ, aruntaki yeṉṟār. kuṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa, “ciratti, patti, alō pattuvam, tayi, catti, kṣami, viññāṉam” eṉpa. iṟimi, aracaṉāntaṉmi. “koliyiṉ tiṇmi”1 “teruḷuṭimaṉattiṉ”2 eṉṟu toṭaṅkum ceyyuṭkaḷil, “kuvalayat tiṟimi” mutaliya pala pēṟukaḷi yurittaliṉ, “muṉṉurittapēṟu” eṉṟār. muṟṟummi pirittuk kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. iḵtu āttaṉuritta ākama muṭipeṉpār, “muṟimi” yeṉṟār.
muṉṉē tokuttuc coṉṉa payaṉi 
viritturittal
241.	uṟuporuḷ nilimi taṉṉi yuṟṟuṇar vaṟiva tākum
	aṟiporu ḷataṉiṟ ṟūymi yakatteḻu teḷivu kāṭci
	naṟumalarp piṇṭi nātaṉ nallaṟap perumi taṉpāl
	iṟukiya makiḻcci kaṇṭāy itaṉatu pirivu meṉṟāṉ.
uri:- uṟuporuḷ nilimi taṉṉi - aṟitaṟ kaminta poruḷkaḷiṉ nilimiyiṉi, uṟṟu uṇarvatu - tūlacūkkuma meṉṟa iruvaḻiyum porunti yaṟivatu, aṟivatākum - naṉñāṉamākum, aṟiporuḷataṉiṉ tūymi - aṟiyappaṭṭa poruḷiṉ uṇmi yiyalpiṉi, akatteḻu teḷivu - aiyam tiripu mutalāka maṉattiṉ kaṇ eḻum vikaṟpa miṉṟit teḷivatu, kāṭci - naṟkāṭciyākum, naṟumalarp piṇṭinātaṉ - naṟiya pūkkaḷiyuṭiya acōkamarattiṉ nīḻalil vīṟṟirukkum arukaparamaṉ  aruḷiya, nallaṟapperumi taṉpāl - viṉiyik keṭuttu vīṭupēṟṟiṉip payakkum nallaṟaṅkaḷiṉ pāl, iṟukiya makiḻcci - mikkuṟṟa aṉpupūṇ ṭoḻukuvatu, itaṉatu pirivum eṉṟāṉ - ikkāṭciyiṉpāṟ paṭṭatēyākum e-ṟu.
avatiñāṉa maṉappariyaya ñāṉaṅkaḷippōlātu, kēvala ñāṉam poruḷkaḷiṉ tūla cūkkuma nilimikaḷi oppa aṟiva tākaliṉ, ataṉiyē vitantu. “uṟuporuḷ nilimi taṉṉi yuṟṟuṇar vaṟivatākum” eṉṟār. “vitiyivi vikalan tūlam cakala niccayamumāmē”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟiṉar. iṟivaṉ, āciriyaṉ mutalāyiṉār taṉmiyum ākamam poruḷ eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉ uṇmi yiṉiyum aiyantiripu mutaliya kuṟṟamiṉṟit teḷivatu cammiyataricaṉam eṉpavākaliṉ, ataṉi, “aṟiporuḷataṉil tūymi yakatteḻu teḷivu kāṭci” yeṉṟār; “iṟivaṉu muṉiyu nālu miyātu mōr kuṟṟa millā, neṟiyiṉit teḷital kāṭciyām”2 eṉpatu kāṇka. ettuṇi iṭiyīṭukaḷuṇṭāyiṉum mēṟ koṇṭa nallaṟattiṉ kaṇ ūṉṟiya aṉpuṭiyarāy caliyā toḻukutal akkāṭciyiṉ payaṉeṉṟaṟku “nallaṟapperumi taṉpāl iṟukiya makiḻcci” eṉṟār. kaṇṭāy, muṉṉiliyaci. teḷinta kāṭciyiṉ payaṉ aṟattiṉkaṇ mikka kātaluṇṭā talātaliṉ, akkātali. “itaṉatu pirivu” eṉṟār. ummi-  ākkapporuṭṭu.
nalloḻukkattiṉ iyalpu kūṟal
242.	perukiya koliyum poyyuṅ kaḷavoṭu piṟaṉma ṉikkaṇ
	terivilāc celavu cinti poruḷvayiṟ ṟiruku paṟṟum
	maruviya maṉattu mīṭci vatamivi yintō ṭoṉṟi
	oruviṉa pulicu tēṉkaḷ oruvuta loḻukka meṉṟāṉ.
uri:- perukiya koliyum - mikka pāvamākiya uyirk koliyum, poyyum - poyyurittalum, kaḷavoṭu - piṟar poruḷi avar aṟiyāmal kaḷavāṭutalum, piṟaṉ maṉikkaṇ terivilāc celavum - piṟaṟkuriyāḷi nayantu varum paḻipāvatti ārāytal iṉṟic ceṉṟoḻukutalum, cinti poruḷvayiṉ tiruku paṟṟum - maṉam poruḷmēṟ ceṉṟu ivaṟi niṟkum kaṭum paṟṟum, maruviya maṉattu mīṭci - poruntiya maṉatti avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭalākiya, vatam ivi aintōṭu oṉṟi - ivvaṇuviratam aintaṉōṭu porunti, oruviṉ - ākāveṉac cāṉṟōrāl vilakkappaṭṭa, pulicu - pulāluṇavum, tēṉ - matuvuṇṭalum, kaḷ - kaṭkuṭittalum ākiya ivaṟṟi, oruvutal - nīkkiyoḻukuvatu, oḻukkam eṉṟāṉ - nalloḻukkam eṉṟu akampaṉa muṉivaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
ellāp pāvattiṉum kolippāvam mikap peritātaliṉ, “perukiya koliyum” eṉṟāṉ. oṭu - eṇṇoṭu, piṟaṉuk kuriyāḷ oruttiyiṉ peṇṇalattik kātalittoḻukutal ārāyu miṭattu aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉṟa nāṉkiṉuḷ oṉṟiṉiyum payavātu immikkaṭ paḻiyum maṟumikkaṇ pāvamum narakamum payattaliṉ, “piṟaṉmaṉikkaṇ terivilāc celavu” eṉṟār. poruḷmēṟceṉṟa paṟṟuḷḷattāl taṉpāluḷḷa poruḷi naṉṉeṟikkaṭ celaviṭāmiyēyaṉṟip piṟar poruḷiyum tī neṟiyāl veḵki yoḻukum kuṟṟamum akappaṭa, “cinti poruḷvayiṉ tirukupaṟṟum” eṉṟār. ivvakik kuṟṟattiṉ pālum cellum uḷḷatti mīṭṭalip pañcāṇu viratam eṉṟum, pulicu tēṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi oruvutali nivirtti yeṉṟum kūṟupa. pañcāṇuvatamum pulicu mutaliya nivirttiyum aṟam palavaṟṟiṟkum mūlamātaliṉ mūlakuṇameṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. “periyakoli poy kaḷavu piṟar maṉi loruval, poruḷ varital, mattamatu puli cuṇaliṉīṅkal”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. mīṭci -  mīṭṭal; “neṟivaḻi yeṅkuñ cellu
mīṭci”2 eṉpuḻippōla.
ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉ kūṟal
243.	koliyiṉa tiṉmi kūṟiṟ kuvalayat tiṟimi ceyyum
	malitalil vāymi yārkkum vāymoḻi matippi yākkum
	viliyilpē raruḷiṉ māṭci viḷippatu kaḷaviṉ mīṭci
	ulitalil perumi tiṭpa muṟuvali yoḻinta tīyum.
uri:- kūṟiṉ - koli mutaliyavaṟṟi oruvutalāl uṇṭā kum payaṉik kūṟumiṭattu, koliyiṉatu iṉmi - kollāmiyākiya aṟam, kuvalayattu iṟimi ceyyum - ivvulakattiṉ iṟivaṉān taṉmiyi nalkum, yārkkum - ettiṟattārkkum, malitalil vāymi - māṟupāṭillāta meymmiyāṉatu, vāy moḻi matippi ākkum - vāyāṟ collum ovvoru collukkum naṉmatippiyuṇṭu paṇṇum, kaḷaviṉ mīṭci - kaḷaviṉkaṇ cellāta maṉamuṭimi, viliyil - matikka muṭiyāta, pēraruḷiṉ māṭci viḷippatu - mikka aruḷuṭi miṭiyāṟ piṟakkum māṇpiṉi yuṇṭupaṇṇum, ulitalil perumi - keṭāta perumiyiṉiyum, tiṭpam uṟu - maṉattiṭpam poruntiya, vali - pērāṇmiyiṉi yum, oḻintatu īyum - ēṉiya piṟaṉmaṉi nayavāmi koṭukkum e-ṟu.
iṟimi - evvuyirum kikūppit toḻattakka talimi. kollāvaṟamuṭiyōṉ maṟumikkaṇ iṟivaṉāy yāvarum vaṇaṅkum ciṟappeytuvatu kuṟittu, “koliyiṉatu iṉmi kūṟiṉ kuvalayat tiṟimi ceyyum” eṉṟār; “kollāṉ pulāli maṟuttāṉik kikūppi ellāvuyirun toḻum”3 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka. maṉattōṭu māṟupāṭillāta meymmi yeṉṟaṟku, “malitalil vāymi” yeṉṟār. vāymi - yātoṉṟum tīmiyilātavaṟṟiyē colvatākaliṉ, ataṉiyā varum virumpi yēṟṟuc ceyvaṉa ceytu ciṟappippa reṉpār, “vāymoḻi matippi yākkum” eṉṟār; “ulakattār uḷḷattuḷ ellā muḷaṉ”1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. aruḷum celvamākap pōṟṟap paṭutaliṉ, “viliyil pēraruḷ” eṉṟār. ciṟumikku maṟutaliyātal paṟṟi perumiyi, ‘ulitalil perumi’ yeṉṟum, maṉattiṭpam ceyviṉiyāl viḷaṅkutaliṉ, ceyaṟkariya ceyyum perumiyi yaṭuttum, kūṟiṉār. piṟaṉ maṉiyi nōkkāmi pērāṇmi yeṉappaṭutaliṉ, “tiṭpa muṟuvali” yeṉṟār. “piṟaṉmaṉi nōkkāta pērāṇmi cāṉṟōrkku, aṟaṉoṉṟō āṉṟa voḻukku”2 eṉpa. puṟappakiyi vēṟaliṉum akappakiyāya kāmatti yaṭakkum tiṟaluṭimi viḷaṅka, “tiṭpamuṟuvali” yeṉṟu ciṟappittāreṉṟu mām. ēṉiyatu, “piṟaṉmaṉikkaṇ terivilāc celavu” (242).
244.	teruḷuṭi maṉattiṟ ceṉṟa teḷintuṇar vāya celvam
	poruḷvayi ṉiṟukka miṉmi puṇarttiṭum pulicu tēṉkaḷ
	oruviya payaṉu maṟṟē* yoḷiyiṉō ṭaḻaku veṉṟi
	poruḷmiku kulaṉō ṭiṉpam puṇartalu māku mātō.
uri:- poruḷvayiṉ iṟukkam iṉmi - poruṭ celvat tiṉ pāl ivaṟutal illāmi, teruḷuṭi maṉattiṉ ceṉṟa - teruṇṭa maṉattāṟ koṇṭa, teḷinta uṇarvāya celvam puṇarntiṭum - teḷinta uṇarcciyākiya celvattit tarum. pulicu - pulāluṇṭaliyum, tēṉkaḷ - tēṉiyum kaḷḷiyum, oruviya payaṉum aṟṟē - nīṅkutalāluṇṭākum payaṉum avvaṇṇamē, oḷiyiṉōṭu - pukaḻuṭaṉ, aḻaku - vaṉappiyum, veṉṟi - veṟṟiyiyum, miku poruḷ - mikka poruḷiyum, mikukulaṉoṭu - kuṭicciṟappuṭaṉ, iṉpam puṇartalum - iṉpa nukarcciyiyum, ākum - tarum e-ṟu.
maṉattil teruḷuṭimiyāṟ piṟakkum payaṉ teḷinta uṇarvātal tōṉṟat, “teruḷuṭi maṉattiṟ ceṉṟa teḷintuṇarvu” eṉṟār. teḷinta uṇarvu, teḷintuṇarveṉa niṉṟatu. uṇarvu kēṭil viḻuccelvamātal paṟṟi, “teḷintuṇarvāya celvam” eṉṟāreṉka. “iṟukiya paṟṟu iṟukkamāyiṟṟu; aḵtāvatu kaṭum paṟṟuḷḷam kāraṇamākap piṟakkum ivaṟaṉmi. ulōpamiṉmi aṟattāṟṟiṟ poruḷic celutti nalluṇarvu mutaliya nalaṅkaḷip payakkum tuṇipupaṟṟi iṟukka miṉmi celvam puṇarttiṭum eṉṟār. aṇuviratam pōlap pulāluṇavu tēṉ kaḷ mutaliyaṉavum naṟpayaṉiyē eytuvikkumeṉpār, “aṟṟē” yeṉṟum, “oḷiyi ṉōṭaḻaku veṉṟi, poruḷ miku kulaṉōṭiṉpam puṇartalum” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. mikutalip poruṭku mēṟṟuka. mātu - ō- aci.
caṇṭakarumaṉ kūṟal
245.	cilipayil vayirat tōḷāy ceppiya poruḷi tellām
	ulitaliṉ makiḻvō ṭuḷḷat tuṇarntaṉi koḷka eṉṉik
	koliyiṉi loruva liṉṟik koṇṭaṉeṉ aruḷiṟ ṟellām
	aliceyva toḻiyiṉ vāḻkki yaḻiyumaṟ ṟaṭika ḷeṉṟāṉ.
uri:- cilipayil vayirat tōḷāy - villēntiya valiya tōḷkaḷi yuṭiya caṇṭakarumaṉē, ceppiya poruḷ itu ellām - itu kāṟum colliya poruḷkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum, ulitalil makiḻvōṭu - keṭātavūkkattuṭaṉ, uḷḷattu uṇarntaṉi koḷka - maṉattē uṇarntu kikkoḷvāyāka, eṉṉa - eṉṟu akampaṉaṉ collavē, aṭikaḷ - aṭikaḷē, aruḷiṟṟellām, tāṅkaḷ urittavellāvaṟṟiyum, koli yiṉil oruval iṉṟi - kolit toḻil viṭuvatiyiṉṟi, koṇṭaṉeṉ - ēṉik kuṟṟaṅkaḷik kiviṭutali mēṟkoṇṭēṉ, aliceyvatu oḻiyiṉ - uyirkaḷik kollum kolittoḻilik kiviṭu vēṉāyiṉ, vāḻkki yaḻiyum - aṭiyēṉatu vāḻkki muṟṟum keṭṭaḻiyum, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu caṇṭakarumaṉ coṉṉāṉ e-ṟu.
aṇuvirata mintum - pulāluṇavu mutaliya nivirttiyum mūla kuṇameṉa oṉṟāyaṭaṅkutaliṉ, “ceppiya poruḷ itu” eṉṟār. ellām eṉṟatu. ataṉ uṭpakuti yaṉittum eñcāmal taḻuvi niṉṟatu. iṉi, orumi paṉmi mayakkameṉṟumām. “aruḷiṟ ṟellām” eṉpuḻiyum ītokkum. makiḻvu, kāraṇa
mākiya ūkkattiṉ mēl niṉṟatu. ūkkattuṭaṉ ipporuḷkaḷi āyntuṇarvataṉ payaṉ akattuṭ kōṭalātaliṉ, “uḷḷattu uṇarntaṉi koḷka” eṉṟār. aṉuvirata mintaṉuḷ kollāmi yoṉṟoḻiya ēṉi nāṉkiyum viṭuvatākak kūṟiya caṇṭakarumaṉ, koliyi viṭāmikku ētu iḵteṉpāṉ, “aliceyva toḻiyiṉ vāḻkkiyaḻiyum” eṉṟāṉ. maṟṟu - acinili.
akampaṉar kūṟal
246.	eṉṟaṭi paṇintu caṇṭa ṉicittatu kaṭavuḷ kēṭṭu
	naṉṟiṉit teḷintā yalli navilici yamuta nalyāḻ
	oṉṟiya ceviṭu mūmum oruvaṉāṟ peṟuta luṇṭō
	iṉṟunī  yuritta taṟṟē yiyampuva tuḷatu kēṇmō.
uri:- eṉṟu - eṉpatāka, caṇṭaṉ - caṇṭakarumaṉ, aṭi paṇintu icittatu - muṉivaṉ aṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkic coṉṉati, kaṭavuḷ kēṭṭu - avvakampaṉa muṉivaṉ kēṭṭu, iṉi - ippōtu, naṉṟu teḷintāyalli - nallaṟattit terintu koṇṭāyilli, ceviṭum, ūmum oṉṟiya oruvaṉāl - ceviṭum ūmiyumākiya irutaṉmiyum oruṅku poruntiya oruvaṉālē, nalyāḻ navil iciyamutam - nalla yāḻiṭattē piṟakkum iciyākiya amutatti. peṟutal uṇṭō - nutartal muṭiyātām. iṉṟu- ippōtu, nī urittatu aṟṟu - nī coṉṉatum attaṉmittātaliṉ, iyampuvatu uḷatu - nī aṟiya urikkat takuvatoṉṟu uṇṭu, kēṇmō - kēṭpāyāka e-ṟu.
kaṭavuḷ - muṉivaṉ. kollāviratam taliyāya aṟamātaliṉ ataṉi teḷiyāturitta caṇṭakarumaṉukku, “nallatu teḷintā yilli” eṉṟāṉ. nīriṉ varutal, nīr niṉṟiḷakal, vāṭpuṇṇuṟutal, urumēṟuṇṭal mutaliya kuṟṟamillāta yāḻ eṉṟaṟku “nalyāḻ” eṉṟum, ataṉpāṟ piṟakkum ici amutampōl iṉpañ ceytaliṉ, “naviliciyamutam” eṉṟum, kūrtta ceviyaṟivum iṉitta miṭaṟṟōciyu moruṅkuṭiyārkkē ici iṉpam ceytaliṉ, iraṇṭumillā reṉṟaṟku, “oṉṟiya ceviṭu mūmum oruvaṉāl perutaluṇṭō” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. aruḷiṉmiyum maṭamiyu moruṅkuṭiya niṉakku yāmuritta nallaṟa vamirtam cellātāyiṟ ṟeṉṟāṉāyiṟṟu. āyiṉum accaṇṭakarumaṉit teruṭṭuvatē karuttātaliṉ, ‘iyampuvatuḷatu kēṇmō’ eṉṟāṉ.
247.	āruyir varuttaṅ kaṇṭā laruḷperi toḻuku kaṇṇāl
	ōruyir pōla neñcat turukinin tuyya niṟṟal
	vāriyiṉ vataṅkaṭ kellā maracanal vatama taṟkē
	cārtuṇi yākak koḷka takavumat tayavu meṉṟāṉ.
uri:- ār uyir varuttam kaṇṭāl - niṟinta uyirkaḷ paṭukiṉṟa tuṉpattik kāṇiṉ, aruḷ peritu oḻukukaṇṇāl - aruḷ mikapperukum kaṇkaḷōṭu, ōr uyir pōla - ataṉuyirit taṉṉuyirēpōlak karuti, neñcattu nintu uruki - maṉam karintu uruki, uyya niṟṟal - atu taṉ varuttattiṉ nīṅki uyyumāṟu utavi ceytu niṟpatu; vāriyiṉ vataṅkaṭkellām - aṟappayaṉukku varuvāyākavuḷḷa virataṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum, araca nalvatam - nalla taliyāya viratamām, ataṟku - avviratattukku, takavum. ceppa muṭimiyiṉiyum, attayavum - ataṉ payaṉākiya aruḷiyum, cārtuṇiyākak koḷka - poruntiya tuṇiyākak koḷvāyāka e-ṟu.
āruyir - peṟutaṟkariya uyir eṉṟumām. uyirkaḷ paṭum tuṉpattik kaṇṭāl, avaṟṟiṉ pāl maṉamiraṅki vēṇṭum utaviyiṉip purintu attuṉpattiṉiṉṟum avaṟṟi uyvittalē koliviṉiyi oruvutaṟkuriya eḷiya neṟiyeṉpār, “neñcat turuki nintuyya niṟṟal” eṉṟār. irakkamuḷatātaṟku vāyil itu veṉṟaṟku, “ōruyirpōla” neñcil karutuka eṉṟār. vāri- varuvāy; “puyaleṉṉum vāri”1 eṉpuḻippōla. taliyāya viratameṉṟaṟku, “aracanalvatam” eṉṟār. aṇuviratam - kuṇaviratam ciṭcāviratam eṉap palavuṇmiyiṉ, “vataṅkaṭkellām” eṉṟār. takutiyi valiyuṟuttiṉār, tamakkuṇṭākum tuṉpattukku ētu tamviṉiyē yaṉṟip piṟa uyirkaḷalla eṉpati yuṇarntu naṭunili vaḻuvātu nalam ceytaṟku. annalam ceytaṟku irakkam neṟiyātaliṉ, “tayavum” cārtuṇiyākak koḷka” eṉṟār. takavum tayavum tuṇiyākakkoṇṭu uyirkaṭku aruḷic ceyka eṉa vitittavāṟu.
248. iṟantanā ḷeṉṉu* muḷḷat tiraṅkuta liṉṟi veytāyk
     kaṟantuyi ruṇṭu kaṉṟik karuviṉi perukac ceytāy
     piṟantunī piṟavi tōṟum perunavi yuṟuva tellāñ
     ciṟantanal laṟatti ṉaṉṟit tīrumā ṟuṇari ṉuṇṭō.†
uri:- iṟanta nāḷ - iṟanta kālattil, uḷḷattu - niṉ maṉattiṉkaṇ, eṉṉum iraṅkutaliṉṟi - ciṟitum  irakkamuṟātu, veytāy - accatticceytu, uyir kaṟantu uṇṭu - uyiri varuttikkoṉṟu, kaṉṟi - akkoli viṉiyil aṭipaṭṭu niṉṟu, karuviṉi perukac ceytāy - tīviṉiyi mikutiyākac ceytāy, nī piṟantu - nī pala piṟappum piṟantu, piṟavitōṟum - appiṟappukkaḷ tōṟum, perunavi uṟuvatellām - mikka tuṉpameytuvatellām, ciṟanta nal aṟattiṉ aṉṟi - ciṟanta aṟaṅkaḷālaṉṟi, uṇariṉ - ārāyumiṭattu, tīrumāṟu uṇṭō - nīṅkum vāyil vēṟuḷatō illiyaṉṟō e-ṟu.
mīṭṭaṟkarumi kuṟittu, “iṟantanāḷ” eṉṟār; “iṟanta nāḷyāvar mīṭpār iṟṟeṉap peyarkkalāmō”1 eṉṟu tēvarum kūṟiṉar. eṉṉum, ciṟitum. veytu, accam. ākkam ceytaṉ mēṉiṉṟatu. eḷitil kavaralākāmi tōṉṟa, “kaṟantuyiruṇṭu” eṉṟār; “kaṟantu kūṟṟuṇṇum ñāṉṟu”2 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. karanteṉpatu etuki nōkkik kaṟanteṉa niṉṟa teṉṟalu moṉṟu. karuviṉi yeṉpuḻik karumi koṭumi mēṟṟu. karuvip piṟappākki, ataṟ kētuvākiya viṉiyeṉṟumām. “iruḷuṭi narakat tuykkum iruviṉi” yeṉṟum, “iruḷ cēr iruviṉi” yeṉṟum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvatu paṟṟik karuviṉi yeṉṟā reṉṟalu moṉṟu. aṟaviṉi tuṉpam keṭuttu iṉpam nukarvittaliṉ, “ciṟanta nallaṟat tiṉaṉṟit tīrumā ṟuṇari ṉuṇṭō” eṉṟār; “tiruntiya nallaṟac cempoṟ kaṟpakam, poruntiya poruḷoṭu pōkam pūttalāl, varuntiṉum aṟattiṉi maṟatta lōmpumiṉ”3 eṉṟu tēvarum kūṟiṉār.
249.	niliyilā vuṭampiṉ vāḻkki neṭituṭa ṉiṟuva leṉṟik
	koliyiṉāṉ muyaṉṟu vāḻuṅ koṟṟava rēṉu muṟṟac
	cilapaka laṉṟi niṉṟār cilariva ṇilli kaṇṭāy
	alitaru piṟavi munnī raḻuntuva raṉantaṅ kālam.
uri:- niliyilā uṭampiṉ vāḻkki - niliyillāta uṭampōṭu kūṭi vāḻum vāḻkkiyi, neṭituṭaṉ niṟuval - neṭitirukkumāṟu ceyvēṉ, eṉṟu - eṉṟu niṉittu, ikkoli yiṉāl muyaṉṟu - cāṉṟōrāl veṟukkappaṭṭa ivvuyirk koliyiṉicceytu, vāḻum koṟṟavarēṉum - vāḻkiṟa veṟṟi. vēntar yāvarāyiṉum, ivaṇ - innilavulakattē, cilapakal niṉṟār aṉṟi - cila nāṭkaḷ vāḻntaṉarē yaṉṟi, cilar muṟṟa illi - pala nāṭkaḷ vāḻntavar palarilarāyiṉum orucilar tāmum karuttu muṟṟa uḷarāṉār illi, alitaru piṟavi munnīr - varuttukiṉṟa piṟaviyākiya kaṭalil, aṉantam kālam - neṭuṅkālam, aḻuntuvar - kariyēṟa māṭṭātu mūḻki varuntuvar e-ṟu.
niliyāmiyē iyalpākavuṭiya uṭampi nilipeṟac ceytu koḷḷak karututal pētimiyeṉpār, “niliyilā vuṭampiṉ vāḻkki neṭituṭaṉ niṟuval” eṉṟum, ūṉuṇavāl uṭampi uram mikuvippiṉ neṭitu vāḻalām eṉṟa karuttāl koli viṉiyic ceytu vāḻkiṉṟa reṉṟaṟku, “koliyiṉāl muyaṉṟu vāḻum koṟṟavar” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. uyirkkoliyē tamakkuk koṟṟamākak karutuva reṉṟaṟkuk “koṟṟavar” eṉṟār. neṭitu vāḻavēṇṭi muyaṉṟavar tam muyaṟci muṟṟāmiyiṉ, oru cila nāṭkaḷēṉum neṭitu iruntār illi yeṉpatu viḷaṅka, “cilapakalaṉṟi niṉṟār cilarivaṇ illi” eṉṟār. ciṟappummi vikārattāl tokkatu. kaṇṭāy, muṉṉiliyaci. piṟappiṟappuk kaḷāl iṭiyaṟātu tuṉpuṟutaliṉ, “alitaru piṟavi munnīr” eṉṟum, munnīr eṉṟataṟ kēṟpa, “aḻuntuvar” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. na+antam - anantam; aṉantameṉa vantatu; muṭivillāmi eṉpatu poruḷ. neṭuṅkālam piṟavikkaṭalik kaṭantu kariyēṟa māṭṭātu mūḻkuva reṉpatām.
250.	iṉṉumī tiya kēṭka yacōmati tanti yāya
	maṉṉava ṉaṉṉi yōṭu māviṉaṟ kōḻi taṉṉik
	koṉṉavil vāḷiṟ koṉṟa koṭumiyiṟ kaṭiya tuṉpam
	piṉṉavar piṟavi tōṟum peṟṟaṉa pēca lāmō.
uri:- aiya - aiyaṉē, iṉṉum ītu kēṭka - mēlum itaṉik kēṭpāyāka, yacōmati tantiyāya maṉṉavaṉ - vēntaṉāṉa yacōmatikkut tantiyākiya yacōtara maṉṉavaṉ, aṉṉiyōṭu - taṉ aṉṉiyākiya cantiramatiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, māviṉ nalkōḻi taṉṉi - māvāl ceyyappaṭṭa kōḻi yoṉṟi, koṉnavil vāḷil koṉṟa - uyirkaṭku accattit tarukiṉṟa vāḷiṉāl koṉṟataṉāluṇṭākiya, koṭumiyiṉ kaṭiya tuṉpam - koṭumi poruntiya mikka tuṉpam, piṉ - tām iṟanta piṉṉar, avar piṟavi tōṟum peṟṟaṉa - avar eṭutta piṟappukkaḷ tōṟum aṭinta vaṟṟi, pēcal āmō - colla muṭiyumō muṭiyātu e-ṟu.
tuṉpam peṟṟaṉa pēcalāmō eṉa iyiyum. yacōtaraṉākiya maṉṉava ṉeṉṉātu, “yacōmatikkut tantiyāya maṉṉavaṉ” eṉṟār, ippōtu caṇṭakarumaṉukku aracaṉāka iruppavaṉ av yacōmati yātaliṉ. “aṉṉiyōṭu” eṉṟatu, av yacōtarataṟkut tāyākiya cantiramatiyē mākkōḻiyik koṉṟa koliviṉikku ēvum viṉimutalumātal paṟṟi. māvāl ceyyappaṭṭa tāyiṉum, uyiruṭiyatu pōlat tōṉṟum toḻil nalamuṭimi tōṉṟa, “māviṉ nal kōḻi” eṉṟār. koṉṟa - kāraṇapporuṭṭāya peyareccam. iṉ - alvaḻikkaṇ vantatu. kaṭi, mikuti - ōkāram, etirmaṟi. pēciṉ, atu kūṟiyatu kūṟalāy vēṟupayaṉoṉṟum viḷiviyāmiyiṉ, “pēcalāmō” eṉṟār.
251.	vīṅkiya viṉikaḷ tammāl veruvarat takka tuṉpam
	tāṅkiṉar piṟanti ṟantu taḷarntaṉar vilaṅkiṟ celvār
	āṅkavar tāṅkaḷ kaṇṭā yaruviṉi turappa vantār
	īṅkuniṉ ṉayala kūṭṭi liruntakō ḻikaḷā yeṉṟāṉ.
uri:- vīṅkiya viṉikaḷ tammāl - mikkuṟṟa viṉikaḷāl, veruvarattakka tuṉpam - añcattakka tuṉpaṅkaḷi, tāṅkiṉar - tāṅki, piṟantu iṟantu taḷarntaṉar - piṟantum iṟantum mikattaḷarntu, vilaṅkiṉ celvār - vilaṅkukatiyuḷ avyacōtaraṉum, cantiramatiyum piṟantuḻalvārāka, āṅku - avviṭattum, avartāṅkaḷ - avarkaḷ, iruviṉi turappa - pōkkutaṟkariya viṉikaḷ celutta, īṅku - ivviṭattē, niṉ ayala - niṉpakkattēyuḷḷa, kūṭṭil irunta - kūṭṭil iṭappaṭṭu vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟa, kōḻikaḷāy vantār - kōḻiyāyp piṟantu vantuḷḷārkaḷ e-ṟu.
kaṇṭāy, muṉṉiliyaci. palavāyp perukiya perukkam tōṉṟa, “vīṅkiya viṉikaḷ” eṉṟār. veṟukkappaṭuvatōṭu evvuyirālum añcappaṭuvatupaṟṟi, tuṉpatti, “veruvarattakka tuṉpam” eṉṟār. piṟappiṟappukkaḷāl uyirkaḷ iḷittumelital iyalpātaliṉ, “piṟantiṟantu taḷarntaṉar” eṉṟār. “ellāp piṟappum piṟantiḷittēṉ” eṉṟu māṇikkavācakapperumāṉum kūṟiyirut talik kāṇka. vilaṅkukatiyuṭ piṟantiṟantu tuṉpuṟṟa avarkaḷē iṅkēyuḷḷa kōḻikaḷāyp piṟantuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟaṟku, “īṅku niṉṉayala kūṭṭil irunta kōḻikaḷāy vantār” eṉṟum, ap piṟappiṉkaṇṇum avarkaḷ īṭṭiya viṉikaḷ avarkaḷi viṭṭila eṉpār, “aruviṉi turappa vantār” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
252.	uyirava ṇilli yēṉu muyirkkoli niṉippi ṉālim
	mayarikaḷ piṟavi tōṟum varuntiya varuttaṅ kaṇṭāl
	uyiriṉi laruḷoṉ ṟiṉṟi yuvantaṉar koṉṟu ceṉṟār
	ceyirtaru naraki ṉallāṟ celliṭa milli yeṉṟāṉ.
uri:- avaṇ uyir illi yēṉum - māvāṟ ceyta kōḻiyiṭattē uyir illiyāyiṉum, uyirkkoli niṉip piṉāl - uyiroṉṟik kolluvatākak karutic ceyta tī niṉi vāl, immayarikaḷ - immayakkamuṭiyavuyirkaḷ, piṟavitōṟum varuntiya varuttam - eṭutta piṟappukkaḷtōṟum eytiya tuṉpatti, kaṇṭāl - ārāyumiṭattu, uyiriṉil - uyirkaḷiṭattē, aruḷ oṉṟu iṉṟi - irakkam ciṟitumillāmal, uvantaṉar koṉṟu tiṉṟār - viruppattuṭaṉ koṉṟu vāḻntavar, cel iṭam - iṟantāl pukumiṭam, ceyirtaru narakiṉ allāl illi - tuṉpantarum narakamallatu vēṟilli, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu muṉivaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ - e-ṟu.
“māviṉ nalkōḻi”1 yiṉpāl kōḻiyiṉ vaṭivamuṇṭē yaṉṟi uyirilli yātaliṉ, ataṉi vāḷāl koṉṟa vaḻik koliviṉi yilliyeṉa veḻum aiyak kūṟṟiṉi maṟuttaliṉ, “uyiravaṇ illiyēṉum” eṉa mēṟkoṇṭu, uyirilla tāyiṉum uyiruṭiya teṉak karutik koliceytaliṉ koliyē yeṉpār, kolip payaṉmēl vittu, “uyirkkoli niṉippiṉāl immayarikaḷ piṟavi tōṟum varuntiya varuttam kaṇṭāl” eṉṟār. oṉṟu - ciṟiteṉṉum poruḷpaṭa niṉṟatu. kolippāvam ceytavarkku maṟumikkaṇ narakamē pukaliṭam eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttavē, “ceyirtarunarakiṉ, allāl celliṭa milli”  eṉṟār. narakiṉ - iṉ - alvaḻikṇ vantatu.
caṇṭakarumaṉ kūṟal
253.	maṟṟavaṉ iṉiya kūṟa maṉanaṉi kalaṅki vāṭic
	ceṟṟamuñ ciṉamu nīkkit tiruvaṟat teḷivu kātal
	paṟṟiṉaṉ vataṅkaḷ muṉṉam pakarntaṉa vaṉittuṅ koṇṭu
	peṟṟaṉa ṉaṭikaḷ numpāṟ* perumpaya ṉeṉṟu pōntāṉ.
uri:- avaṉ iṉiya kūṟa - akampaṉaṉ ivvāṟu kūṟa, maṉam naṉi kalaṅki vāṭi - caṇṭakarumaṉ maṉaamiti mikakkulintu vāṭṭa meyti, ceṟṟamum ciṉamum nīkki - taṉ maṉattiṉkaṇ kuṭikoṇṭirunta pakimiyum vekuḷiyum pōkki, tiru aṟatteḷivu kātal paṟṟiṉaṉ - ciṉa tarumattiṉ teḷinta niliyiṉkaṇ aṉpukoṇṭu, muṉṉam pakarntaṉa - muṉṉē kūṟappaṭṭaṉavākiya, vataṅkaḷ aṉit tum koṇṭu - virata mellāvaṟṟiyum viṭātu mēṟkoṇṭu, aṭikaḷ - aṭikaḷē, numpāl - tēvarīriṭattē, perum payaṉ peṟṟaṉaṉ - peritākiya ñāṉappayaṉip peṟṟēṉ, eṉṟu - eṉṟu colli vaṇaṅki viṭipeṟṟukkoṇṭu, pōntāṉ - tāṉ muṉṉirunta iṭattiṟku varalāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷikkoṉṟu vāḻpavaṉātaliṉ, caṇṭa karumaṉ, kolippāvappayaṉi akampaṉaṉ kūṟakkēṭṭalum pēraccameyti varuntat toṭaṅkiṉāṉeṉpār, “maṉam naṉi kalaṅkivāṭi” eṉṟār. maṉattiṉatu kalakkam mey vāṭṭattāṟ pulaṉātaliṉ, “vāṭi” yeṉṟār. ciṉattiṉ kāriyamāyt tīviṉi nikaḻccikkuk kāraṇamāy niṟkum maṉanili ceṟṟamākum. ciṉa milvaḻic ceṟṟam nikaḻāmiyiṉ, “ceṟṟamum ciṉamum” iṇittuk kūṟiṉār. vataṅkaḷi muṉpu “perukiya koliyum” (242) eṉṟu toṭaṅkum ceyyuḷ mutalāyavaṟṟuḷ kūṟiṉārātaliṉ, “muṉṉam pakarntaṉa” eṉṟār. piṟavāneṟiyaruḷum pēraṟapporuḷi mēṟ koṇṭa makiḻcciyāṟ kūṟuvatu tōṉṟa, “peṟṟaṉa ṉaṭika ṇumpāṟ perumpayaṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
kōḻikaḷ tammuḷ niṉittal
254.	kēṭṭalu maṭikaḷ vāyiṟ keḻumiya moḻikaḷ tammik
	kūṭṭiṉu ḷirunta maṟṟak kōḻikaḷ piṟappu ṇarntiṭ
	ṭōṭṭiya ciṉatta vāki yuṟuvata muyntu koṇṭa
	pāṭṭarun taṉmit taṉṟē pāṉmiyiṟ paricu tāṉum.
uri:- aṭikaḷ vāyil keḻumiya moḻikaḷ tammi - akampaṉa muṉivar tamatu vāyāṟ colliya coṟkaḷi, kūṭṭiṉuḷ irunta - caṇṭakarumaṉ amittirunta kūṭṭukkuḷ irunta, ak kōḻikaḷ - akkōḻikaḷ iraṇṭum, kēṭṭalum - kēṭṭavuṭaṉē, piṟappu uṇarntiṭṭu - tammuṭiya piṟarppu varalāṟṟi niṉin taṟintu, ōṭṭiya ciṉattavāki - tam maṉattiṉ kaṭkoṇṭirunta ciṉa mutaliya kuṟṟaṅkaḷi aṟakkaṭintu, uṟuvatam - mikkaṉavākiya pañcāṇuviratam mutaliyavaṟṟi, koṇṭu uynta - viṭātu mēṟkoṇṭu piṟavikkētuvāya neṟiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅki uyti peṟṟaṉa, pāṉmiyiṉ paricutāṉum - ūḻviṉiyiṉ taṉmiyum, pāṭu arum taṉmittu - pāṭutaṟku ariya taṉmiyiṉiyuṭittu e-ṟu.
kōḻikaḷ moḻikaḷ tammikkēṭṭalum piṟappuṇarntiṭṭu ciṉattavāki, koṇṭuynta eṉakkūṭṭi muṭikka. uyntu koṇṭa eṉpatik koṇṭuynta eṉappirittuk kūṭṭuka. uyntaṉa eṉaṟ pālatu uynta eṉa niṉṟatu. ōṭṭiya ciṉattavāki eṉṟatu “ceṉṟu cērkallāppuḷḷa viyaṉkuḷam” eṉpatupōla niṉṟatu. aṇuviratam piṟaviyaṟa muyalvārkku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavātaliṉ “uṟuvatam” eṉṟār. tiruvaṟam kēṭkum iyipu ciṟitumiṉṟik kūṭṭiṉuṭ kiṭantu, avvaṟamaṟiyāṉpāl vaḷarnta kōḻikaṭkum ataṉik kēṭṭaṟ kaminta vāyppiṉi ūḻviṉi kūṭṭiyatukaṇṭu viyantu kūṟaliṉ, “pāṭṭarun taṉmittaṉṟē pāṉmiyiṟ paricutāṉum” eṉṟār. aṉṟē eṉpuḻi ēkāram etirmaṟi yātaliṉ, ām eṉṉum uṭaṉ pāṭṭup poruṇmi tōṉṟa niṟpatāyiṟṟu. tāṉ, kaṭṭuric cuvi kuṟittu niṉṟatu. ummi - ciṟappu.
255.	piṟavika ḷaṉittu neñciṟ peyarntaṉa niṉittu muṉṉar
	maṟaviyiṉ mayaṅki māṟṟiṉ maṟukiṉa maruku ceṉṟē
	aṟaviṟi yaṭikaḷ tammā laṟavamir tārap peṟṟēm
	piṟaviyiṉ maṟuku vennōy piḻittaṉa meṉṟa vāṟē.
uri:-peyarntaṉa piṟavikaḷ aṉittum - kaḻintaṉavākiya piṟappukkaḷ aṉittiyum, neñcil niṉittu - tam neñcilē niṉintu maṟaviyiṉ mayaṅki - maṟatiyāl mayakkamuṟṟu, muṉṉar - muṉpellām, māṟṟil maṟukiṉam - uṭal vāḻkkiyil icci yuṟṟu aṟiviḻantuḻaṉṟōm. aṟa iṟi aṭikaḷ tammāl - ciṉa tarumattiṉ talivarākiya ivvakampaṉa muṉikaḷāl, arukuceṉṟu - avar arukēyiruntu, aṟavamirtu ārappeṟṟēm - aṟamākiya amutatti nirampap peṟṟōm, piṟaviyiṉ maṟuku vennōy - piṟaviyāl eytum mayakkattiṉ payaṉāka vuḷatākum vevviya tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum, piḻittaṉam - nīṅkiṉōm, eṉṟavāṟu - eṉṟaporuḷ tōṉṟumāṟu kūviṉa1 e-ṟu.
maṉavuṇar villāta piṟavi yeṭuttuḷḷaṉavāyiṉum niṉit talicceytaṉa eṉat tām vēṇṭiya karutti muṭittaṟku, “neñcil niṉittu” eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. niṉikkappaṭṭaṉa ivi yeṉpār, “peyarntaṉa piṟavikaḷaṉittum” eṉṟār. vīṭupēṟṟiṟ kuriya neṟikkaṭ celuttātu, mēlum piṟattaṟ kētuvāya neṟikaḷilēyē uyirkaḷic celuttutaliṉ, ulaka vāḻkkiyi “māṟṟu” eṉṟum, ataṉāl viḷivatu, mayakkamum ataṉ payaṉākap piṟappumē eṉṟaṟku, “maṟaviyiṉ mayaṅki māṟṟiṉ maṟukiṉam” eṉṟum niṉittaṉa. “aṟiyiṉ vīṭṭatum māṟṟatum ākumē”2 eṉṟum, “māṟṟil niṉṟatu viyaka mūṉṟiṉum, āṟṟavum pariyaṭṭa morintiṉāl, tōṟṟam vītal toṭarntiṭiyil viṉik, kāṟṟiṉāṟkati nāṉkiṟ cuḻalumē”1 eṉṟum mērumantara purāṇam kūṟutal kāṇka. aṟam kēṭṭa payaṉ itu veṉṟaṟkup “piṟaviyiṉ maṟuku vennōy piḻittaṉam” eṉṟu kūviṉa.
kōḻiyiṉ kūkkurali yaracaṉ kēṭṭal
256.	aṟivaraṉ caraṇa mūḻki yaṟatteḻu viruppa muḷḷāk
	kuṟivilā vamutaṅ koṇṭu kuḷirntaka makiḻntu kūvac
	ceṟipoḻi lataṉuṭ ceṉṟu ceviyiṉu ḷicippa maṉṉaṉ
	muṟuvalkoṇ mukattu nallār mukattoru ciliva ḷittāṉ.
uri:- aṟivaraṉ caraṇam mūḻki - aṟivaṉākiya akampaṉa muṉikaḷiṉ tiruvaṭiyi vaṇaṅki, aṟatteḻu viruppam uḷḷā - ciṉa tarumattiṉpāl eḻunta viruppamāṉatu uḷḷattē kiḷarn teḻuvatāl, kuṟivilā amutamkoṇṭu - perukukiṉṟa avvaṟa vamirtattiyuṭ koṇṭu, akam kuḷirntu makiḻntu kūva - maṉam kuḷirppum makiḻcciyum oruṅkeytavē ak kōḻikaḷ kūviṉavāka, ceṟi poḻilataṉuḷ ceṉṟu - marañceṭikaḷ ceṟintirunta cōlikkuḷ ceṉṟu, maṉṉaṉ ceviyiṉuḷ icippa - aṅkē yirunta yacōmati vēntaṉuṭiya ceviyil akkūkkural olikka, muṟuval koḷ mukattu nallār - avaṉikkaṇṭu muṟuvalitta mukatti yuṭiyarākiya urimi makaḷir kāṇa, mukattu - avar mukattiluḷḷa puruvatti yotta, oru cili vaḷittāṉ - taṉ taṉivilli vaḷittu akkūkkural vanta ticinōkki ampu toṭuttu viṭṭāṉ e-ṟu.
muṉivaraṉ eṉpatu pōla aṟiñaruḷ uyarntōṉ eṉpatu paṭa aṟivāṉ eṉpatu niṉṟatu. aṭivaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭuvati aṭi mūḻkuta leṉpatu perumpālum ciṉa nūl vaḻakku, “aṟivaṉaciraṇa mūḻki”2  “arukaṉic caraṇamūḻki”3 eṉṟu mērumantara purāṇamum ivvāṟē cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliyaṉavum vaḻaṅkutalikkaṇṭu koḷka. piṟavit tuṉpattāl ventu varuntiya varuttam nīṅkutalāl. “akam kuḷirntu” kūviṉa eṉavaṟika. iruḷ paṭap potuḷiya poḻil eṉṟaṟkuc “ceṟipoḻil” eṉṟum, aṉṉatāyiṉum ataṉuḷḷirunta vēntaṉ cevikkuḷ akkōḻikaḷiṉ kūkkural ceṉṟu olikkāvāṟu taṭukkum matukiyuṭittaṉ ṟeṉpataṟkuc “ceṟipoḻilataṉuḷ ceṉṟu” eṉṟum vicēṭittār. aṟattiṉpāṟ ceṉṟa vēṭkiyāṟ piṟakkum ñāṉam amutampōl iṉpam ceyvatupaṟṟi, ataṉi amutam eṉṟum, atu mēṉmēlum perukuvatupaṟṟi, “kuṟivilāvamutam” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār pōlum. piṟavikkētuvāya niṉivu ceyalkaḷāl mayaṅkik keṭuvatu neṟiyaṉ ṟeṉpatupōla ak kūkkural maṉṉaṉ cevimuta licitta teṉpār, “ceviyiṉuḷ icippa” eṉṟum, apporuṇmi tērntu naṉṉeṟikkaṭ cellum ūḻilaṉātaliṉ, vekuṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, ataṟku avaṉic cūḻavirunta makaḷir kūkkural kēṭṭut tiṭukkiṭṭu maruṇṭu naṭuṅkiyatē kāraṇameṉṟum, ātalāl avaṉ ammakaḷi ruṭiya murinta puruvampōṉṟa villivaḷittu ampu toṭuttu ak kuralpōnta tici nōkki viṭṭāṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. mukam - ākupeyar. taṉpāl vittu viṭutta ampiṉi yutittu ōciyiṭṭa kōḻikaḷit tākkikkoṉṟa ciṟappukkuṟittu, “orucili” eṉṟār. vaḷittal, viṭuttaṟkātaliṉ, kāriyattik kāraṇamāka upacarittār.
kōḻikaḷ iṟantu maṉitavuṭam peṭuttal
257.	collaṟi kaṇiyi vāṅkit  toṭuttavaṉ viṭutta lōṭum
	nalliṟip paṟavi tammi naṭukkiya  taṭuttu vīḻac
	cillaṟi viṉika ḷēṉun tiruvaṟap perumi yālē
	vallitiṉ maṟintu pōki māṉuṭam pāya vaṉṟē.
uri:- avaṉ - avyacōmati vēntaṉ, col aṟikaṇiyi - colpōla virintēkum ampiṉi, vāṅki - eṭuttu, toṭuttu - villil toṭuttu, viṭuttalōṭu - eytatum, nal iṟippaṟavi tammi aṭuttu - nalla ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya kōḻikaḷi yaṭuttut tākki, naṭukkiyatu vīḻa - koṉṟu vīḻttavē, cil aṟiviṉi kaḷēṉum - cilavākiya poruḷi yaṟitalākiya viṉikaḷāyiṉum, tiru aṟapperumiyāl - ciṉa tarumaṅkaḷākiya perumiyuṭiyaṉa vākiyāl. vallitiṉ - virivāka, maṟintu pōki - akkōḻi kaḷiṉ uyir uṭaliṉ nīṅkicceṉṟu, māṉuṭampāya - makkaḷuṭampip poruntiṉa e-ṟu.
oruvarikkuṟittu oru collic colliyavaḻi, accol avarit tappāmaṟ ceṉṟu cārtalpōla, oṉṟikkuṟittu eytavaḻi, ataṉit tappāmaṟ ceṉṟu tākkum ciṟappukkuṟittu, “collaṟi kaṇi” eṉṟār. intiravilpōlum ciṟaku eṉa muṉṉarc 1 ciṟappit tamiyiṉ, “īṇṭu nalliṟippaṟavi” yeṉṟār. vīḻa, piṟaviṉip poruṭṭu. ciṉmi, kēṭkappaṭum poruḷ mēṉiṉṟatu. uṭaliṉiṉṟum uyir nīṅkuvatu evarkkum pulaṉā kāmiyiṉ “maṟintu pōki” eṉṟār. māṉuṭampu, makkaḷuṭampu.
aracaṉ taṉ kōyili yaṭital
258.	viriceṟi poḻili ṉuḷḷāl vēṉalil viḷinta ellām
	aracaṉu mamarntu pōki yakanakark kōyi leyti
	muracoli kaḻumap pukku moymmali kuḻali ṉārō
	ṭuriceya lariya vaṇṇa muvakiyiṉ mūḻki ṉāṉē.
uri:- viriceṟi poḻiliṉuḷḷāl - maṇam niṟinta cōlikkuḷḷē, vēṉalil viḷinta ellām - vēṉiṟ paruvattu viḷiyum iṉpaṅkaḷellām, aracaṉum amarntu - vēntaṉākiya yacōmatiyum virumpi nukarntu, pōki - ataṉiṉ nīṅkic ceṉṟu, akanakark kōyil eyti - akanakarkkaṇṇuḷḷa taṉṉaraṇ maṉiyi yaṭintu, muracoli kaḻuma - muracoli muḻaṅka, pukku - uṭceṉṟu - moymmalark kuḻaliṉārōṭu - neruṅkiya pūvaṇinta kūntaliyuṭiya makaḷiruṭaṉ, uriceyal ariya vaṇṇam - urittaṟku oṇṇāta vakiyil, uvakiyil mūḻkiṉāṉ - iṉpak kaṭaluḷ mūḻkik karikāṇātu tiḷittāṉ e-ṟu.
pūkkaḷ niṟinta cōli yeṉpār, “viri ceṟi poḻil” eṉṟār. vēṉalil viḷinta ellām eṉṟatu vēṉiṟ kālat tayarum viḷiyāṭṭu vakikaḷ. aracaṉ kōyiliṉ nīṅkic cellum pōtum, tirumpap pōtarum pōtum muracu muḻaṅku taluṇmiyiṉ, “muracolikaḻuma” eṉṟum, makaḷirōṭukūṭa avaṉ iṉpuṟṟa vakikaḷik kūṟal nīrmiyaṉmiyiṉ, “uri ceyalariya vaṇṇam uvakiyiṉ mūḻkiṉāṉ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
puṭpāvali iraṭṭiyarip peṟutal
259.	iṉṉaṇa maraca celvat ticōmati cellu nāḷuḷ
	poṉṉiya laṇikoḷ puṭpā valiyeṉum poṅku koṅki
	iṉṉiya liraṭṭi yāku miḷiyari yīṉṟu ciṉṉāḷ
	piṉṉumōr ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉip peṟṟaṉaḷ pēti tāṉē.
uri:- iṉṉaṉam - iv vaṇṇam, araca celvattu - araca pōkattil, icōmati cellu nāḷuḷ - yacōmati vāḻntuvaruṅ kālattil, poṉ iyal aṇikoḷ puṭpāvali yeṉum - poṉṉāṉiyaṉṟa aṇikaḷi yaṇinta puṭpāvali yeṉṉum, poṅku koṅki - perutta mulikaḷiyuṭiya aracamātēvi, iṉṉiyal iraṭṭiyākum iḷiyari - iṉiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiya iraṭṭippiḷḷikaḷi, īṉṟu - peṟṟu, cila nāḷ piṉṉum - cila āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarum, ōr ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉi - oru makaṉi, pēti - pētiyākiya avaḷ, peṟṟaṉaḷ - peṟṟeṭuttāḷ e-ṟu.
niliyillātākiya araca pōkatti niliyuḷḷatākak karuti ataṉkaṇ tōyntu piṟavuyar niṉivukaḷiṉṟi oḻukiṉāṉeṉpār, “aracacelvattu icōmati cellu nāḷuḷ” eṉṟār. “poṉṉi yalaṇikoḷ” eṉṟum, “poṅka koṅki” eṉṟum vicēṭittār, puṭpāvaliyiṉ ceyaṟkiyum - iyaṟkiyumākiya aḻakutōṉṟa. mutaṟkaṇ āṇum peṇṇumākiya iraṭṭip piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟamitōṉṟa, “iṉṉiyal iraṭṭiyākum iḷiyariyīṉṟu” eṉṟum, avviruvarkkuppiṉṉē vēṟoru makaṉip peṟṟā ḷeṉṟum kūṟiṉavar “pēti”yeṉa avaḷi vicēṭittār, avaḷuṭiya kuṇattic ciṟappittaṟku. tāṉ eṉpatu kaṭṭuric cuvi kuṟittu niṉṟatu. ēkāram - īṟṟaci.
makkaḷiṉ peyarnalam kūṟal
260.	aṉṉavar tammuḷ muṉṉō ṉapayamuṉ ṉuruci taṅki
	aṉṉameṉ ṉaṭiyi ṉāḷu mapayamuṉ matiyeṉ pāḷām
	piṉṉavar vaḷaru nāḷuṭ piṟantava ṉiṟaṅkoḷ pintār
	iṉṉiḷaṅ kumara ṉāma micōtara ṉeṉpatākum.
uri:- aṉṉavar tammuḷ muṉṉōṉ - ammakkaḷuḷ muṉ piṟantavaṉ, apayamuṉ uruci - apayameṉṟa peyarkku muṉ ruciyeṉpatu kūṭavarum apayaruci eṉṉum peyariṉaṉākum, taṅki - avaṉukkut taṅkiyākiya, aṉṉam melnaṭiyiṉāḷum - aṉṉam pōlum melliya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiyavaḷ, apayamuṉ matiyeṉ pāḷām - apayamatiyeṉṉum peyaruṭiyaḷāvāḷ, avar vaḷarum nāḷuḷ. avviruvarum piṟantu vaḷarntu varum nāṭkaḷil, piṉ piṟantavaṉ - avarkaṭkup piṉṉē piṟantavaṉum, niḷaṅkoḷ pintār iṉṉiḷaṅkumaraṉ - mārpiṭattēyaṇinta paciyamāliyiṉi yuṭiyavaṉum iṉiya iḷamippaṇpuṭiyavaṉumākiya kumaraṉuṭiya, nāmam - peyar, icōtaraṉ eṉpatākum - yacōtaraṉ eṉpatām e-ṟu.
apayaruciyē ikkūṟṟiṉi nikaḻttukiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, taṉ taṅkiyi “aṉṉameṉṉaṭiyiṉāḷ” eṉṟum, yacōtaraṉi, “niṟaṅ koḷ pintār iṉṉiḷaṅkumaraṉ” eṉṟum ciṟappikkiṉṟāṉ. niṟam - mārpu. avar vaḷarum nāḷuḷ piṉ piṟantavaṉ eṉa iyikka.
aracakumaraṉ vaḷarnta tiṟam kūṟal
261.	parimicip paṭipa yiṉṟum pārmicit tērka ṭāyum
	variciyiṟ karimēṟ koṇṭu vāṭṭoḻil payiṉṟu maṉṉark
	kuriyaat toḻilka ḷōṭu kalikaḷiṉ celavi yōrntum
	araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ ceṉṟā ṉaṭuttatu kūṟa luṟṟēṉ.
uri:- parimicippaṭi payiṉṟum - kutiri mēliruntu ceyyum pōrviṉikkuriya paṭittoḻil kaṟṟum, pārmicit tērkaṭāyum - tarimēl tēr celuttiyum, variciyiṉ - muṟip paṭi, karimēṟkoṇṭu - yāṉimēlēṟi, vāḷ toḻil payiṉṟum vāḷ viṉikkuriyavaṟṟik kaṟṟum, maṉṉarkku uriya attoḻil kaḷōṭu - aracarkkuriya appayiṟcikaḷōṭu, kalikaḷiṉ celavi ōrntu - palavakik kalikaḷiyum kaṟṟu, araciḷaṅkumaraṉ ceṉṟāṉ - aracakumaraṉāṉa yacōtaraṉ vaḷartaluṟṟāṉ. aṭuttatu kūṟuluṟṟēṉ - iṉi avvaracakumaraṟ kuṟṟattik kūṟattoṭaṅku kiṟēṉ e-ṟu.
yāṉiyēṟṟam, kutiriyēṟṟam, tērēṟutal, mutaliyaṉa araca kumarar payiṟaṟkuriya payiṟcikaḷ. ivaṟṟi neṟiyaṟintu celuttalum ivaṟṟiṉmēliruntu paṭi vaḻaṅkutalum pōr
viṉikku iṉṟi yamiyātaṉa. paṭikkalam payiṟaluṭaṉ palvakik kalikaḷiṉ aṟivu kāvaṟ cākāṭu kippōṟkuk kaṇpōl vēṇṭap
paṭutaliṉ “kalikaḷiṉ celavi yōrntum” eṉṟār.
nāṉkāvatu carukkam muṭintatu.
aintāvatu carukkam
itaṉkaṇ, vēntaṉāṉa yacōmati vīrar puṭicūḻa vēṭṭi mēṟcelvatum, kāṭṭakattē cutatta muṉikaḷik kāṇpatum, ammuṉivaṉik kollak karuti vāḷiyuruvuvatum, avaṉ ceyalik kaṇṭa kaliyāṇamittiraṉeṉṉum uyirttōḻaṉ avaṉit taṭuppatum, avaṉ yacōmatikku muṉivaṉuṭiya ciṟappum varalāṟum kūṟuvatum, vēntaṉ maruṇṭu taṉ taliyit tuṇittu muṉivaṉ tiruvaṭiyiliṭak karutuvatum, kaliyāṇamittiraṉ taṭuttuc ceyvaki kūṟuvatum, atukēṭṭa vēntaṉ muṉivaṉi vaṇaṅkuvatum, muṉivaṉ vēntaṟku acōkaṉ eytiya piṟappu nalam kūṟuvatum, avaṉ tāyākiya amirtamati narakattil tuyaruṟum tiṟam kūṟuvatum, yacōtaraṉum cantiramatiyum mākkōḻiceytu ataṉik koliceyta tīviṉi kuṟittup palvakip piṟappuṟṟu varuntiyatu kūṟuvatum, avarkaḷ iruvarum avvēntaṉuṭiya iraṭṭi makkaḷeṉpatum, aracaṉ avarkaḷi muṉivaṉpāl varuvittalum, makkaḷ pōntu paḻampiṟap puṇarntu varuntuvatum, yacōmati taṉakku uytiyaruḷavēṇṭumeṉa muṉivaṉi yirappatum, muṉivaṉ aṟaṅkūṟak kēṭṭut tuṟavu pūṇpatum, piṉpu apayaruci tāṉ ēṟṟa aracurimiyit tampi yacōtaraṟ kaḷittut taṅkiyāṉa apaya matiyuṭaṉ tuṟavumēṟkoṇṭu cutatta muṉikaḷiyaṭintu avaric cūḻavarum upācakar kuḻuviluṟivatum, ammuṉivaṉuṭaṉ irāca purattukkup puṟattuḷḷa poḻiliṟṟaṅkic cariti vantavāṟu kūṟuvatum, ivaṟṟik kēṭṭirunta māritattaṉ tuṟavu mēṟkoḷvatum, piṉpu avaṉ muṉivaṉātalum, apayaruciyum apayamatiyum tavaneṟik kaṇṇē niṉṟu muṟṟic cācārakaṟpattut tēvarāyiṉpuṟutalum, innūlik kēṭpatāl varum payaṉ kūṟuvatum, payaṉkoḷvār ceyyattakuvaṉa iviyeṉpatum piṟavum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
yacōmati vēṭṭimēṟ cēṟal
262.	nūṟpaṭuva lippoṟimu taṟkaruvi nūṟṟō
	ṭēṟṟuṭiye yiṟṟuñama likkulamirippa
	nāṟpaṭinaṭukkaṭalna ṭucceynama ṉēpōl
	vēṟpaṭipi ṭittaracaṉ vēṭṭiyiṉvi rintāṉ.
uri:- nūṟpaṭu valippoṟi mutal - nūlāṟpiṉṉappaṭṭa valiyākiya poṟi mutalākavuḷḷa, karuvi nūṟṟōṭu - karuvikaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ, ēṟṟuṭi eyiṟṟu ñamalikkulam - ēṟṟamikka paṟkaḷi yuṭiya nāykkūṭṭam, irippa - kurippa, nāṟpaṭik kaṭalnaṭu - nāl vakip paṭikaḷākiya kaṭaliṉ naṭuvē, naṭuccey namaṉēpōl - uyirkkum uṭaṟkum naṭuniṉṟu uyiri uṭaliṉiṉṟum pirikkum namaṉippōla, aracaṉ - aracaṉākiya yacōmati, vēṟpaṭi piṭittu - vēṟpaṭiyik kiyilēntik koṇṭu, vēṭṭiyiṉ virintāṉ - vēṭṭikku virintu celvāṉāyiṉāṉ  e-ṟu.
poṟipōla uyirkaḷi yakappaṭutaliṉ, valiyip poṟi yeṉṟār; irupeyaroṭṭu. nūṟu, palaveṉṉum poruṭṭu; “nūṟṟitaḻttāmari”1 pōla. ēṟṟuṭi yeṉpuḻi, ēṟṟam eṉpatu ammuk kuṟintu niṉṟatu. ēṟṟam, nīṭci. paṭi naṭuviruntu namaṉ porutaḻiyum uyirkaḷiyuṇpatupōla, vīrar naṭuvaṇiruntu kāṉat tuyirkaḷik koṉṟu kaḻittalpaṟṟi yacōmatiyi “namaṉē pōl” eṉṟār. “paṭikkaṭal naṭuvaṇ, iruntu kūṟṟuyiruṇac ceyu miruñ camar” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. vēntaṟkuc ciṟappuṭiya paṭiyātaliṉ, vēṟpaṭiyi yeṭuttu moḻintār. virintāṉ eṉṟatu, uyirkaḷik kōṟaṟkaṇ yacōmatik kirunta vēṭki mikutiyi yuṇartti niṉṟatu.
cutatta muṉivaṉ tavam ceytiruttal
263.	itattiṉi yuyirkkiṉita ḷittiṭumi yaṟkic
	cutattamuṉi tottiruvi ṉittukaḷtu ṭikkum*
	matattayaṉma takkaḷiṟe ṉappaṭima niṟpak
	katattiṉaṉi ḻittaṭu†ka ṭattiṭima ṭuttāṉ.
uri:- uyirkku - ellāvuyirkaṭkum, itattiṉi iṉitu aḷittiṭum iyaṟki - nalatti mikacceyyum iyalpiṉi yuṭiya, cutatta muṉi - cutattaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉākiya, tottu iruviṉittukaḷ - uyirippaṟṟi niṟkum iruviṉikaḷākiya māciṉi, tuṭikkum - pōkkum. patattu ayaṉ - tiruvaṭiyi yuṭiya mēlōṉuṭiya, paṭimam - tiruvuruvam, matakkaḷiṟu eṉa niṟpa - matam paṭṭa kaḷiṟṟiṇip pōlac calippuṇarātu niṟkakkaṇṭu, katattiṉaṉ - kōpatti yuṭiyaṉākiya aracaṉ, iḻittu - ikaḻntu nōkki, avaṉirunta, aṭukaṭattiṭi - tāṉ uyirkaḷik kollak karuti vanta kāṭṭiṭatti, maṭuttāṉ - vantaṭintāṉ e-ṟu.
aracaṉ uyirkaḷik kollum karuttiṉaṉāy vantāṉeṉ pati muṭittaṟku, muṉivaṉatu uyirkkaruḷum iyaṟkiyi eṭuttu vitantār. tottutal - paṟṟi niṟṟal. nalviṉiyum tuṉpamuṭi viṟṟātal paṟṟi, “iruviṉittukaḷ” eṉṟār; “iruḷ cēr iruviṉiyum cērā”1 eṉṟāṟpōla. tukaḷeṉavē, tuṭittal viṉi kūṟiṉār. aiyaṉ eṉpatu ayaṉ eṉa vikāram. tavattāl poṟipulaṉkaḷi yaṭakkiyiruttal paṟṟi, muṉivaṉ tiruvuruvip paṭimam eṉṟār. calippiṉmikku matakkaḷiṟu uvamamāyiṟṟu; iṉi ataṉi aracaṉmē lēṟṟalu moṉṟu. kaṭam - kāṭu. veḷḷam pōlat tāṉi puṭi tiraṇṭu varutaliṉ, maṭuttāṉ eṉṟār.
vēntaṉ cutatta muṉivaṉi vekuḷutal
264.	kūṟṟameṉa aṭavipuṭi taṭaviyuyir kōṟaṟ
	kēṟṟapaṭi  peṟṟatila ṉiṟṟiviṉi muṟṟum
	pāṟṟiyava ṉiṉṉuyirpa ṟippaleṉa vantāṉ
	māṟṟariya cīṟṟamoṭu mātavaṉiṉ mēlē.
uri:- aṭavi - ikkāṭṭil, puṭitaṭavi - iṭameṅkum cuṟṟiyum, kūṟṟameṉak kōṟaṟku ēṟṟapaṭi - namaṉ pōlak kollutaṟ kēṟṟavakiyil, peṟṟatu ilaṉ - uyiroṉṟum peṟṟēṉilli. ivaṟṟi viṉi muṟṟum pāṟṟiyavaṉ - iṉṟu eṉatu vēṭṭi viṉi muḻutum payaṉiṉṟik keṭacceyta im muṉivaṉuṭiya, iṉṉuyir paṟippal - iṉiya uyirip pōkkuvēṉ, eṉa - eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭu, māṟṟariya cīṟṟamoṭu - nīkka muṭiyāta kōpattuṭaṉē, mātavaṉiṉ mēlē - cutatta muṉivaṉ mēl, vantāṉ - maṇṭivaralāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
mēlēyum “paṭikkaṭal naṭuccel namaṉē pōl” (262) eṉṟārākaliṉ, īṇṭum avviyalpu tiriyāmiyiṉ “kūṟṟameṉa” eṉṟār. ōriṭamum viṭātu tēṭiṉami tōṉṟa, “aṭavipuṭi taṭavi” yeṉṟār. ēṟṟapaṭiyāvatu, palvaki uyirkaḷiyum koṉṟu kuvippēṉeṉak karuttuṭkoṇṭu pōnta mēṟkōḷ. oruyirum taṉakku akappaṭātoḻintataṟkuk kāraṇam ammuṉivaṉatu iruppeṉṟu niṉittaliṉ, muṉivaṉi, “iṟṟi viṉi muṟṟum pāṟṟiyavaṉ” eṉṟum, atu tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa viṉikku iṭiyūṟāvatu niṉintu vekuḷi mikak kōṭaliṉ, “iṉṉuyir paṟippal” eṉṟum kūṟalāṉāṉ. muṉivaṉatu tavamum taṉ ceyaliṉatu avamum ciṟitu eṇṇiya vaḻiyum, uṇṭākiya ciṉam taṇintu paṇivikkum eṉpār, “māṟṟariya cīṟṟamoṭu mātavaṉiṉ mēlē” eṉa vaṟpuṟuttiṉār.
aiññūnūṟu nāykaḷi ammuṉivaṉ mēlēvutal
265.	konteri yumiḻntetir kurittatirva kōṇāy
	aintiṉoṭu porutatoki yimpati ṉiraṭṭi
	centacikaḷ ceṉṟukavar kiṉṟeṉa*vi ṭuttāṉ
	nantiyaruḷ maḻipoḻiyum nātaṉavaṉ mēlē.
uri:- kontu eri umiḻntu - kottākat tīyiṉik kakki, etir kurittu - etirē kurittukkoṇṭu, atirva - ēṉiyuyir kaṭku naṭukkattip paṇṇuvaṉavākiya, kōḷ nāy - koli puriyum nāykkūṭṭam, aintiṉoṭu poruta toki aimpatiṉ iraṭṭi - aintāṟ perukkiya nūṟākavarum aiññūṟum, iṉṟu - ippoḻutē, ceṉṟu - muṉivaṉi yaṇukippaṟṟi, centacikaḷ kavarka eṉa - (avaṉuṭiya) cevviya tacikaḷik kavarka eṉṟu, aruḷ maḻi nanti - aruḷi maḻipōla mikuttu, poḻiyum nātaṉ avaṉmēl - poḻiyum tavamākiya celvattiyuṭiya ammuṉivaṉmēl, viṭuttāṉ - celuttiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
vēṭṭi nāykaḷiṉ iyalpu kūṟuvār, tīppaṟakkum kaṇṇum, ēṉiyuyirkaḷi naṭuṅkuvikkum kurippum, koli vaṉmiyum uṭiyaveṉṟār. atirtal - naṭukkam; īṇṭup piṟaviṉip poruṭṭu. aimpatiṉ iraṭṭi toki - aimpatiṉ iraṭṭippākiya toki; nūṟeṉpatām. aḵtu aintoṭu uṟaḻntavaḻi aiññūṟāyiṟṟu. tavattāl muṉivaṉ mēṉi poṉpōl oḷiviṭṭut tikaḻvatu kaṇṭu, “centacikaḷ” eṉṟum, nāykaḷi ēvutal tōṉṟa, iṉṟu kavarka eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ, ataṉik kaṇṭuvittum, cutatta muṉivaṉ veṟuppō viruppō koḷḷātu aruṇmaṇaṅ kamaḻa viruntami viḷaṅka, “nanti yaruṇ maḻi poḻiyum nātaṉavaṉ mēlē” eṉṟu vitantār. nātaṉ eṉṉum vaṭacoṟcitivu celva muṭiyōṉ eṉṉum poruṭṭu. makāmakōpāttiyāya paṇṭita maṇiyavarkaḷ, “nātaṉṟāḷ vāḻka” eṉpataṟ keḻutiya urikāṇka.
nāykkaṇam mēṟcellāmikaṇṭu 
aracaṉ vāḷuruvutal
266.	aṟapperumi ceytaruḷta vapperumi taṉṉāl
	uṟappuṇarta lañciyoru viṟkaṇavi niṟpak
	kaṟuppuṭimaṉatteḻuka tattaraca ṉiyō
	maṟappaṭivi ṭakkaruti vāḷuruvu kiṉṟāṉ.
uri:- aṟapperumi taṉṉāl - mēṟ koṇṭirukkum ciṉa tarumattiṉ perumiyālum, ceytu aruḷ tavapperumi taṉṉāl - uyirkaṭku nalam ceytaruḷum tavattiṉ perumiyālum, uṟap puṇartal añci - muṉivaṉuṭiya meyyuṟa aṇukuvataṟ kañci, oruviṟkaṇ - oru villaḷavu tolivilēyē, avi niṟpa - anta nāykkūṭṭam niṉṟoḻiyavē, kaṟuppuṭi maṉattu - eppōtum vekuḷiyē kuṭikoṇṭirukkum maṉattiṉkaṇ, eḻukatattu aracaṉ - eḻunta ciṉamikutiyiyuṭiya aracaṉākiya yacōmati, maṟappaṭi viṭak karuti - koliviṉikkuriya paṭiyic celutta niṉintu, vāḷ uruvukiṉṟāṉ - uṟiyili rukkum vāḷiyuruvuvāṉāyiṉāṉ, aiyō - ivaṉ ceyaliruntavāṟu eṉṉō e-ṟu.
taṉṉāl eṉpati aṟapperumikkum kūṭṭuka. aracaṉuṇa rāṉāyiṉum, avaṉ viṭutta nāykkūṭṭam muṉivaṉuṭiya aṟap perumi tavapperumikaḷit teḷiya vuṇarntu koṇṭaṉa eṉpār, “uṟappuṇartal añci” yeṉṟum, avvuṇarviṉ payaṉ ōraḷavu vilakiniṉṟu vaḻipaṭuvatākaliṉ, “oruviṟ kaṇ avi niṟpa” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. avaṉuṇarāmikku ētu avaṉ maṉaviyalpu eṉpār, “kaṟuppuṭi maṉattu” eṉṟum, ataṉ payaṉāka, avaṉ ciṉam mikuntu vāḷiyuruvaluṟṟāṉ eṉpār, “eḻukatattaracaṉ maṟappaṭi viṭakkaruti vāḷuruvukiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāreṉavuṇarka. kolik kurittātaliṉ, vāḷ, “maṟappaṭi” yeṉappaṭṭatu. aiyō, nūlāciriyar iraṅkik kūṟiyatu.
kaliyāṇamittiraṉ aracaṉit taṭuttal
267.	kāḷitaku kaliyāṇa mittiraṉe ṉumpēr
	āḷiyaṭu tiṟalvaṇika ṉaracaṉuyi raṉiya
	kēḷoruvaṉ vantiṭipu kuntaraca keṭṭēṉ
	vāḷuruvu kiṉṟateṉi mātavaṉmu ṉeṉṟāṉ.
uri:- kāḷi taku - kāḷipparuvam takkirukkiṉṟa, kaliyāṇa mittiraṉ eṉum pēr - kaliyāṇamittiraṉ eṉṉum peyariyuṭiya, āḷi aṭutiṟal vaṇikaṉ - ciṅkatti vellum meyvaliyiṉi yuṭiya vaṇikakumaraṉum, aracaṉ - vēntaṉākiya yacōmatikku, uyir aṉiya kēḷ oruvaṉ - uyirotta naṇpaṉumākiya oruvaṉ, vantu - avviṭattiṟku vantu, iṭi pukuntu - vāḷuruvi vīcakkarutum  aracaṉit taṭuttu, araca - vēntē, mātavaṉ muṉ - immāmuṉikaḷiṉ tirumuṉṉē, vāḷ uruvukiṉṟatu eṉi - tīṅku kuṟittu vāṭpaṭi yeṭuppatu eṉṉiyō e-ṟu.
keṭṭēṉ -  irakkakkuṟippu. kaliyāṇamittiraṉuṭiya mati nuṭpamum, mey vaṉmiyum aracaṉpāl avaṟkuḷḷa toṭarpum, viḷakkutaṟku, “kāḷi” yeṉṟum, “āḷiyaṭutiṟal vaṇikaṉ” eṉṟum, “aracaṉ uyiraṉiya kēḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. oppaṟṟa tuṇivaṉ  eṉpār “oruvaṉ” eṉṟār; mikacciṟantāṉ eṉpatu karuttu; iṉṟēl, iṭipukuntu vilakkutal iyalāteṉavuṇarka. ac ceyalāl viḷiyum kēṭṭiṉi aṟintu pōntu vilakkukiṉṟāṉā taliṉ, iṭi pukuntu “keṭṭēṉ”  eṉṟu colleṭuttu, “vāḷuruvukiṉṟa teṉi mātavaṉ muṉ” eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. māvataṉ eṉṟatu, nī karutiyavāṟu muṭikka voṇṇātu taṭukkum perumiyum, nī karutum karuttu niṉṉiyēyaṭivikkum tavavaṉmiyum uṭiyaṉ eṉpatum karuttu viḷaṅkak kūṟiyavāṟām.
kaliyāṇamittiraṉ muṉivaṉ ciṟappeṭut turittal
268.	veṟuttuṭaṉ viṭuttaraci ṉittukaḷe ṉappēr
	aṟapperu malippoṟi yeṭuttavaṉa ṭikkaṇ
	ciṟappiṉiyi yaṟṟali*ci ṉatterima ṉattāṉ
	maṟappaṭiye ṭuppatevaṉ† mālimaṟa vēlōy.
uri:- māli maṟavēlōy - māli yaṇinta maṟam poruntiya vēli yēntiya vēntē. araciṉi - aracapōkatti, tukaḷ eṉa veṟuttu - aṟpap poruḷākak karutip paṟṟaṟaveṟuttu, uṭaṉ viṭuttu - avveṟupput tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉē oḻittuviṭṭu, pēr aṟapperumalippoṟi yeṭuttavaṉ - tuṟavaṟamākiya periya maliyi eṭuttut tāṅkiyirukkiṉṟa muṉivaṉuṭiya tiruvaṭik kaṇṇē, ciṟappiṉi iyaṟṟali - ciṟappākiya vaḻipāṭṭic ceyyāmal, ciṉattu eri maṉattāl - ciṉattāl vetumpukiṉṟa maṉattōṭu, maṟappaṭi - vāṭpaṭiyi, eṭuppatu evaṉ - kiyil eṭuppatu eṟṟukku, urippāyāka e-ṟu.
vēlukku māli yaṇivatum marapātaliṉ, “māli maṟa vēlōy” eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu; itaṉi, “ivvē (ivvēṟ paṭikaḷ), pīliyaṇintu māli cūṭṭik kaṇṭiraṇōṉ kāḻtirutti neyyaṇintu kaṭiyuṭi viyaṉakaravvē”1 eṉpataṉālu maṟika. viḻumitu terinta viḻumiyōrkku, “noytā lamma tāṉē miyaṟṟu vicumpuṟa vōṅkiya veṇkuṭi, muracu keḻu vēntar aracu keḻu tiruvē”2 eṉṟu cāṉṟōrum kūṟutal kāṇka. tuṟavaṟattiṉ perumi yum poṟuttaṟ karumiyum tōṉṟa, “pēraṟapperumali” yeṉṟum, ataṉi mēṟkoṇ ṭoḻukum āṇmi nalam viḷaṅkap “poṟi yeṭuttavaṉ” eṉṟum, attakiyōrik kaṇṭaviṭattu aracarāyiṉum vaḻipaṭuvatē uṭaṉ ceyaṟpāla teṉpāṉ, “ciṟap piṉi yiyaṟṟali” yeṉṟum, nī ceyvatutītu payappatām eṉṟa kuṟippāl “maṟappaṭi yeṭuppa tevaṉ” eṉṟum vaṇikaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ.
269.	ākaveṉiṉ ākumivar aḻikaveṉiṉ aḻiyum*
	mēkamivaṇ varukaveṉiṉ varumatuvum vitiyiṉ
	ēkamaṉa rāmmuṉivar perumiyitu vākum
	mākamaḻi vaṇkimata yāṉimaṇi mārpōy†
uri:- māka maḻi vaṇki - vāṉattil ulāvum maḻi mēkam pōlum vaḷḷaṉmi poruntiya kiyiṉiyum, matayāṉi - matam poruntiya yāṉikaḷiyum, maṇi mārpōy - maṇiyiḻitta mārpaṇiyu muṭiya vēntē, ivar - immuṉivar, āka eṉiṉ ākum - oruporuḷ ākkam peṟuka eṉpārāyiṉ avvāṟē ciṟitum piḻiyiṉṟi ākkameytum, aḻika eṉiṉ aḻiyum - kēṭeytuka eṉṟāl avvāṟē akkaṇamē apporuḷ keṭum, ivaṇ mēkam varuka eṉiṉ - ippoḻutu ivviṭattē maḻi peyka eṉṟāl, vitiyiṉ - avar vitikkumāṟē, atuvum varum - ammēkamum vantu maḻi poḻiyum, ēkam maṉarām muṉivar perumi ituvākum - oru neṟiyil piṟaḻviṉṟi niṟkum maṉamuṭiyarākiya im muṉivaratu perumi it taṉmittākum, itaṉi nī niṉiyātoḻukuvatu kūṭātu e-ṟu.
itaṉi nī eṉpatu mutaliyaṉa kuṟippeccam. “yāṉiyuṭiya paṭi kāṇṭal iṉi” teṉpavākaliṉ, nāṟpaṭiyiṉum mata yāṉippaṭiyi vitantōtiṉāṉ. maḻi vaṇki poruṭ perumiyum, matayāṉi paṭipperumiyum cuṭṭi niṉṟaṉa. ipperumikaḷ ātaṟkum aḻitaṟku muriyavāvaṉa; avvākka vaḻivukaḷ iraṇṭaṉiyum niṉitta poḻutē ceyyavallareṉ pāṉ, “akaveṉiṉ...... varumatuvum vitiyiṉ” eṉṟāṉ. poṟivaḻi yōṭippaḵṟalip paṭṭalitaliṉṟi oru neṟiyē niṟkum ciṟappuk kuṟittu, “ēkamaṉarāmuṉivar” eṉṟāṉ. muṉivar perumi itu veṉavē, aracar perumi poruḷ paṭi yeṉṟa iraṇṭāy vakutturikkappaṭṭatu. aracarkkuriya mūvakiyāṟṟaluḷ, perumi, poruḷ paṭi eṉṟa iraṇṭiṉmēl niṟkumeṉpatu araciyal neṟi.
270.	aṭintavarkaḷ kātaliṉo ṭamararara cāvar*
	kaṭantavarkaḷ tamatikaḻviṉ kaṭinarakiṉ vīḻvar
	aṭintaniḻal pōlaruḷu muṉivumila raṭikaḷ
	kaṭantativa ṇulakiyalpu kaṭavuḷavar ceyalē.
uri:- aṭikaḷ - aracē, kātaliṉoṭu aṭintavarkaḷ - uḷḷaṉpuṭaṉ immuṉivar tiruvaṭi yaṭintavarkaḷ, amarar aracu āvar - tēvarkkaracarākiya intirarākuvar, tamatu ikaḻviṉ kaṭantavar - tamatu mayakkavuṇarvāl aṟattiṉ varampu kaṭanta ceyali ivarpāṟ ceypavar, kaṭinarakiṉ vīḻvar - ellā narakiṉum kīḻppaṭṭa narakattiyaṭivar. aṭinta niḻalpōl - taṉṉi yaṭintavarkkut taṭpam payantu vikāramiṉṟiyirukkum niḻal pōla, aruḷum muṉivum ilar - yāvarpālum viruppum veṟuppum ilarāvar, kaṭantatu ivaṇ ulakiyalpu. ivarkaḷ tuṟantatu immikkaṇ ivvulaka pōkattiṟ cellum viruppēyākum. kaṭavuḷavar ceyal - mēṟkoṇṭatu muṉivarkkuriya oṉṟaṉi vēṇṭalum vēṇṭāmiyum illāta ceyalē e-ṟu. 
tammi yaṭintārkku aṟamuṇartti avari naṉṉeṟik kaṇ uyttaliṉ, avar anneṟiyāl amarar paravum ciṟappeytuvatu kuṟittu, “aṭintavarkaḷ.... amarararacāvar” eṉṟum ivarpāl mikka ceyal ceytu piḻippōr narakil vīḻvatu kūṟi, aracaṉuḷḷatti neṟippaṭuttak karuti “kaṭantavarkaḷ....narakiṉ vīḻvar” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. kaṭattal, muṉṉatu aṟavarampu kaṭantu mikkaceyal ceytal; piṉṉatu tuṟattal. ikaḻtaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya cerukkum aṟiyāmiyum mayakkuṇarvum ellā meyta ikaḻviṉ eṉak kāriyattāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ. aṭintār amarararacātaṟkum, kaṭantār narakil vīḻtaṟkum kāraṇarātaliṉ, ivar viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ uḷapōlum eṉṉum aiyamaṟuttaṟku, “aruḷum muṉivum ilar” eṉṟum, avi ulakiyalāyp piṟavikkētuvātal paṟṟit tuṟakkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpāṉ “kaṭantati vaṇ ulakiyalpu” eṉṟum, mēṟkoṇṭatu viruppu veṟuppaṟṟa maṉappāṉmi yeṉṟaṟkuk “kaṭavuḷavar ceyalē” yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. vēṇṭu talum vēṇṭāmiyum iṉmi kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyātaliṉ, “kaṭavuḷavar ceya”lāyiṟṟu.
271.	intirarkaḷ vantaṭipa ṇintaruḷu keṉiṉum
	cintiyilar ventuyarka ḷeṇṇilakaḷ ceyiṉum*
	tantamviṉi yeṉṟunamar piṟareṉavu niṉiyār
	antarami kantaruḷta vattaracar tārōy.
uri:- tārōy - māli yaṇinta aracē, intirarkaḷ vantu - tēvarulaka vēntarkaḷākiya intirar tāmē pōntu, aṭipaṇintu - tam tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅki, aruḷuka eṉiṉum - aruḷ ceyka eṉṟu vēṇṭip paraviṉālum, cintiyilar - tam ceyaliṉ viḷivi yōrāmal, ventuyarkaḷ eṇṇilakaḷ ceyiṉum - koṭiya tīviṉikaḷ aḷaviṟantaṉa ceytālum, tantam viṉiyeṉṟum - iruvakiyālum varum iṉpattuṉpaṅkaṭkuk kāraṇam tāmtām ceyta viṉiyē eṉṟu niṉittum, namar piṟar eṉavum - iṉpam ceytāri namakkuriyār eṉṟu uvattalum tuṉpañceytārip pakivareṉa veṟuttalum, niṉiyār - niṉiyāmalum, antaram ikantu - viṇṇulakattē peṟum iṉpatti veṟuttu, aruḷ tavattaracar - aruḷum, tavamum nalkum ñāṉaviṉpatti nukarn toḻukum tava vēntarāvar e-ṟu.
mēlē “aruḷum muṉivumilar” eṉṟu kūṟiyataṉi viḷakku mukattāl intirar parāvalum, ēṉiyōr tīmiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi avviruviṉikaḷiyum immuṉivar karutum tiṟam itu veṉpāṉ “tantam viṉiyeṉṟu” niṉippar eṉṟum, “namarpiṟar eṉa niṉi yār” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. itu “ceñcān teṟiyiṉum cettiṉum pōḻiṉum neñ cōrntu nāṭā nilimi” eṉpatu. cintittavaḻit tām ceyyum ceyaliṉ viḷivu pulaṉāmātaliṉ, “cintiyilar” eṉṟāṉ. antaram - ākupeyar. tavattukku aruḷ uruvātaliṉ, “aruḷ tavattaracar” eṉpa. ivar “antaramikantaruḷ tavattaracar” eṉṟatu tammaṭi paṇivār peṟum avviṇṇiṉ pattik tām poruḷāka matiyāmi yuṇaraniṉṟatu.
muṉivaṉip paṇika eṉa vaṇikaṉ kūṟal
272.	ivvulaki ṉevvuyiru memmuyiri ṉēreṉ
	ṟavviyama kaṉṟaruḷcu rantuyirvaḷarkkum
	cevvimiyiṉ niṉṟavarti runtaṭipa ṭintuṉ
	vevviṉikaḷ tantaṉivi ḷaṅku*viṟal vēlōy
uri:- viṟal vēlōy - viṟal poruntiya vēliyuṭiyāy, ivvulakil - ivvulakattil, evvuyirum em uyiriṉ nēr eṉṟu - ettakiyavuyirum emmuṭiya uyiriyē nikarkkum eṉṟu karuti, avviyam akaṉṟu - avaṟṟip puṟakkaṇikkum mayakkamiṉṟi, aruḷ curantu - aruḷ purintu, uyir vaḷarkkum cevvimiyiṉ - avvuyirkaḷip purakkum cemmi neṟiyiṉkaṇ, niṉṟavar - niṟkiṉṟa immēlōruṭiya, tiruntaṭi paṭintu - aḻakiya caraṇaṅkaḷilē mūḻki, uṉ vevviṉi kaṭantaṉi - uṉṉuṭiya tīviṉiyākiya aḻukkiṉip pōkki, viḷaṅku - cīvacuttiyiṉi eytuvāyāka e-ṟu.
virintatu tokuttal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi, mēlē viriyak kūṟivantavaṟṟik tokuttu, maṉṉuyirit taṉṉuyireṉak karututalum, avaṟṟiṟku uṟukaṇ ceyyātu aruḷ purivatum, avaṟṟiṟku aṟamuṇarttic cemmi neṟikkaṇ niṉṟoḻuku vittalum immuṉivarar kaṭaṉ eṉṟāṉāyiṟṟu. ittakiya periyōr tiruvaṭiyil maṉamoṉṟi vaḻipāṭāṟṟiyavaḻi niṉakkum avviyalpē yuṇṭā meṉṟum, atuvē vāyilāka nī uyirkkoli tavirntu avaṟṟi vaḷarkkum uyar ceyali mēṟkoṇṭu, pēriṉ pattukkuriya uyirttūymi (cīvacutti) eytuvāy eṉṟum moḻivāṉ, “tiruntaṭi paṭintu uṉ vevviṉi kaṭantaṉi viḷaṅku” eṉṟāṉ. tiruvaṭi aruṭkaṭalātaliṉ, ataṉi vaṇaṅku talippaṭitaleṉṟāṉ. piṟāṇṭum “veṉṟavar caraṇamūḻki” eṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. viṉimācu nīṅkiyavaḻi uyir tūymi peṟuvatu kuṟittu, “kaṭantaṉi viḷaṅku” eṉṟāṉ. kaṭantaṉi - muṟṟeccam.
maṉṉaṉ muṉivaṉi yāvaṉeṉa aṟital
273.	eṉṟiṉitu kūṟumvaṇi kaṉcolika ḻātē
	kaṉṟuciṉa muṅkarata lappaṭiyu māṟṟi
	iṉṟivaṉi yeṉṉitoḻu māṟaṉiyaṉ yāvaṉ
	kaṉṟutukaḷ tuṉṟukaru mēṉiyiṉa ṉeṉṟāṉ.
uri:- eṉṟu - eṉpatāka, vaṇikaṉ iṉitu kūṟum col - kaliyāṇamittiraṉākiya vaṇikaṉ iṉitākak kūṟiya colli, ikaḻātu - nekiḻātu mēṟkoṇṭu, kaṉṟu ciṉamum - mikkuniṉṟa vekuḷiyiṉiyum, karatalappaṭiyum - kiyil uruvippiṭittirunta vāṭ paṭiyiṉiyum, māṟṟi - muṟiyē taṉittum uṟiyiṟ cerukiyum, iṉṟu ivaṉi toḻumāṟu eṉṉi - ippōtu ivaṉit toḻum vakiyātu, kaṉṟu tukaḷ tuṉṟu karumēṉiyiṉaṉ - mikka puḻuti paṭintu karuttamēṉiyiṉi yuṭiyaṉākiya, aḷiyaṉ - aḷikkattakka ivaṉ, yāvaṉ - yāvaṉākum, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu yacōmati avvaṇikaṉi viṉaviṉāṉ e-ṟu.
kaṉṟu ciṉamum karatalattiṟ paṭiyumēntit tītu kuṟitta cintiyaṉāyirunta vēntaṉ maṉattil aṉpum muṉivaṉpāl naṉmatippum uṇṭāmāṟu pēciyatu kuṟittu, “iṉitu kūṟum vaṇikaṉ col” eṉṟum, muṉivaṉpāl aṟaṅkēṭṭaṟkuriya nallūḻ piṉṉēyiruntu ūkkutalāl, “ikaḻātu’ eṉṟum, kūṟiṉār. ciṉatti māṟṟutalāvatu taṇivittal; vāṭpaṭiyi māṟṟutal, maṟuvalum uṟiyiṟ cerukikkōṭal. cutatta muṉivaṉatu mēṉiyiṉik kaṇṭu aruvaruttu niṉṟami tōṉṟa, “kaṉṟu tukaḷ tuṉṟu karumēṉiyaṉ” eṉṟum, “aḷiyaṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. mēṉiyiṉ tōṟṟattāl toḻutaṟkut taṉ uḷḷam cellāmiyik kuṟippāṟ kāṭṭuvāṉāy, “iṉṟi vaṉi eṉṉi toḻumāṟu” eṉkiṉṟāṉ. kaṉṟal - mikutal.
vaṇikaṉ muṉivaṉatu varalāṟu kūṟal
274.	iṅkulaku toḻumuṉiyi yāvaṉeṉi ṉitukēḷ
	kaṅkikula tilakaṉivaṉ kaliṅkapati yataṉip
	poṅkupuya valiyil potu viṉṟimuḻu tāṇṭa
	ciṅkamiva ṉeṉṟuteḷi tēntuṇariṉ vēntē*
uri:- tēntuṇariṉ vēntē - tēṉ porunti pūṅkottuk kaḷāliyaṉṟa māli yaṇinta aracē, iṅkē - iṅkē eḻuntaruḷi yirukkiṉṟa, ulaku toḻu muṉiyi - aṟivuṭiyōr paravum ic cutattamuṉivaṉi, yāvaṉ eṉiṉ - yāvaṉ eṉṟu kēṭkiṉṟāyāyiṉ, itukēḷ - yāṉ kūṟappōku mitaṉik kēṭpāyāka, ivaṉ kaṅki kulatilakaṉ - ivaṉ kaṅki kulattiṟpiṟanta mēṉmi yuṭiyaṉ, kaliṅkapati - kaliṅka nāṭṭiṟku aracaṉ, ataṉi - ak kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉi, poṅku puyavaliyil - uyarnta taṉ tōḷ vaṉmiyāl, ivaṉ potuviṉṟi muḻutu āṇṭa ciṅkam - ivaṉ potuccoṟ poṟātu nāṭu muḻutum tāṉē yāṭci purinta ciṅkampōlum āṟṟaluṭi yaṉāvāṉ, eṉṟu teḷi - eṉṟu teḷivāy; aṟivāyāka e-ṟu.
tuṇar pūṅkottu; ākupeyar, ulaku, uyarntōr mēṟṟātaliṉ, uyarntōrāl toḻappaṭum muṉivaṉi, “ulaku toḻu muṉiyi” eṉṟāṉ. tilakaṉ - mēlāṉavaṉ. nilavulaku aracarellārkkum potu veṉṉuñ collik kēṭkap poṟātu valiyuṭiya taṉakkē uriyatu eṉṉum ūkkamum uṭkōḷum uṭiyaṉātal tōṉṟa, “potuviṉṟi muḻutāṇṭa” eṉṟum, pakiyaracarkku accatti viḷittalāl “ciṅkam” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. mēṉiyiṉ niṟamum tavakkōlamum piṟavum kaṇṭu kaṉṟiya ciṉamum kiyil vāṭpaṭiyum ēnti maṉam maruṇṭuniṉṟa yacōmatikku aṟivuṟuttu kiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, “ciṅkamivaṉ eṉṟu teḷi” eṉṟu vaṇikaṉ ceppiṉāṉ. aracaṉpāl viyappu meyppaṭavē, kaṇṭa vaṇikaḷ mēlum cila kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
275.	mēkameṉa miṉṉumeṉa* villumeṉa vallē
	pōkamoṭu poruḷiḷami poṉṟunaṉi yeṉṟē
	ākatuṟa varuḷperuku maṟaṉoṭata ṉiyalē†
	pōkamiku poṉṉulaku pukuvaṉeṉa niṉivāṉ.
uri:- mēkameṉa miṉṉumeṉa villumeṉa - mēkamum miṉṉum intiravillumpōla, pōkamoṭu poruḷ iḷami - muṟiyē pōkamum poruḷum iḷamiyum, vallē naṉi poṉṟum eṉṟu - viriya mikakkeṭum eṉṟu, āka - teḷiya vuṇarntāṉāka, tuṟavu - tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭu, aruḷ perukum aṟaṉoṭu - aruḷ mikutaṟkuriya nallaṟattōṭu, ataṉ iyal - ataṉāl iyalum payaṉākiya, pōka miku poṉṉulaku - iṉpam mikuvikkum tuṟakka vulaki, pukuvaṉ eṉa niṉivāṉ - aṭivēṉ eṉṟu akkaliṅkapati niṉivāṉāyiṉaṉ e-ṟu.
mēkam mutaliya mūṉṟum muṟiyē pōkam mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum uvami. āmiṭattu viriyavākātu meṉmelac ciṟitu ciṟitāy ātalum keṭumiṭattu mika virivil iṟappavum mikutiyākak keṭutalum ippōkam mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum iyalpātaliṉ, “vallē naṉi poṉṟum” eṉṟu kaṇṭāṉ. avvāṟu kaṇṭāṟkut tuṟavē tuṇiporuḷāyt tōṉṟiya teṉpār, “eṉṟēyāka” eṉṟum “tuṟavu niṉivāṉ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. aruḷi vaḷarkkum nallaṟam ciṉa tarumameṉ ṟuṇarntu ataṉik kaṭippiṭittami tōṉṟa, “aruḷ peruku maṟaṉoṭu” eṉṟum, ataṉi mēṟkoṇṭoḻukiya vaḻi yeytum payaṉ tuṟakkaviṉpam eṉṟaṟku, “ataṉ iyalē pōkamiku poṉṉulaku” eṉṟum niṉittāṉ. iyalum payaṉi iyaleṉṟē yoḻintār; vaḻakkiṉ payaṉi “aṉpuṟṟamarnta vaḻakkeṉpa”1 eṉṟāṟpōla. poṉṉulaku niṉitaṟ kētu īteṉ pār, “pōkamiku poṉṉulaku” eṉṟāreṉka.
276.	nāṭunaka raṅkaḷuna laṅkoḷmaṭa vārum
	āṭukoṭi yāṉiatir tēr*puravi kālāḷ
	cūṭumuṭi mālikuḻi tōḷvaḷiyo ṭāram
	āṭimuta lāyiṉavo ṭakalkeṉa viṭṭāṉ.
uri:- nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷum - nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷiyum, nalam koḷ maṭavārum - aḻakukoṇṭa makaḷirkaḷiyum, āṭu koṭi yāṉi yoṭu - acikiṉṟa koṭiyēntiya yāṉippaṭiyiṉiyum, atir - pakivarkku naṭukkattic ceyyum, tēr puravi kālāḷoṭu - tērppaṭi, kutirippaṭi, kālāṭpaṭikaḷiyum, cūṭu muṭi māli yoṭu - tāṉ aṇikiṉṟa muṭiyiyum māliyiyum, kuḻi tōḷ vaḷiyoṭu āram - kātilaṇiyum kuḻiyiṉiyum tōḷilaṇiyum vaḷikaḷiyum mārpilaṇiyum muttumāli yiṉiyum, āṭimutalā yiṉavoṭu - paṭṭiṉum paruttiyiṉu miyaṉṟa uyariya uṭi mutaliya vaṟṟiyum, akalka eṉa viṭṭāṉ - nīkkumiṉ eṉat tuṟantoḻittāṉ e-ṟu.
nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷum eṉpati ummittokiyākkātu viṉit tokiyākki ulakamuḻutum tiraṇṭu oruṅkuvariṉum vaḻaṅkat tavā vaḷamuṭimiyiṉ ēṉi nāṭṭavarum nakarattavarum virumpum nakaraṅkaḷum eṉṟurittalumoṉṟu, nalam, iṉpa nukarccikku vēṇṭum peṇmi nalam. maṭavār, iḷiyamakaḷir,. tōḷvaḷi, vākuvalayam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. oṭuvum ummiyum eṇṇup poruṭṭu. oṭuveṉpatu ēṉiyiṭattum kūṭṭappaṭṭatu, “eṉṟum eṉavum oṭuvum tōṉṟi, oṉṟuvaḻi yuṭiya eṇṇiṉuṭ pirintē” eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. tuṟavuḷḷam muṟuki niṟṟaliṉ, māli mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇarntāri vēṇṭāveṉa vilakkiṉāṉ eṉka. viṭutal - paṟṟu viṭutal. mutalāyiṉa eṉpuḻi eytutaṟkuriya cāriyi vikārattāl niliyātāyiṟṟu.
277.	vāṉavaru maṇṇiṉmici yaracarkaḷu malimēl
	tāṉavarum vantutoḻu tavavuruvu koṇṭāṉ
	ūṉamaṉa miṉṟiyuyir kaṭkuṟuti yuṉṉik*
	kāṉamali nāṭukaḷka lantutiri kiṉṟāṉ.
uri:- vāṉavarum - viṇṇulakattut tēvarkaḷum, maṇṉiṉ mici yaracarkaḷum - maṇṇulakattu vēntarkaḷum, malimēl tāṉavarum - cēṭi mutaliya malikaḷilvāḻum vittiyātararkaḷum, vantu - taṉpāl vantu, toḻutavavuruvu koṇṭāṉ - toḻutu vaṇaṅkum tavavoḻukkatti mēṟkoṇṭa iccutattaṉ, maṉam ūṉam iṉṟi - maṉattiṭattē kuṟṟamāṉa niṉivu ciṟitu miṉṟi, uyirkaṭku uṟuti yuṉṉi - uyirkaṭku uṟutiyāvaṉavaṟṟiyē niṉintu, kāṉam mali nāṭukaḷ - kāṭum maliyum nāṭum mutaliya ellā viṭaṅkaṭkum ceṉṟu, kalantu tirikiṉṟāṉ - ōriṭattum nilipēṟiṉṟit tirintoḻukuvāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
cutatattaṉ mēṟkoṇṭirunta tavakkōlattiṉ ciṟappum oḻukkattiṉ viḻuppamum kūṟuvāṉ, vāṉavarum tāṉavarum maṇṇiṭattu vēntarum vantu toḻum ciṟappiṉi yeṭuttuk kūṟiṉāṉ. naṉṉiṉivē niṉiyum maṉamuṭiyaṉāyiṉum, paṇṭip payiṟci vācaṉiyāl neñcil niṉivuvāyilākak kuṟṟam ciṟitu muṇṭā kāvāṟu taṉṉik kāttukkoṇ ṭoḻukumāṟu tōṉṟa, “ūṉam maṉam iṉṟi uyirkaṭ kuṟuti yuṉṉi” eṉṟum, kāṉam mali nāṭu mutaliya vaṟṟuḷ ētēṉummōriṭattē nilittiruppiṉ atuvāyilāka viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ piṟantuviṭu mātaliṉ, “kāṉamali nāṭukaḷ kalantu tirikiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟu vaṇikaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. kalanteṉavē, ōriṭattum taṅkāmipeṟṟām.
278.	yāṉumala teṉatumala  titamumala teṉṉum*
	māṉamuṭi mātavaṉiṉ mēṉimaki ḻāṉāy
	ēṉiviṉi mācutaṉa turuviṉiṟu vātē†
	ñāṉavoḷi nakiceykuṇam nāḷumaṇi kiṉṟāṉ.
uri:- iṉmēṉi - taṉakku iṉitāy aminta mēṉiyāṉatu, yāṉu malatu eṉatu malatu - yāṉum aṉṟu eṉakkēyuriyatu maṉṟu, itamum alatu - nalam ceyvatu maṉṟu, eṉṉum - eṉṟu karutum, māṉamuṭi mātavaṉ - aṟivupperumiyiṉiyuṭiya mātavaṉ, makiḻāṉāy - ataṉ pāl paṟṟuc ciṟitum ilaṉāy, taṉatu uruviṉ - taṉatu mēṉiyil, ēṉiviṉimācu - ēṉiviṉikaḷākiya kuṟṟat tāluṇṭākum vaciyiṉi, niṟuvātu - mēṟkoḷḷātu, ñāṉa voḷi naki ceykuṇam - ñāṉavoḷi viḷaṅkac ceyyum naṟ kuṇattāluṇṭākum iciyākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷi, nāḷum aṇikiṉṟāṉ - eppōtum mēṟkoṇṭiruppāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
“uruviṉ mikkatōr uṭampatu peṟutalumaritē”1 eṉpa vākaliṉ, “iṉmēṉi” yeṉṟār. uṭaṟkuṇṭākum ākkakkēṭukaḷāl uyirkku iṉpamum tuṉpamum uṇṭātaliṉ, “eṉatu” eṉṟum, uyir nīṅkuṅkāṟum uṭaṉiruntu piṉpu piritaliṉ “eṉatu” eṉṟum, uṭalōṭu kūṭiyirukkumaḷavum uyirit taṉṉip piriya voṇṇātapaṭi paṟṟucceytaliṉ “itam” eṉṟum, uṭampu makkaḷāṟ karutappaṭiṉum, akkaruttaṉittum piṟappiṟ kētuvāyt tuṉpam payattalpaṟṟi, “yāṉumalatu eṉatu malatu itamum alatu eṉṉum māṉamuṭi mātavaṉ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. uṭali niliyil poruḷeṉ ṟaṟintu, atukoṇṭu nilitta payaṉic ceytu koḷḷutaliṉ perumi piṟitilliyātaliṉ, “māṉamuṭimātavaṉ” eṉṟār. ēṉit tīviṉi muṟṟum paḻiyum tīrāvaciyum payattaliṉ, ataṉi aḻaku tarum aṇiyākki, “nāḷum aṇikiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. vaci eṉṟum nilittiruppatu paṟṟi, “niṟuvātu” eṉṟār.
279.	īṭiṉmuṉi yōkiṉatu perumiyiḵ tiṟiva*
	kāṭupaṭu koliyiṉoṭu kaṭiyaviṉi niṉṉik
	kūṭuvato ḻintatukoḷ† iṉṟukoli vēlōy
	nāṭuvateṉ ñamaliyivi naṇukalakaṇ kāṇāy.‡
uri:- iṟiva - aracē, īṭuilmuṉi - oppaṟṟa iccutatta muṉivaṉatu, yōkiṉatu perumi - yōkattiṉ perumi, iḵtu - it taṉmiyākum, kolivēlōy - uyirkaḷikkollum vēliyuṭiya vēntē, kāṭupaṭukoliyiṉoṭu - kāṭṭiṭattē ceyta uyirkkoliyāl uṇṭākiya, kaṭiya viṉi - tuṉpam tarum tīviṉiyāṉatu, iṉṟu kūṭuvatoḻintatu koḷ - ippōtu nī im muṉivaṉik kaṇṭataṉāl niṉṉi vantaṭiyātu oḻintateṉṟu koḷvāyāka, nāṭuvatu eṉ - iṉi vēṟu pala niṉivatu vēṇṭā, ñamali ivi naṇukala - nī ēviya nāykaḷ im muṉivaṉi naṇṇā vāyiṉa, kaṇ kāṇ - avaṟṟi niṉkaṇkaḷālē naṉku kāṇpāyāka e-ṟu.
im muṉivaṉpāl yacōmatiyi āṟṟuppaṭuttu avaṉ kūṟum aṟattāl naṉṉeṟippaṭuttum karuttiṉa ṉātaliṉ, kaliyāṇa mittiraṉ, “īṭiṉmuṉi yōkiṉatu perumi iḵtu” eṉṟu muṉ moḻintu koṇṭu, avaṉik kaṇṭa kāṭciyāl varum payaṉ itu veṉpāṉ, “kāṭupaṭu koliyiṉoṭu kūṭiya viṉi niṉṉik kūṭuva toḻintatu koḷ” eṉṟāṉ. ōṭu - āṉurupiṉ poruṭṭu. taṉ kūṟṟiṉi yacōmati iṉitu ēṟṟukkōṭaṟku, yāṉ kūṟuvatit tuṇitaṟkuc cāṉṟu vēṟu virumpa vēṇṭā eṉpāṉ, “nāṭuvateṉ” eṉṟum, tīṅku ceyyap pōnta nāyk kūṭṭam atuceyya naṇuka māṭṭātu cēymikkaṇ niṉṟoḻin tamiyē pōtiya cāṉṟām eṉṟum ataṉi nī niṉ kaṇṇetirē kāṇkiṉṟāy eṉṟum kūṟuvāṉ, “ñamaliyivi naṇukala” eṉṟum, “kaṇkāṇāy” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. kaṇkāṇ eṉṟatu kāṭciyaḷavi kāṭṭiyavāṟu.
yacōmati, muṉivaṉ tiruvaṭiyil taṉ 
taliyiyarintu paliyiṭak karututal
280.	eṉṟavaṉu ḷaṅkoḷavi yampiṉaṉi yampac*
	ceṉṟutiru vaṭimalarkaḷ ceṉṉimici yaṇiyā
	iṉṟeṉatu piḻitaṇiya eṉṟaliya rintē
	niṉṟamuṉi caraṇiṭuva† leṉṟuniṉi kiṉṟāṉ
uri:- eṉṟu - eṉpatāka, avaṉ uḷam koḷa - yacōmatiyiṉ maṉam ēṟkumāṟu, iyampiṉaṉ - colliya  kaliyāṇa mittiraṉ, iyampa - muṉivaṉi vaṇaṅkumāṟu urikka, ceṉṟu - arukē ceṉṟu, tiruvaṭimalarkaḷ ceṉṉimici yaṇiyā - muṉivaṉuṭiya aḻakiya pātamākiya tāmarippūvi vaṇaṅkit taṉ taliyil cūṭikkoṇṭu, iṉṟu - ippōtu, eṉatu piḻi taṇiya - eṉ kuṟṟamellām nīṅka, eṉtali arintu - eṉṉuṭiya taliyi yaṟuttu, niṉṟa muṉi - tavaneṟiyil caliyātu niṉṟa cutatta muṉi vaṉuṭiya, caraṇ iṭuval - tiruvaṭiyil vippēṉ, eṉṟu niṉi kiṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu yacōmati niṉippāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
iyampiṉaṉ - viṉimutalmē ṉiṉṟa viṉippeyar. iyampak kēṭṭatum yacōmati muṉivaṉaṭikaḷi vaṇaṅka niṉittaliṉ, vaṇaṅkumā ṟurittāṉeṉṟu vaṇakkattic ceṉṉimici yaṇita leṉṟār. uyirkkoliyē ceytu kaṉṟiya uḷḷamuṭiya maṟa vēntaṉātaliṉ, taliyarintu vaḻipaṭum maṟavar pōlavē niṉip pāṉ, “eṉtali yarintu niṉṟamuṉi” yeṉavē, tavayōkattil cutattaṉ calippiṉṟi niṟkumāṟu peṟṟām. ivvāṟu niṉitta vēntaṉ taṉ uṭivāḷi yuruvat toṭaṅkutaliṉ, avaṉ karuttu vaṇikaṉ naṉku aṟintu koḷḷattakka niliyil meyppaṭṭami kaṇṭu, avaṉ mēlum cilakūṟi aracaṉ karutti māṟṟukiṉṟāṉ; atu varum ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
kaliyāṇamittiraṉ taṭutturittal
281.	iṉṉatuni ṉintativa ṉeṉṟukiye ṭuttē
	maṉṉaniṉmaṉattatuvi ṭuttiṭuma ṉattil
	taṉṉuyiriṉ maṉṉuyir vaḷarkkitaka vāṉāl
	niṉṉuyiri nīkaḷiyi ṉiṉṉaruḷa teṉṉām.
uri:- ivaṉ niṉintatu iṉṉatu - iv yacōmati niṉittatu ittaṉmittākum, eṉṟu - eṉṟu vaṇikaṉāṉa kaliyāṇamittiraṉ uṇarntu, kiyeṭuttu - kiyeṭuttut toḻutu taṭuttu, maṉṉa - vēntē, niṉ maṉattatu viṭuttiṭu - niṉmaṉatil niṉitta eṇṇatti viṭṭoḻippāyāka, maṉṉuyir - ulakil nilipeṟum uyirkaḷi, maṉattil taṉ uyiriṉ vaḷarkki - maṉatil taṉṉuṭiya uyir pōlak karutippēṇi vaḷarppatu, takavu - takutiyākum. āṉāl - av vāṟāyiṉ, nī niṉ uyirik kaḷiyiṉ - nī niṉṉuṭiya uyirip pōkkuvāyāyiṉ, iṉṉaruḷ atu eṉṉām - piṉpu nī ceytaṟkuriya iṉiya aruṭceykiyāṉatu evvāṟu iyaluvatām e-ṟu.
yacōmati taṉ taliyi yarintu muṉivaṉ tiruvaṭiyi liṭak karutiya karuttit teḷiya vuṇarntu koṇṭamiyiṉ, “iṉṉatu niṉinta tivaṉ” eṉṟum, acceyalit taṭuttaliṉāṉ “kiyeṭuttu niṉ maṉattatu viṭuttiṭu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. tāṉ ceyta kuṟṟa mellām keṭavēṇṭiṉ, aracaṉ aruḷaṟam mēṟkoḷvatē takkateṉkiṉṟāṉākaliṉ, “maṉattil taṉṉuyiriṉ maṉṉuyir vaḷarkki takavu” eṉṟum, ataṉic ceyyum viṉimutal nī yātaliṉ, nī niṉṉuyirip pōkkiviṭiṉ, aruḷviṉi yiṉṟāki, niṉkaruttum niṟiveytā teṉpāṉ, “iṉṉaruḷa teṉṉām” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ; iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāl niṉṉaruḷatu eṉṟu koṇṭu niṉakkuriya aruṭceyki iṉṟātaliṉ, ataṉāl orupayaṉum iṉṟām eṉṟu urittukkoḷka.
vaṇikaṉ mēlum cila kūṟal
282.	muṉṉamuri ceytaporuḷ muṭintilatu muṭiyap
	piṉṉumiki piṟavumuḷa* pēcutiṟa niṉivun
	tuṉṉuvayiṉ† muṉṉitutu ṇintupiḻi tūrāy
	piṉṉuniṉi kiṉṟavitu pīḻiperi teṉṟāṉ.
uri:- muṉṉam uriceyta poruḷ muṭintilatu - muṉṉē yāṉ colliya poruḷ iṉṉum muṭiyavilli, muṭiya - colli muṭittaṟku, piṉṉum miki piṟavum uḷa - colliya apporuṭkum mikutiyāka vēṟu palavum uḷḷaṉa, pēcutiṟam niṉivum tuṉṉuvayiṉ muṉ - vāyāṟ pēcum collum maṉattāl niṉiyum niṉivum  oṉṟucēru muṉṉē, itu tuṇintu - ivvuyirkkoliyic ceyyak karuti, piḻi tūrāy - paṇṭippāvattip pōkkāyāyiṉi, piṉṉum niṉikiṉṟa itu - piṉṉarum nī niṉ taliyiyariyak karutum ikkaruttu, pīḻi peritu eṉṟāṉ - mikavum tuṉpam payakkum tīviṉiyākum eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ e-ṟu.
muṉpu, muṉivaṉatu tavapperumi yītu eṉṟum, avaṉuṭiya kāṭcicciṟappāl aracaṉuṭiya tīviṉi nīṅkiṉa eṉṟum ataṟku avaṉēviya nāykaḷ muṉivaṉi naṇukāmi takka kāṭciyaḷavi yāmeṉṟum kūṟi iṉic ceyyat takuvaṉavum piṟavum kūṟimuṭikku muṉṉē aracaṟkuc cūḻcci piṟantatu meyppaṭṭami kaṇṭu taṉ pēccu neṟimāṟiyatu kūṟuvāṉ, “muṉṉa muri .... piṟavumuḷa” eṉṟum, ippēccu muṭivil aracaṉ tāṉ ceyyattakka taṉi vāyviṭṭuc collic ceyalvaki yārāyntu tuṇivatuvēṇṭu meṉṟaṟku, “pēcutiṟam niṉivum tuṉṉuvayiṉ muṉ itu tuṇintu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. eṇṇummi vikārattāl tokkatu. ittuṇivu, mēlum uyirkkoliyāyp paṇṭu ceytirunta pāvatti mikutippaṭuttutaliṉ, “piḻitūrāy” eṉṟāṉ. pāvam uyirik kīḻ nōkkit taḷḷutaliṉ, “tūrāy” eṉṟāṉ. tūrttal - aṭittal. “piṉṉum niṉikiṉṟa itu” eṉṟatu kūṟiyatukūṟa lāyiṉum, “peṟṟataṉ peyart turi niyamapporuṭ” ṭātal
paṟṟi, niyamitta vāṟu. pīḻi - tuṉpam.
maṉṉaṉ teḷintu muṉivaṉip paravutal
283.	maṉṉavaṉma ṉattativi ṭuttaruḷ*vaḷarkkuñ
	coṉṉavilcutattamuṉi tuṇimala† raṭikkaṇ
	ceṉṉimuṭi tuṉṉumalar ceṉṟuṟava ṇaṅkip
	paṉṉiyaru ḷiṟivavemar pavamuḻutu meṉṟāṉ
uri:- maṉṉavaṉ - vēntaṉākiya yacōmati, maṉattati viṭuttu - taṉ maṉattil niṉitta eṇṇatti viṭṭoḻittu, aruḷ vaḷarkkum col navil cutattamuṉi - aruḷākiya aṟatti mikuvikkum coṟkaḷiyē collum iyaṟkiyuṭiyaṉākiya cutatta muṉivaṉuṭiya, tuṇimalar aṭikkaṇ - tāmaripōlum iraṇṭu tiruvaṭiyilum, ceṉṉi muṭi tuṉṉumalar ceṉṟuuṟa vaṇaṅki - taliyil cūṭiya muṭiyiṟkoṇṭa pūvāṉatu muṉivaṉ tiruvaṭiyiṟ ceṉṟu poruntumāṟu vīḻntu vaṇaṅki, iṟiva - perumāṉē, emar pavam muḻutum - emmuṉṉōraṭinta piṟappu vaki muṟṟum, paṉṉiyaruḷ eṉṟāṉ - urittaruḷka eṉṟu vēṇṭiniṉṟāṉ e-ṟu.
vaṇikaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟi uḷaṅkoḷak kēṭṭut teḷiveytiṉa miyiṉ, “maṉattati viṭuttāṉ” eṉṟār. evvuyirkkum aruḷē niṉitalum ceytalum uṭiyaṉātaliṉ, muṉivaṉi, “aruḷ vaḷarkkum col navil cutatta muṉi” eṉṟār. col mēl vit tōtiṉārāyiṉum ēṉi niṉivu ceyalkaḷum koḷḷappaṭum eṉka. taṉ taṉmiyiṉṟi ammuṉivaṉ ceyyum aruḷ vayatta ṉāṉami tōṉṟa, “tuṇimalaraṭikkaṇ....... vaṇaṅki”ṉāṉ eṉavuṇarka. aṟaṅkēṭkiṉṟamiyiṉ, “iṟiva” eṉṟum, taṉ muṉṉōrceyta ceyviṉip payaṉi yaṟital vēṭki yuṭiyaṉātaliṉ, “emar pavam muḻutum paṉṉiyaruḷ” eṉṟum vēṇṭiṉāṉ. paṉṉiyaruḷ, oru collāy urikka eṉṉum poruḷ paṭa niṉṟatu, iḵtu uyarcol.
muṉivaṉ acōkaṉ eytiya piṟappiṉik kūṟutal
284.	āṅkumuṉi  yavatiyi ṉa ṟintaporu ḷataṉi
	vāṅkiyava ṉuṇarumvaki vittaruḷcey kiṉṟāṉ
	īṅkumuṉi yaṟṟiyata vattiṉi ṉacōkaṉ
	oṅkupuka ḻamarulaka moṉṟiṉu ḷuvantāṉ.
uri:- āṅku - appoḻutu, muṉi - cutatta muṉivaṉ, avatiyiṉ aṟinta poruḷataṉi vāṅki - avati ñāṉattālē aṟiyappaṭuvatāyirunta muṟpavap poruḷiyuṇarntu, avaṉ uṇarum vaki vittu - yacōmati naṉku uṇarntu koḷḷum muṟiyil vakuttamittu, aruḷ ceykiṉṟāṉ - urippāṉā yiṉāṉ, īṅku - ivvulakattē, muṉ iyaṟṟiya tavattiṉiṉ - muṉṉēceyta tavattiṉ payaṉāka, ōṅku pukaḻ amarulakam oṉṟiṉuḷ - mutaṟkaṇ uyarnta pukaḻiyuṭiya tēvarulakaṅkaḷ oṉṟiṉuḷḷē, acōkaṉ uvantāṉ - acōkaṉākiya maṉṉaṉ iṉpuṟṟiruntāṉ e-ṟu.
avati - cammiyañāṉavaki aintaṉuḷ oṉṟu. avi mati
ñāṉam, cutañāṉam, avatiñāṉam, maṉappariyayañāṉam, kēvala
ñāṉam eṉpaṉavākum. vikalam, tūlam, cakala niccayam eṉavarum vaki mūṉṟaṉuḷ, inta avati ñāṉam vikalattiṉ pāṟpaṭum eṉpar. inta ñāṉattāl iṟappil nikaḻntaṉavum, etirvil nikaḻvaṉavum aṟiyap paṭutalāl, itaṉik kāṭciyaṟi veṉṟum kūṟuvar. paṇṭi nikaḻccikaḷi niralē eṭuttukkēṭkum vēntaṉ maṉam teruṇṭu aruḷaṟam mēṟkoḷḷat takka neṟiyiṉkaṇ vittu ōtukiṉṟā ṉeṉpār, “poruḷataṉi vāṅki avaṉuṇarum vaki vittaruḷ ceykiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟār. aṟinta, aṟiyappaṭuvatāyirunta veṉpatu. amarulakatti intirapaṭala meṉṟum, aḵtu aṟupattu mūṉṟu vakippaṭumeṉṟum kūṟupavātaliṉ, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi leytiya acōkaṉi, “oṅkupukaḻamarulakamoṉṟiṉu ḷuvantāṉ” eṉṟār. “antarat taṟupattu mūṉṟatākiya intirap paṭalamum”1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka. nalviṉi yāṟṟip pukaḻey tiṉōrulaka meṉṟaṟku, “ōṅku pukaḻamarulakam” eṉṟāreṉa vuṇarka. uvantāṉ eṉa iṟanta kālattāṟ kūṟiyataṉāl, mutaṟkaṇ eṉpatu varuvikkap paṭṭatu.
acōkaṉ piṉpu piramakaṟpattil iṉitiruttal
285.	arumaṇiyi ṉoḷitikaḻu mamaraṉava ṉākip
	piramaṉula kataṉuṇmiki peṟukaṭalkaḷ pattum
	tirumaṇiya tuṇimuliya teyvamaṭa vārō
	ṭarumiyila ṉakamakiḻvi ṉamarumavaṉ* mātō.
uri:- avaṉ - acōkaṉ, arumaṇiyiṉ, oḷi tikaḻum amaraṉavaṉ āki - kiṭittaṟkariya māṇikka maṇiyiṉatu oḷi vīcum  tēvaṉāki, piramaṉ ulakataṉuḷ - piṉpu piramakaṟpattil, mikipeṟu kaṭalkaḷ pattum - mikutiyākappeṟṟa vāḻnāṭkaḷ pattukkaṭaṟ kālam, tirumaṇiya tuṇi muliya teyvamaṭavārōṭu - aḻakiya maṇimāli yaṇinta iraṇṭākiya muliyiṉiyuṭiya teyva makaḷiruṭaṉ, arumiyilaṉ - pōka nukarccikkaṇ arumi ciṟitu miṉṟi mikka, akamakiḻviṉ amarum - maṉamakiḻcciyōṭu virumpi yuṟivāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
tēvarkaḷ cemmaṇipōlum niṟamuṭiya mēṉiyarākaliṉ, “arumaṇiyiṉ oḷi tikaḻum amaraṉavaṉāki” eṉṟār, “oḷiyumiḻn tilaṅku mēṉi paritiyi ṉiyaṉṟa tokkum”1 eṉṟu tēvarum kūṟuvar. tēvarulakattuc cutarma kaṟpamutalākak kūṟappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ, nāṉku laṭcam vimāṉaṅkaḷiyuṭiya piramakaṟpattil acōkaṉ  iruntāṉ eṉka. mūvaki yōkaṅkaḷum muṟṟi nālvaki ārātaṉi tiyāṉattāl uṭampu nīṅki amararulaku pukuvōr piramakaṟpam pukuvareṉpatu2  ciṉanūṉ muṭipu. pirama piramōttara kaṟpa tēvarkaḷukku āyuḷ pattukkaṭalākum. kaṭaleṉpatu ciṉa nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇum kāla aḷavu. “ayaṉam āṇṭu, paṇiyukam pūvam palla pavvamē yaṉanta mīṟāk, kaṇmutaṟ kālapētam”3 eṉa varuvatu kāṇka. makaḷi riṉpattil tiḷittukkoṇṭiruttali, “muṉceynal viṉiyiṉāl mukiliṉ miṉṉaṉār, iṉcey vāyava rēntu koṅkiyar, vantiṭic cūḻntiṭa vaṇaṅka vāṉavar, antami liṉpattu ḷamaraṉ mēviṉāṉ”4 eṉṟu mērumantara purāṇam kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. tirumaṇiya, tuṇi muliya eṉpa kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷ. makaḷiriṉpam eḷitiṟ peṟuvataṉṟākaliṉ, ataṉi iṉitu eḷitiṟ peṟum acōkaṉi “arumiyilaṉ” eṉṟār; “añcol maṭavārtam mārvak kaḷipoṅka, neñcat tayilēṟṟum nīḷveṅ kaḻuvūrntum, kuñcik kaḷiyāṉik kōṭṭaḻa  luṭpaṭṭum, tuñciṟ ṟulakantō tuṉpak kaṭaluḷḷē”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka. mātum, ovum, aci.
amirtamatiyiṉ piṟappuneṟi kūṟutal
286.	vañcaṉiyi laṉṉiyuṭaṉ maṉṉavaṉi nañciṉ*
	tuñcumvaki cūḻntutoḻu nōymuḻutu māki
	añciṉmoḻi yamirtamati yarunarakiṉ vīḻntāḷ
	nañcaṉiya viṉinaliya nāmanaki vēlōy.
uri:- nāma naki vēlōy - accam poruntiya oḷi vīcum vēliyuṭiya vēntē, vañcaṉiyil - vañcaṉiyāka, maṉṉavaṉ - vēntaṉākiya yacōtaraṉi, aṉṉiyuṭaṉ - avaṉ tāyākiya cantiramatiyuṭaṉē, nañcil tuñcum vaki cūḻntu - viṭattāl iṟakkumāṟu uṇpittu, toḻunōy muḻutumāki - uṭal muḻutum kuṭṭanō yuṟṟu, arunarakil - nīkkutaṟkariya narakattilē, nañcaṉiya viṉinaliya - tāṉ uṇpitta viṭattip pōlum tīviṉiyāṉatu varutta, am cila moḻi yamirtamati - aḻakiya cilavāya coṟkaḷi yuṭiyaḷāṉa amirmatiyāṉavaḷ, vīḻntāḷ - vīḻntu varuntu vāḷāyiṉāḷ e-ṟu.
vañcaṉiyāka nañcūṭṭit taṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa yacōtaraṉiyum avaṉtāy cantiramatiyiyum koṉṟatik kuṟittu, “nañcil” tuñcum vaki cūḻntu” eṉṟār. kāraṇam kāriyamāka upacarikkap paṭṭatu. acceyaliṉ viḷivu toḻu nōyāy immiyil varuttiṟ ṟeṉṟaṟku, “toḻunōy muḻutumāki” eṉṟum, maṟumiyil nara kaṭintā ḷeṉṟaṟku, “nañcaṉiya viṉinaliya” “arunarakiṉ vīḻntāḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. “añciṉmoḻi yamirtamati” yeṉṟatu kuṟippāl colliṟ ciṉmiyuṟiṉum tīya niṉivu ceyalkaḷiṟ paṉmiyuṇmi tōṟṟuvittu ikaḻvu pulappaṭuttiṟṟu.
naraka vakikaḷi muṉivaṉ vēntaṟkuk kūṟutal
287.	iruḷiṉiru ḷiruḷpukiyo ṭaḷaṟumaṇal paraliṉ
	maruḷceyuru viṉaporuḷiṉ varupeyaru maviyē
	veruḷceyviṉi tarutuyaram viḷiyunila micippat*
	teruḷiyeḻu† vakinaraka kuḻikaḷivi tārōy.
uri:- tārōy - māliyi yaṇinta vēntē, veruḷ ceyviṉi tarutuyaram - mayakkatticceyyum tīviṉikaḷ payakkum tuṉpam, viḷiyum nilam icippat teruḷi - vantu varuttum iṭaṅkaḷi yām collakkēṭṭut teḷintu koḷvāyāka, naraka kuḻikaḷ ivi - narakakkuḻikaḷākiya ivi, eḻuvaki - ēḻuvakip paṭum, iruḷiṉ iruḷ - tamat tamappirapi, iruḷ - tamappirapi, maṇal - vālukappirapi, paraliṉ - paraṟkaṟkaḷākiya carkkarāp pirapi, maruḷcey uruviṉa poruḷiṉ - kaṇṭārkku mayakkattic ceyyum irattiṉaṅkaḷ niṟinta arataṉappirapiyeṉa, varupeyarum avi - varum peyarum avaṟṟiṟku aviyē yām e-ṟu.
oḷiyālum, vaṉmiyālum, paṉmiyālum, taṉpālaṭin tārkku arataṉam pōlap perumayakkattic ceytaliṉ, arataṉap pirapiyi, “maruḷce yuruviṉa poruḷiṉ” eṉṟār. uruviṉa eṉpatiyē arataṉappirapikku ēṟṟi, poruḷiṉ eṉpataṟku iruḷiṉ iruḷ mutaliyaṉa tamattamappirapi mutaliyavaṟṟil uḷḷa poruḷ ipporuḷkaḷē eṉṟu urittalumām. pirapi - vaṭṭam. oṭu - eṇṇoṭu. iṉ - cāriyi. veruḷ - mayakkam; “veruḷi māntar”1 eṉṟāṟpōla. teruḷi - muṉṉilikkaṇ vantatu. ikaram - vikuti. piṟatāṇṭum “tiṉṉi” (293) eṉṟataṟkum ituvē kūṟik koḷka; ivi teruṭi, tiṉṟiyeṉa niṟṟaṟpālaṉa. iṅkē kūṟiya narakavakikaḷi, “ēḻuḷa naraka nāma mirataṉañ carkkavālu, vāḻiya paṅkan tūman tamantamat tamattamākum”2  eṉpataṉālumuṇarka. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpi yaṟintāl yacōmati tīviṉiceyya niṉiyāṉ eṉpatu koṇṭu, “icippatteruḷi” yeṉṟār; piṟacāṉṟōrum, “māṟṟu taṟkariya tuṉpam periteṉṟu mayaṅkavēṇṭā, māṟṟutaṟ keḷitu kīḻkīḻ narakattavviyal paṟintāl”1 eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
amirtamati tūmappirapiyil vīḻntu varuntuntiṟatti muṉivaṉ kūṟal
288.	mērukiri yuyttiṭiṉum veppamoṭu taṭpa
	nīreṉavu rukkiṭuni lappuriya* vintām	
	ōriṉuṟu pukinaraki ṉurukiyuṭaṉ vīḻntāḷ
	ārumila ḷaṟaṉumila ḷamirtamati yavaḷē†
uri:- amirmatiyavaḷ - amirtamatiyākiya attīviṉiyāṭṭi, ōriṉ - ārāyumiṭattu, ārum ilaḷ - annarakil taṉakku oppāvār oruttarumiṉṟi, aṟaṉum ilaḷ - aṟattiṉ tuṇi tāṉum iṉṟi, mērukiri yuyttiṭiṉum - mērumaliyik koṇarntu taṉkaṇ iṭṭālum, veppamoṭu - veppattāl, taṭpa nīreṉa urukkiṭum - kuḷircciyi yuṭiya nīrpōla urukki viṭattakka, nilappuriya - nilaṅkaḷākiya purikaḷi, aintām pukiyuṟu narakiṉ - aintāvatākiya pukimikka tūmappirapi yeṉṉum  narakil, urukiyuṭaṉ vīḻntāḷ - vīḻntu vēkalāṉāḷ e-ṟu.
paṉimūṭik kuḷircci mikuntu uṟippuṇṭu calippiṉṟit tiṇṇitāyirukkum malikaṭkut taliyāya maliyāyiṉum, mērumali nilipeyarntu taṉkaṇ vīḻntāl, taṉ veppattāl ataṉi nīrāy urukkiviṭum eṉṟatu, tūmappirapiyiṉ veppamikuti yuṇarttiyavāṟu. naraka movvoṉṟiṟkum palapurikaḷuṇmiyiṉ, “puriya” eṉṟār. itaṉiyalpi, “mērunē riruppu vaṭṭi yiṭṭa vakkaṇattiṉuḷḷē, nīreṉa vurukkuñ citaveppaṅ kaṇiṉṟa kīḻmēl, ārvami laṟivaṉtanta nūliṉin tāvataṉṉiṟ, kārmukil vaṇṇa cīta veppaṅkaṇiṉṟa kaṇṭāy”2 eṉpataṉāl uṇarntu koḷka. amirtamatikku nikarāka mikka tīviṉi ceytār piṟar ilar eṉpataṟku “ārumilaḷ” eṉṟum, aṟam ciṟitēṉum iruntiruppiṉum ak koṭiya naraku pukutaṟku ētu irātu eṉpār, “aṟaṉumilaḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. amirtamatiyākiya avaḷ eṉpuḻiccuṭṭu, avaḷ ceyta tīviṉiyic cuṭṭi niṉṟatu.
avaḷeytiya narakavētaṉiyik kūṟutal
289.	āḻntakuḻi vīḻntapoḻu tarunaraka rōṭic
	cūḻntutuki yāveriyu ḷiṭṭaṉarkaḷ cuṭṭār
	pōḻntaṉarkaḷ puṇperuka vaṉṟaṟipu ṭittār
	mūḻntaviṉi muṉiyumeṉiṉ muṉiyalaru muḷarō.
uri:- āḻnta kuḻi vīḻntapoḻutu - āḻamāṉa annarakak kuḻiyil avaḷ vīḻntapōtu, arunarakar - vilakkaṟkariya narakavīrar, ōṭi-ōṭippōy avaḷippiṭittu, cūḻntu - paralāykkūṭi,  tukiyā - utittumitittu, eriyuḷ iṭṭaṉarkaḷ cuṭṭār - neruppiliṭṭuc cuṭṭārkaḷ. puṇperuka pōḻntaṉarkaḷ - avaḷuṭaliṟ puṇṇuṇ ṭāmāṟu vāḷāṟ piḷantārkaḷ, vaṉtaṟi puṭittār - valiya kampattāl avaḷi yaṭittārkaḷ, mūḻnta viṉi - tām ceytumuṟṟiya viṉi, muṉiyumeṉiṉ - tammi veṟuttup pakikkumāyiṉ, muṉiyalarum uḷarō - veṟātavarum uṇṭō, illi, e-ṟu.
aintām narakamātaliṉ, āḻntakuḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu. narakat tuṉpam kaḻiyuṅkālat taṉṟip piṟāṇṭu evvaḻiyum vilakkuṟātu oṟukkum iyalpiṉarātal tōṉṟa “arunarakar” eṉṟār. tīyiṭṭuk koḷuttalum, vāḷāṟ pōḻtalum, taṟiyāṟpuṭittalum niralē kūṟiyatu, avaḷuṟṟa tuṉpam kūṟumāṟṟāl yacōmatiyi naṉṉeṟip paṭuttaṟku; avaṉ maṉatti varuttutaṟ kaṉṟu; “aruḷ vaḷarkkum coṉṉavil cutattamuṉi” atu ceyyāreṉa vuṇarka. tammuyirkku iṉpan tarumeṉac ceyta tīviṉi atu tārātu tuṉpantarutaliṉ, “mūḻnta viṉi muṉiyiṉ” eṉṟum, oruvaṟku avaṉ ceyviṉi pōlap payaṉ viḷippatil oruvarum nikarātal muṭiyāmiyiṉ ataṉiṟ cuṟṟam cīriyatu piṟitilli  yeṉṟum, accuṟṟamillārkkuc cuṟṟameṉpatē illiyeṉṟum kūṟuvār, “muṉiyalarum uḷarō” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. muḻuteṉṉum col mūḻtteṉa viṉiyāki, etukinōkki melintu niṉṟatu. ummi - ciṟappu. ōkāram - etirmaṟi.
290.	centacikaḷ ventaṉakaḷ tiṉṟaṉimu ṉeṉṟē
	kontaḻalil veṅtukotu kotuveṉako tikkum
	centaḻali ṉintirarkaḷ cempukaḷti ṇippa
	ventaḻali ṉinturuki* viṇṭoḻuku mukaṉē.
uri:- muṉ - iṟappataṟku muṉṉē, centacikaḷ ventaṉakaḷ tiṉṟaṉiyē eṉṟu - cevviya vūṉkaḷi vēkavittut tiṉṟā yaṉṟō eṉṟu colli yikaḻntu, centaḻaliṉ intirarkaḷ - civanta neruppippōlum talivarkaḷ, kontaḻalil ventu - kottāka mikkeḻum neruppil ventu, kotu kotu veṉak kotikkum - kotu kotu veṉṟu kotikkiṉṟa, cempukaḷ tiṇippa - cepput tuṇṭaṅkaḷiyeṭuttu avaḷ vāyiliṭṭut tiṇittārkaḷāka, ventaḻaliṉ - avaṟṟiṉ mikkuṟṟa veppattāl, nintu uruki viṇṭu mukaṉ oḻukum - avaḷuṭiya mukam ventu nīrāyurukit taliyiṉiṉṟum oḻukuvatāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.
narakavīrarkaṭkut talivar neruppuppōlum niṟamum vem mippaṇpum uṭiyārātal tōṉṟa, “centaḻaliṉ intirarkaḷ” eṉṟār. intirar - talivar; ivarkaḷ narakalōkattē vāḻum narakavīrarkaṭkut talivarpōlum. ceppu taṭikaḷ ūṉ taṭipōla iruttaliṉ avaṭku ūṉuṇavāka ivarkaḷ avaṟṟik kotikkak kotikka vēkavittu vāyil tiṇittārkaḷ eṉka. avveppa mikutiyi yāṟṟāmiyāl mukam uruki nintāḷ eṉpār, “ventaḻaliṉ ninturuki viṇṭoḻukum mukaṉē” eṉṟār. eṉṟē yeṉpuḻi ēkāram pirittuk kūṭṭap paṭṭatu. mukaṉē eṉpuḻi yaḵtu īṟṟaci.
291.	karukaruka rintaṉaḷka viṉkoḷoru* pāvi
	perukeriyi ṉiṭṭuruka vituperitu miṉiteṉ†
	ṟarukaṇiya nuntutalu malaṟiyatu taḻuvip
	poruporupo rintupoṭi yāmuṭala mellām.
uri:- karukaru karintaṉaḷ - narakattīyāl mikak karukaruttut tīyntavaḷāṉa amirtamatiyi, kaviṉkoḷ oru pāvi - aḻaku koḷḷac ceyta oru āṇ pāviyik kāṭṭi, peruku eriyiṉ iṭṭu - ataṉi mikkeḻunterinta tīyiliṭṭuk kāyavittu, uruka - uruki nekiḻum patattil, itu peritum iṉitu eṉṟu - itu taḻuvutaṟku uṉakku iṉitāyirukkumākaliṉ taḻuvuka eṉṟu colli, aruku aṇiya nuntutalum - ataṉarukē ceṉṟu taḻuvumāṟu narakavīrar avaḷittaḷḷavum, alaṟi - ōlamiṭṭaḻutu, atu taḻuvi - ataṉi yavar colliyapaṭiyē taḻuvi, uṭalam ellām - avaḷ uṭam pellām, poru poru porintu - poru poru veṉap porintu, poṭiyām - poṭiyāy viḻukiṉṟāḷ e-ṟu.
